# Korail Mobile App — Full API Analysis

## Overview

Package **com.korail.talk**, version **v6.5.0**, generated **2026-07-20**. Method: static decompile of `base.apk` (jadx). The client networking stack is **RETROFIT 1.x** (square `retrofit.*`, **NOT** `retrofit2`) layered over `java.net.HttpURLConnection` — plus a secondary **WebView** surface for a subset of flows. Details:

- **Client build:** `analysis/jadx/sources/com/korail/talk/network/ExecuteDao.java:18-65`. Uses `retrofit.RestAdapter.Builder` (`:11`, `:19`) with `GsonConverter` (`:14`, `:19`) and `retrofit.client.UrlConnectionClient` (`:13`, `:19`) — i.e. plain `HttpURLConnection`, connect/read timeouts 60000ms (`:23-24`). `getService(Class<T>)` (`:63`) creates the Retrofit service proxy via `getRestAdapterBuilder().build().create(cls)` (`:64`); the base endpoint is bound with `.setEndpoint(z.getSSLHost())` (`:60`, from `getRestAdapterBuilder()` at `:59-61`). Log level is toggled by `a.IS_DEBUG_LOG` between `RestAdapter.LogLevel.FULL` and `NONE` (`:19`).
- **Anti-macro token injection:** the overridden `openConnection(Request)` (`:21-54`) inspects `a.IS_MACRO_ACTIVE` (`:25-26`); when active and the request URL matches one of six sensitive paths (`TicketReservation`, `NonMemTicket`, `ScheduleView`, `ScheduleViewSpecial`, `trn.prcFare.do`, `Login` — `:27`), it attaches an `x-dynapath-m-token` request header generated by `kr.scripters.dynapath.sdk.android.DynaPathMobileSDK.Companion.generate()` (`:45-47`). See §2 (Auth & crypto) and §7 (Security & anti-tamper) for the full DynaPath / anti-tamper treatment.

## 1. Network transport & hosts

This section documents the on-the-wire transport layer for **com.korail.talk v6.5.0** (`VERSION_NAME="6.5.0"`, `VERSION_CODE=60500002`, `FLAVOR="product"`, `BUILD_TYPE="release"`, `DEBUG=false` — `G4/a.java:7-18`). Every fact below is taken from the decompiled release build under `analysis/jadx/sources` and `analysis/apktool`.

### 1.1 Networking stack

| Layer | Implementation | Source |
| --- | --- | --- |
| REST framework | **Square Retrofit 1.x** (`retrofit.*`, **NOT** retrofit2) | `ExecuteDao.java:11-14`; services import `retrofit.http.*` e.g. `LoginService.java:6-9`; `BaseDaoHelper.java:15-17` |
| HTTP client | `java.net.HttpURLConnection` via Retrofit's `UrlConnectionClient` (OkHttp is **not** used) | `ExecuteDao.java:13,19-21` |
| JSON (de)serialization | Gson via `GsonConverter(new com.google.gson.e().create())` | `ExecuteDao.java:19` |
| Threading | Android `AsyncTask` — `BaseDaoHelper.HttpTask.doInBackground()` calls `dao.executeDao()` | `BaseDaoHelper.java:43-46` |
| Retrofit log level | `FULL` if `I4.a.IS_DEBUG_LOG` else `NONE`; `IS_DEBUG_LOG=false` in this release | `ExecuteDao.java:19`; `G4/a.java:12,23` |

There is **no `import retrofit2` and no `import okhttp3` anywhere** in `com/korail/talk`.

**RestAdapter factory.** `ExecuteDao.getDefaultRestAdapterBuilder()` builds a single `RestAdapter.Builder` (`ExecuteDao.java:18-57`); `getRestAdapterBuilder()` sets the endpoint to `S4.z.getSSLHost()` (`ExecuteDao.java:59-61`); `getService(cls)` builds a **fresh adapter per call** (`ExecuteDao.java:63-65`). No DAO overrides the endpoint or builder, so all 34 services share `getSSLHost()`.

### 1.2 Base URLs / hosts per environment

`S4.z.getSSLHost()` (the Retrofit API endpoint) and `getWebHost()` are byte-identical selectors keyed on `K4.g.SERVER_TYPE` (`z.java:46-53`). Host string constants are mirrored in `K4/g.java:20-25`.

| Environment | Enum code | API host = Web host (`getSSLHost`/`getWebHost`) | Source |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| **REAL (prod) — ACTIVE** | `"3"` | `https://smart.letskorail.com` | `z.java:48`; `g.java:21` |
| TEST | `"2"` | `https://dev3.letskorail.com` | `z.java:48`; `g.java:25` |
| STAGING | `"1"` | `https://dev2.letskorail.com` | `z.java:48`; `g.java:24` |
| DEV (fallback default) | `"0"` | `https://mobiledev.letskorail.com` | `z.java:48`; `g.java:20` |

**Active environment = REAL / production.** `K4.g.SERVER_TYPE = EnumC5607a.fromCode("3")` (`g.java:90-91`); enum codes are `DEV="0"`, `STAGING="1"`, `TEST="2"`, `REAL="3"` (`d5/EnumC5607a.java:6-9`). Build config confirms `G4.a.CONNECT_SERVER="3"` (`G4/a.java:7-18`). All production letskorail API traffic therefore targets **`https://smart.letskorail.com`**.

**Other hosts (per environment):**

| Purpose | REAL | TEST | STAGING | DEV | Source |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| Multi-language web (`getMultiLangWebHost`) | `https://www.korail.com` | `https://dev5.letskorail.com` | `https://www.korail.com` | `https://dev5.letskorail.com` | `z.java:36-39` |
| Push agent host (`getPushAddress`) | `smart.letskorail.com` | `smartbeta.letskorail.com` | `smartbeta.letskorail.com` | `dev.letskorail.com` | `z.java:41-44`; `g.java:68-71` |
| NetFunnel (virtual waiting room) | `nf.letskorail.com` (https, port 443) — same all envs | — | — | — | `KTApplication.java:78-86`; `g.java:51,43` |
| SRT web reservation | `https://eapp.srail.kr/` (prod) | `https://teapp.srail.kr/` (DEV only) | — | `https://teapp.srail.kr/` | `g.java:76-78,116-120` |

### 1.3 Global request parameters (form fields, NOT HTTP headers)

Every business API call carries three common fields injected from `BaseRequest` and passed as `@Field("Device"/"Version"/"Key")` on the service methods (e.g. `LoginService.java:19`):

| Field | Value | Constant | Source |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `Device` | `AD` | `BaseRequest.ANDROID` | `BaseRequest.java:7,15` |
| `Version` | `250601003` | `BaseRequest.VERSION` | `BaseRequest.java:9,16` |
| `Key` | `korail1234567890` | `BaseRequest.APP_KEY` | `BaseRequest.java:8,17` |

The WebView equivalent is the single string `K4.g.COMMON_PARAMETER = "Device=AD&Version=250601003&Key=korail1234567890"` (`g.java:11`).

> **Note:** `Version=250601003` is the API/protocol version (= `G4.a.API_VERSION`), which is distinct from the app version `6.5.0`.

### 1.4 Global / implicit HTTP headers

There are effectively **no global HTTP headers** on the Retrofit layer — no service declares any `@Header`/`@Headers` annotation. The only custom request header is `x-dynapath-m-token`, set via `HttpURLConnection.setRequestProperty` inside `ExecuteDao`'s anonymous `UrlConnectionClient.openConnection()` override (`ExecuteDao.java:47`), and only when:

1. `I4.a.IS_MACRO_ACTIVE` is `true` (default **false**, `I4/a.java:14`; `ExecuteDao.java:26`), **and**
2. the request URL contains one of these 6 anti-macro endpoints (`ExecuteDao.java:27`):
   - `/classes/com.korail.mobile.certification.TicketReservation`
   - `/classes/com.korail.mobile.nonMember.NonMemTicket`
   - `/classes/com.korail.mobile.seatMovie.ScheduleView`
   - `/classes/com.korail.mobile.seatMovie.ScheduleViewSpecial`
   - `/classes/com.korail.mobile.trn.prcFare.do`
   - `/classes/com.korail.mobile.login.Login`

The token value comes from `kr.scripters.dynapath` — `DynaPathMobileSDK.Companion.generate()` (`ExecuteDao.java:45`); the SDK is initialized via `DynaPathMobileSDK.Companion.initialize(getApplication())` in `IntroActivity.java:584`.

The **WebView** stack uses its own User-Agent = default UA + `" korailtalk AppVersion/6.5.0"` (`PUSH_APPTYPE` + `G4.a.VERSION_NAME`, `BaseWebViewActivity.java:1125`).

### 1.5 Timeouts, keep-alive, retry

| Setting | Value | Source |
| --- | --- | --- |
| Connect timeout | `60000` ms | `ExecuteDao.java:23` (mirror `g.java:12` `CONNECTION_TIMEOUT`) |
| Read timeout | `60000` ms | `ExecuteDao.java:24` (mirror `g.java:72` `READ_TIMEOUT`) |
| Image connect / read timeout | `10000` ms each | `g.java:27-28` |
| HTTP keep-alive | **globally disabled** — `System.setProperty("http.keepAlive", C6077m.FALSE)` (resolves to `"false"`) | `KTApplication.java:92-94` |
| Application-level retry | **none** | `BaseDaoHelper.java:43-96` |

`BaseDaoHelper.HttpTask` executes the DAO exactly once; on `RetrofitError` it wraps an `R4.b` exception and returns (`BaseDaoHelper.java:50-96`). The only special path is HTTP **403** + `"forbidden"` + response header `DynaPath-Result < 0`, which extracts a JSON `message` to show an anti-macro dialog (`BaseDaoHelper.java:54-90`) — this is error display, not retry.

### 1.6 TLS / cleartext posture & certificate pinning

- **Certificate pinning: NONE.** No `CertificatePinner`, `X509TrustManager`, `HostnameVerifier`, `SSLContext`, or `SSLSocketFactory` exists anywhere in `com/korail/talk`. `network_security_config.xml` declares **no `<pin-set>`** (`res/xml/network_security_config.xml:1-9`).
- **Cleartext:** `network_security_config.xml` sets `cleartextTrafficPermitted="true"` (`includeSubdomains`) for exactly four domains — none of which are letskorail API hosts:
  - `1.255.59.22`
  - `bot-dev-lb-100453984-927a54b5c9cb.kr-gov.lb.naverncp.com`
  - `teapp.srail.kr`
  - `app.srail.kr`
- **All letskorail API traffic is HTTPS** — `K4.g.SECURE_PROTOCOL = "https://"` (`g.java:73`) and every `getSSLHost()` value is an `https://` URL (`z.java:46-49`).

### 1.7 Interceptors, sessions & cookies

- **Interceptors:** no Retrofit `RequestInterceptor` and no response interceptor are registered. The sole request-mutation hook is the anonymous `UrlConnectionClient` subclass overriding `openConnection()` in `ExecuteDao` (`ExecuteDao.java:19-56`), which applies the 60 s timeouts and the conditional DynaPath header.
- **Session / cookies:** authentication is **cookie-based `JSESSIONID`**, not a bearer/OAuth token. `KTApplication` installs a process-global `java.net.CookieManager` with `CookiePolicy.ACCEPT_ALL` as the default `CookieHandler` (`KTApplication.java:72-76`), so `HttpURLConnection` auto-persists and resends `JSESSIONID` across all Retrofit calls. `KTApplication.getCookie()` extracts `JSESSIONID=<val>` (`KTApplication.java:105-116`); `setSessionId()` copies it into the WebView's `android.webkit.CookieManager` keyed on `getSSLHost()` to bridge native↔web session (`KTApplication.java:129-132`), invoked right after login (`LoginDao.java:241`).

### 1.8 WebView-driven traffic (parallel to Retrofit)

`BaseWebViewActivity` issues a second, non-Retrofit traffic stream built from Intent extras `WEB_POST_URL`/`WEB_GET_URL` + `WEB_POST_PARAMETER`/`WEB_GET_PARAMETER`:

- **POST** → `webView.postUrl(url, (COMMON_PARAMETER&params).getBytes())` (`BaseWebViewActivity.java:1049,1087`)
- **GET** → `webView.loadUrl(url + "?" + COMMON_PARAMETER&params)` (`BaseWebViewActivity.java:1053,1060`)
- also `loadDataWithBaseURL` (`:819`) and JS bridge `loadUrl("javascript:fn('"+json+"')")` (`:987-990`)
- `COMMON_PARAMETER` is prepended when `IS_WEB_DEFAULT_PARAMETER` (default `true`, `:1029-1033`)

Web hosts come from `getWebHost()` (prod `smart.letskorail.com`); multi-language pages from `getMultiLangWebHost()`.

### 1.9 Push & NetFunnel transports (non-HTTP)

- **Push is NOT HTTP/Retrofit.** It is the H2O Systech "SmartAlimi/SmartAgent" push agent bound over **AIDL** to `com.h2osystech.smartalimi.servicealimi.MessageManager` (`b5/g.java:116-120`). The server is set via `SmartAgentInterface.setServerIPPort(z.getPushAddress(), K4.g.PUSH_PORT)` over **TCP port 3101**, `appType="korailtalk"` (`b5/g.java:57-58`; `z.java:41-44`; `g.java:67-71`). (Note the separate HTTP `PushService` in §1.10 is a distinct registration API, not this socket agent.)
- **NetFunnel** virtual-waiting-room uses host `nf.letskorail.com` over https, `serviceID="service_1"`, `actionID="act_8"`, timeout `3` (`KTApplication.java:78-86`; `g.java:43,51`).

### 1.10 Service inventory (all rooted at `https://smart.letskorail.com` / `getSSLHost`)

**34 Retrofit service interfaces exposing ~164 methods.** Almost all business endpoints are `@POST @FormUrlEncoded` to `/classes/com.korail.mobile.<module>.<Action>[.do]`; a few are `@GET` (station data, logout, product reservation, push) plus static `@GET /file/CACHE/*.cache`. Annotations used across services: `@Field`, `@FieldMap`, `@FormUrlEncoded`, `@GET`, `@POST`, `@Query`, `@QueryMap` (no `@Body`/`@Multipart`/`@Streaming`/`@Path`). Every service shares the cookie session and HTTPS; auth column below is `cookie` unless the endpoint is unauthenticated.

## 2. Auth & crypto

Korail's authentication is **session-cookie based** (server `JSESSIONID`, no bearer token) over a Square **Retrofit v1** stack. All password material sent over the wire is encrypted with a **server-supplied AES key**, while a *separate* device-bound AES key protects credentials cached on disk. This section documents every auth/account endpoint, both crypto helpers, session/cookie handling, device identity, and the two anti-automation tokens.

### 2.1 Networking stack & common request envelope

- **HTTP stack:** Square Retrofit **v1** (`retrofit.*` / `retrofit.RestAdapter`, *not* retrofit2) with a `UrlConnectionClient` and a `GsonConverter`. `ExecuteDao.getRestAdapterBuilder()` sets `endpoint = z.getSSLHost()` and connect/read timeouts of 60 s (`ExecuteDao.java:23-24`). Auth calls are Retrofit interface methods on `LoginService`; their DAOs live under `com/korail/talk/network/dao/login/`.
- **Real API host:** `https://smart.letskorail.com` (`S4/z.java` `getSSLHost`, ~`:48-52`).
- **Common envelope — hardcoded shared secrets:** every request extends `BaseRequest`, whose constructor hardcodes three values that are then sent as `Device` / `Version` / `Key` form (or query) params on nearly every endpoint (`BaseRequest.java:7-18`):

  | Field | Constant | Value |
  |-------|----------|-------|
  | `device` | `ANDROID` | `AD` |
  | `version` | `VERSION` | `250601003` |
  | `key` | `APP_KEY` | `korail1234567890` |

  `APP_KEY = "korail1234567890"` is a **static, app-wide shared secret** — not per-user and not per-session.

### 2.2 Auth & account endpoints

All paths are relative to `https://smart.letskorail.com`. Every field name below is the literal Retrofit `@Field`/`@Query` annotation value (preserved through obfuscation).

| Name | Method | Path | Key params | Response | Auth | Source |
|------|--------|------|-----------|----------|------|--------|
| **login** (member + easy/social + auto) | POST (form) | `/classes/com.korail.mobile.login.Login` | `Device`, `Version`, `Key`, `txtMemberNo` (=loginId, plaintext), `txtPwd` (encrypted, see §2.3), `txtInputFlg` (=loginType/provider), `checkValidPw` (Y/N), `custId` (social user id), `etrPath`, `idx` | `LoginDao.LoginResponse` | No (establishes session) | `LoginService.java:17-19`; `LoginDao.java:236-243` |
| **logout** | GET | `/classes/com.korail.mobile.login.Logout` | *(none)* | `BaseResponse` | Yes (session) | `LoginService.java:29-30`; `LogoutDao.java` |
| **certMember** (userCheck / member certification) | POST (form) | `/classes/com.korail.mobile.login.userCheck` | `Device`, `Version`, `Key`, `txtAcptPsNm`, `acept`, `txtCpNo`, `memNum`, `txtEmailNo` | `MemberCertDao.MemberCertResponse` (`mbCrdNo`, `strCustNo`) | No | `LoginService.java:13-15`; `MemberCertDao.java` |
| **loginAthnReg** (register trusted-device login) | POST (form) | `/classes/com.korail.mobile.login.loginAthnReg.do` | `Device`, `Version`, `Key`, `lognTpCd` (login-type code), `custId` | `BaseResponse` | Yes (session) | `LoginService.java:21-23`; `MyPageActivity.java:418-424` |
| **loginAthnRmv** (remove trusted-device login) | POST (form) | `/classes/com.korail.mobile.login.loginAthnRmv.do` | `Device`, `Version`, `Key`, `srvQryDvVal`, `lognTpCd` | `BaseResponse` | Yes (session) | `LoginService.java:25-27`; `MyPageActivity.java:431` |
| **memberCheck** (joinCfm) | POST (form) | `/classes/com.korail.mobile.login.joinCfm.do` | `Device`, `Version`, `hmpgPwd` (**plaintext**, no `Key` field), `custNm` | `BaseResponse` | Yes (session) | `LoginService.java:32-34`; `MemberCheckDao.java` |
| **memberDrop** (mbSced / withdraw membership) | POST (form) | `/classes/com.korail.mobile.login.mbSced.do` | `Device`, `Version` | `BaseResponse` | Yes (session) | `LoginService.java:36-38`; `MemberDropDao.java` |
| **onepass login** (digital One-Pass, WebView) | GET (WebView) | `/classes/com.korail.mobile.onepass.login.do` | *(WebView flow)* | `userKey` via intent extra | No | `LoginActivity.java` `moveToOnepassLogin` / `onActivityResult` (req 128) |

Notes:
- `AutoLoginDao` and `EasyLoginDao` subclass `LoginDao` and hit the **same** `Login` endpoint.
- On success `LoginDao.executeDao()` calls `KTApplication.getInstance().setSessionId()` to persist the `JSESSIONID` cookie (`LoginDao.java:241`).
- **`memberCheck` sends the homepage password (`hmpgPwd`) in plaintext** and omits the `Key` field entirely — unlike `login`, it does *not* apply the §2.3 wire cipher.

### 2.3 Password encryption for transmission (the wire cipher)

The plaintext password becomes `txtPwd` via `K5/b.java` `E0()` (`:124-140`) and the identical `BaseActivity.L()`. The behavior is **server-driven**, gated by `CommonCodeDao.Login.pwdAESCphd`, which is fetched in the `LOGIN_DATA` common-code payload (`CommonCodeDao.java:668-686`):

- **If `pwdAESCphd == "Y"`:**
  `txtPwd = F4.a.encryptBase64( C0812l.encryptAES(login.getKey(), pw) )`
- **Otherwise (any other value):**
  `txtPwd = F4.a.encryptBase64(pw)` — **Base64 only, no encryption**.

`C0812l.encryptAES(keyString, plaintext)` is the wire cipher (`S4/C0812l.java:18-24`):

- **Algorithm:** `AES/CBC/PKCS5Padding`
- **Key:** `SecretKeySpec(keyString.getBytes(), "AES")` where `keyString` is the **server-supplied** `login.getKey()`. Its byte length selects AES-128/192/256.
- **IV:** `IvParameterSpec(keyString.substring(0,16).getBytes())` — the **first 16 chars of the same key string are reused as the IV**. The IV is therefore **not random** and is fully derived from the (server-supplied) key.
- **Output:** `Base64.encode(ciphertext, 0)` — flag `0` = default/newline-wrapped Base64.

This result is then re-wrapped by `F4.a.encryptBase64` (`F4/a.java:43-45`), which is `Base64.encodeToString(..., 2)` — flag `2` = `NO_WRAP`. Net wire value:

```
txtPwd = base64_nowrap( base64_default( AES-CBC-PKCS5(pw, key=login.getKey(), iv=key[0:16]) ) )
```

Both key and IV originate from the server-provided key string; the same helper (`C0812l.getAmountEncrypt`, `:26-41`) applies the identical Y/N gate to encrypt **amounts** as well.

### 2.4 Session / cookie handling

`KTApplication` manages the session entirely through cookies (`KTApplication.java`):

- A `java.net.CookieManager` with `CookiePolicy.ACCEPT_ALL` is installed as the default `CookieHandler` (`f()`, `:72-76`), so `JSESSIONID` is auto-managed by the HTTP stack.
- `getCookie()` (`:105-116`) walks the cookie store and returns the `JSESSIONID=<value>` pair.
- `setSessionId()` (`:129-132`) copies that cookie into the **WebView** `android.webkit.CookieManager`, keyed to `z.getSSLHost()`, so WebViews share the native session.
- `clearCookie()` (`:99-103`) wipes WebView cookies (used on logout) and reinstalls the `CookieHandler`.

There is **no bearer/JWT token** — the session *is* the server `JSESSIONID` cookie. Logout (`GET .../Logout`, no params) invalidates it server-side; the app additionally calls `clearCookie()`.

### 2.5 Local credential encryption (device-bound, on-disk only)

A **separate** cipher, `F4.a` (`F4/a.java:12-45`), is used only for at-rest storage on the device — **never** on the wire:

- **Algorithm:** `AES/ECB/PKCS5Padding` — **no IV** (ECB mode).
- **Key derivation `a(context)` (`:13-15`):**
  `Arrays.copyOf( UUID.nameUUIDFromBytes( Settings.Secure.getString(ANDROID_ID).getBytes("UTF-8") ).toString().getBytes("UTF-8"), 16 )`
  i.e. take the SSAID (`android_id`), build a **name-based (MD5/v3) UUID** from its bytes, take the 36-char UUID *string*, and use its **first 16 bytes as an AES-128 key**.
- **Output:** `Base64.encode(..., 0)` (default flag).
- `encryptBase64`/`decryptBase64` (`:28-30`, `:43-45`) are **plain Base64 (`NO_WRAP`, flag 2)** — no encryption.

This device-bound key encrypts `KEY_LOGIN_ID`, `KEY_LOGIN_PW`, `KEY_LOGIN_CUST_NO`, stored credit cards, and cached issue/ticket lists (card/ticket blobs also via `H4/a.java`).

### 2.6 Credential storage location & keys

Encrypted blobs are written to **plain default `SharedPreferences`** via `S4/H.java` (`PreferenceManager.getDefaultSharedPreferences`) — **not** Android `EncryptedSharedPreferences`/`MasterKey` (a grep for those in the app package returns nothing). On login success, `K5/b.java onReceive` (~`:442-490`) stores:

| Pref key | Value |
|----------|-------|
| `KEY_LOGIN_ID` | `F4.a.encryptAES(id)` |
| `KEY_LOGIN_PW` | `F4.a.encryptAES(pw)` |
| `KEY_LOGIN_CUST_NO` | `F4.a.encryptAES(custNo)` |
| `KEY_LOGIN_TYPE` | plaintext |
| `KEY_AUTO_LOGIN` / `KEY_MEMBER_NUM` | boolean |
| `LOGIN_DATA` | `CommonCodeDao.Login` JSON (**includes the AES `key` used for `txtPwd`**) |
| `EASY_LOGIN`, `VAR_DATA` | common codes |
| `KEY_SETTING_SECURE_ANDROID_ID` | device-id selection flag |

Auto-login (`BaseActivity.G()` and `K5/b.z0()`, ~`:211-225`) reads these back with `F4.a.decryptAES`, then re-encrypts the password with the §2.3 wire cipher (`E0`/`L`) before sending.

### 2.7 Device identity (no dedicated device-register endpoint)

There is **no device-registration call at login**. A device id is resolved lazily by `TicketListActivity` when listing tickets:

- `E4.a.getAdvertising` → Google `W1.a.getAdvertisingIdInfo` (`TicketListActivity.java:986`).
- If limit-ad-tracking is enabled, it falls back to `Settings.Secure` `android_id` (SSAID); when emergency tickets exist it may fall back to a zero UUID (`:1035-1053`).
- The chosen id is sent as `txtDeviceId` in the my-ticket-list request (`setTxtDeviceId`, `:937`).

`android_id` also seeds the §2.5 local-storage AES key. No advertising id / UUID is registered server-side at login.

### 2.8 Anti-automation / bot-protection tokens

Two independent mechanisms:

1. **DynaPath (`x-dynapath-m-token` header).** `ExecuteDao` (`ExecuteDao.java:20-52`) calls `kr.scripters.dynapath.sdk.android.DynaPathMobileSDK.generate()` and adds the header `x-dynapath-m-token` when `IS_MACRO_ACTIVE` and the request URL is on a whitelist. **The whitelist includes the `Login` endpoint** (`com.korail.mobile.login.Login`) plus reservation/schedule/fare endpoints.

2. **`sid` token.** `C0812l.getSid()` (`S4/C0812l.java:43-50`) = `C0812l.encryptAES("2485dd54d9deaa36", "AD" + System.currentTimeMillis())`, i.e. `AES/CBC/PKCS5Padding` with the **hardcoded 16-byte key `"2485dd54d9deaa36"`** (and IV = the same 16 bytes, since `substring(0,16)` equals the whole key), Base64-encoded. This `sid` is attached to **seat/car/train search** requests (`SearchSeatListDao`, `SearchCarListDao`, `TrainInquiryDao`, `LimousineTrainInquiryDao`), **not** to login.

### 2.9 Social / easy login

Easy/social login reuses the same `Login` endpoint via `EasyLoginDao`. `K5/b.executeEasyLogin` (~`:236-244`) sets `txtInputFlg` (=loginType) and `custId` (the social user id) plus `checkValidPw`. Provider type codes are recognized by `u.isEasyLoginType` (`S4/u.java:126-128`):

| Code | Provider |
|------|----------|
| `K` | Kakao |
| `N` | Naver |
| `G` | Google |
| `D` | One-Pass (digital) |

One-Pass uses a WebView `GET {webHost}/classes/com.korail.mobile.onepass.login.do` that returns a `userKey`, fed back into the `Login` endpoint (type `D`). The Kakao SDK is initialized in `KTApplication.onCreate` (`:126`).

Success/type helpers:
- `u.isLoginSuccess` (`S4/u.java:130-132`) treats response `h_msg_cd` of `IRZ000001` or `S200` as success.
- Login-tab codes for `txtInputFlg` come from `StbkAcntDao` constants: `ACCOUNT_REGISTER = "4"`, `CHANGE_PASSWORD = "5"`, `tab2 = "2"` (used in `K5/b.D0()`, ~`:119-122`).

### 2.10 Post-auth identity payload (`LoginDao.LoginResponse`)

`LoginResponse` (`LoginDao.java`, class body up to `:234`) extends `BaseResponse` and carries the member identity + auth flags, including:

- Base: `strResult`, `h_msg_cd`, `h_msg_txt`
- Identity: `strCustNo`, `strCustNm`, `strEmailAdr`, `strCpNo`, `strBtdt`, `strMbCrdNo`
- **Server-side encrypted ids:** `encryptCustNo`, `encryptMbCrdNo`, `encryptHMbCrdNo`
- Classification/auth flags: `strCustClCd`, `strCustDvCd`, `strCustSrtCd`, `strCustMgSrtCd`, `strSubtDcsClCd`, `strAthnFlg5`, `strAthnFlg7`, `strLognTpCd6`, `strHdcpFlg`/`strHdcpTpCd`/`strHdcpTpCdNm`, `strYouthAgrFlg`, `strCustLeadFlg`
- Account-integration prompt: `intgFlg`, `intgMsgTxt`, `intgUrl`
- 2nd-factor / redirect: `coupClsFlg`, `notiTpCd`, `dlayDscpInfo`, `strRedirectUrl`

### 2.11 Other crypto primitives & inventory

- `C0813m.e()` (`S4/C0813m.java:81-90`) uses `MessageDigest` **MD5 only to hash cache filenames** — not for auth.
- `F4.a.encryptBase64`/`decryptBase64` are plain Base64 (`NO_WRAP`).
- **Negative results (grep across the app package):** **no RSA / `KeyPairGenerator`, no HMAC, no PBKDF, no `SecureRandom`, no `EncryptedSharedPreferences`/`MasterKey`.** All in-app cryptography is symmetric **AES + Base64 + MD5**.

**Crypto helper summary:**

| Helper | Mode | Key | IV | Purpose |
|--------|------|-----|----|---------|
| `C0812l.encryptAES` | AES/CBC/PKCS5 | server-supplied `login.getKey()` | `key[0:16]` (not random) | wire password/amount cipher (§2.3) |
| `C0812l.getSid` | AES/CBC/PKCS5 | hardcoded `2485dd54d9deaa36` | same 16 bytes | anti-macro `sid` for search (§2.8) |
| `F4.a.encryptAES` | AES/ECB/PKCS5 | first 16 bytes of MD5-UUID of `android_id` | none (ECB) | on-device credential storage (§2.5) |

### 2.12 Transport security

- API base is HTTPS (`https://smart.letskorail.com`).
- `network_security_config.xml` sets `cleartextTrafficPermitted="true"` for **4 domains** (`1.255.59.22`, a Naver NCP dev load-balancer, `teapp.srail.kr`, `app.srail.kr`) and defines **no certificate pinning**.
- `AndroidManifest.xml` references `@xml/network_security_config` and sets `allowBackup="false"`.

**Obfuscation note:** the two crypto helpers are obfuscated single-letter classes — `S4/C0812l.java` (originally `l`) and `F4/a.java` (originally `a`) — and the UI login logic sits in obfuscated `K5/b.java` and `view/base/BaseActivity.java` (methods `E0`/`L`/`G`/`z0`). DAO/Service/Response class names and all request field names (`txtPwd`, `lognTpCd`, `custId`, …) are preserved by the Retrofit annotations.


## 3. Endpoint catalog by service domain

This section catalogs all client-reachable endpoints grouped by functional service domain (3.1–3.16). Each subdomain lists its Retrofit `*Service` interfaces / endpoints, WebView-tier calls where relevant, request field constants, and source references.

### 3.1 Auth / Login / Session

All endpoints in this domain are served from the runtime-resolved SSL host `https://smart.letskorail.com` (production, `SERVER_TYPE = REAL`). The host is chosen by `S4.z.getSSLHost()` (`analysis/jadx/sources/S4/z.java:46-49`), driven by `K4.g.SERVER_TYPE = EnumC5607a.fromCode("3")` = `REAL` (`analysis/jadx/sources/K4/g.java:90`, `analysis/jadx/sources/d5/EnumC5607a.java:9`). Dev/test/staging hosts (not used in this build) are `mobiledev.letskorail.com`, `dev3.letskorail.com`, `dev2.letskorail.com`.

The networking stack is legacy **square Retrofit 1.x** (`retrofit.http.*`, `RestAdapter`, `UrlConnectionClient`, `GsonConverter`) — synchronous blocking interface methods run inside an `AsyncTask`, with 60 000 ms connect/read timeouts (`analysis/jadx/sources/com/korail/talk/network/ExecuteDao.java:18-65`, `analysis/jadx/sources/com/korail/talk/network/BaseDaoHelper.java:24-97`). Every Retrofit request is `@FormUrlEncoded` POST **except `Logout` (a bare GET)**, and each carries three constant identity fields injected from `BaseRequest`: `Device="AD"`, `Version="250601003"`, `Key="korail1234567890"` (`analysis/jadx/sources/com/korail/talk/network/BaseRequest.java:7-18`). Session state is a plain `JSESSIONID` cookie held by a global `java.net.CookieManager` with `CookiePolicy.ACCEPT_ALL` (`analysis/jadx/sources/com/korail/talk/application/KTApplication.java:73-75`); there is **no bearer token / Authorization header anywhere** in this domain.

#### Retrofit endpoints (`LoginService` + `IndependentService`)

| Name | Method | Path | Key params | Response | Auth | Source |
|------|--------|------|------------|----------|------|--------|
| Member login (ID + password) | POST | `/classes/com.korail.mobile.login.Login` | `Device`, `Version`, `Key`, `txtMemberNo` (loginId), `txtPwd` (AES+Base64), `txtInputFlg` (loginType), `checkValidPw` (Y/N), `custId`, `etrPath` (always null), `idx` (from CommonCode) | `LoginDao.LoginResponse` (29 String fields + `strResult`/`h_msg_cd`/`h_msg_txt`) | **None — establishes the session**; on success `KTApplication.setSessionId()` propagates JSESSIONID to WebView | `analysis/jadx/sources/com/korail/talk/network/dao/login/LoginService.java:17-19`; impl `LoginDao.java:236-243` |
| Logout | **GET** | `/classes/com.korail.mobile.login.Logout` | *(none)* | `BaseResponse` | Session cookie only | `analysis/jadx/sources/com/korail/talk/network/dao/login/LoginService.java:29-30`; impl `LogoutDao.java:9-12` |
| Member identity check / user lookup | POST | `/classes/com.korail.mobile.login.userCheck` | `Device`, `Version`, `Key`, `txtAcptPsNm` (name), `acept` (lookup discriminator), `txtCpNo` (mobile), `memNum` (member no), `txtEmailNo` (email) | `MemberCertDao.MemberCertResponse` (`mbCrdNo`, `strCustNo`) | Session cookie (called from logged-in flows); no per-request auth field | `analysis/jadx/sources/com/korail/talk/network/dao/login/LoginService.java:13-15`; impl `MemberCertDao.java:78-83` |
| Social login registration (link account) | POST | `/classes/com.korail.mobile.login.loginAthnReg.do` | `Device`, `Version`, `Key`, `lognTpCd` (K/N/G/D), `custId` (provider user id) | `BaseResponse` | Session (invoked from MyPage while logged in) | `analysis/jadx/sources/com/korail/talk/network/dao/login/LoginService.java:21-23`; impl `LoginAthnRegDao.java:35-40` |
| Social login removal (unlink account) | POST | `/classes/com.korail.mobile.login.loginAthnRmv.do` | `Device`, `Version`, `Key`, `srvQryDvVal` (hardcoded `"U"`, **declared first**), `lognTpCd` (K/N/G/D) | `BaseResponse` | Session | `analysis/jadx/sources/com/korail/talk/network/dao/login/LoginService.java:25-27`; impl `LoginAthnRmvDao.java:35-40` |
| Member/password confirmation before withdrawal | POST | `/classes/com.korail.mobile.login.joinCfm.do` | `Device`, `Version`, `hmpgPwd` (**CLEARTEXT**), `custNm` — **no `Key` field** | `BaseResponse` | Session | `analysis/jadx/sources/com/korail/talk/network/dao/login/LoginService.java:32-34`; impl `MemberCheckDao.java:35-40` |
| Membership withdrawal (탈퇴) | POST | `/classes/com.korail.mobile.login.mbSced.do` | `Device`, `Version` only — **no `Key`, no user identifier** | `BaseResponse` | Session (identity is purely the JSESSIONID) | `analysis/jadx/sources/com/korail/talk/network/dao/login/LoginService.java:36-38`; impl `MemberDropDao.java:16-21` |
| Anti-macro warning acknowledgement | POST | `/classes/com.korail.mobile.login.poppCfmRec.do` | `Device`, `Version`, `Key`, `@FieldMap` (only key populated is `notiTpCd`) | `BaseResponse` (discarded) | Session (fires right after a successful login) | `analysis/jadx/sources/com/korail/talk/network/dao/independent/IndependentService.java:12-14`; caller `S4/C0811k.java:49-53` |

#### Web-tier / derived auth endpoints (WebView + server-directed handoff)

These are not Retrofit declarations. WebView pages are POSTed (or GET for `WEB_GET_URL`) with `Device=AD&Version=250601003&Key=korail1234567890` (`K4.g.COMMON_PARAMETER`, `K4/g.java:11`) auto-prepended by `BaseWebViewActivity.V0()/W0()` (`analysis/jadx/sources/com/korail/talk/ui/web/BaseWebViewActivity.java:1029-1049,1063-1087`) unless `IS_WEB_DEFAULT_PARAMETER=false`.

| Sign-up (회원가입) | POST | `/ebizmk/member/mk_join_member.do` | `Device`, `Version`, `Key` | HTML | None | `analysis/jadx/sources/K4/g.java:92`; launch `K5/b.java:397-401` |
| Find member ID (아이디 찾기) — legacy | POST | `/ebizmk/member/mk_check_member.do` | `Device`, `Version`, `Key` | HTML → found id as Intent extra `USER_ID` (resultCode 8) | None | `analysis/jadx/sources/K4/g.java:93`; launch `K5/b.java:344-350` |
| Find member password (비밀번호 찾기) — legacy | POST | `/ebizmk/member/mk_pass_member.do` | `Device`, `Version`, `Key`, `memberId` (login id EditText) | HTML (resultCode 9 clears pw field) | None | `analysis/jadx/sources/K4/g.java:94`; launch `K5/b.java:368-372` |
| Unified find-ID / find-password portal (통합 찾기) | GET | `/ticket/membership/findMember` (on **`https://www.korail.com`**) | `tabNm` (`memberNo`/`password`), `Device`, `Version`, `Key` | HTML (resultCode 8→`USER_ID`, 9→clears pw) | None | `analysis/jadx/sources/K4/g.java:16`; launch `K5/b.java:351-357,361-366` |
| Identity verification (본인확인, MCertify) — WebView | POST | `/classes/com.korail.mobile.certification.MCertify.do` | `Device`, `Version`, `Key` | HTML | Session (logged-in bank-account flows) | `analysis/jadx/sources/K4/g.java:111`; callers `StbkRegisterAccountListActivity.java:99` etc. |
| Phone authentication (휴대폰 인증, mCertify) — WebView | GET | `/classes/com.korail.mobile.mypage.mCertify.do` | `Device`, `Version`, `Key` (query string) | HTML (returns on requestCode 122) | Session (shown right after login) | `analysis/jadx/sources/K4/g.java:113`; dispatch `S4/u.java:112-121` |
| Common-code fetch — login prerequisite | POST | `/classes/com.korail.mobile.common.code.do` | `Device`, `Version`, `Key`, `code` (List), `deviceWidth`/`deviceHeight`, `departDate`/`arrivalDate`/`holidayYn`, `OSVersion` | `CommonCodeDao.CommonCodeResponse` (auth sub-objects `Login`, `EasyLogin`, `Athn`, `Data`) | None — called at app start (IntroActivity) | `analysis/jadx/sources/com/korail/talk/network/dao/common/CommonService.java:30-32` |

#### Notes

- **Three DAO variants hit the single `Login` endpoint.** `LoginDao` = manual ID/password login (`K5/b.java:102-113`); `AutoLoginDao extends LoginDao` = silent re-login using AES-at-rest credentials from SharedPreferences on session expiry (`BaseActivity.java:385-402`, `K5/b.java:211-225`); `EasyLoginDao extends LoginDao` = social login, sending **only** `loginType`+`custId`+`checkValidPw` with no `txtMemberNo`/`txtPwd`/`idx` (`K5/b.java:236-244`). Login success is `h_msg_cd ∈ {"IRZ000001", "S200"}` (`S4/u.java:130-132`).

- **Password encryption chain.** `txtPwd` is never a cleartext form field. When `CommonCodeDao.Login.pwdAESCphd == "Y"`: `txtPwd = Base64( Base64( AES/CBC/PKCS5Padding( key = CommonCode Login.key, IV = key.substring(0,16), plaintext ) ) )`; otherwise plain Base64 of the password. See `K5/b.java:124-140`, mirror in `BaseActivity.java:145-161`, AES primitive `S4/C0812l.java:18-24`, Base64 `F4/a.java:43-45`. The AES key and the `idx` form field both come from `CommonCodeDao.Login` (persisted to SharedPreferences key `"LOGIN_DATA"`, `K5/b.java:105,110`), so a **common-code fetch is a hard prerequisite for login**.

- **`etrPath` is always null** on login: it is declared in the interface (`LoginService.java:19`) and has a setter (`LoginDao.java:60-62`), but `setEtrPath()` is never invoked for a `LoginRequest` (only `TrainInquiryRequest` uses it).

- **`txtInputFlg` (loginType) enum**, derived from login-tab index (`K5/b.java:119-122`, `res/values/arrays.xml:213-217`): `"4"`=휴대폰번호 (phone), `"5"`=이메일주소 (email), `"2"`=회원번호 (membership number). Social values reuse the same field: `"K"`=Kakao, `"N"`=Naver, `"G"`=Google, `"D"`=Digital Onepass (`S4/u.java:126-128`).

- **No biometric/fingerprint login exists in this build** — a repo-wide grep over `com/korail/talk` finds only `Build.FINGERPRINT` (`IntroActivity.java:293`). `loginAthnReg.do`/`loginAthnRmv.do` are exclusively social-account **linking**, driven from `MyPageActivity.java:417-435`.

- **Simple/easy login sends no password** — `EasyLoginDao` sends only `loginType`+`custId`+`checkValidPw`, where `custId` is the provider user id (Kakao/Naver SDK, `GoogleSignInAccount.getId()`, or the Onepass `userKey` scraped from the WebView redirect, `OnepassWebViewActivity.java:64-70`).

- **`loginAthnRmv` argument-order inversion.** Unlike `loginAthnReg`, the DAO/interface places `srvQryDvVal` **before** `lognTpCd` (`LoginService.java:27`). `srvQryDvVal` is hardcoded `"U"` (`MyPageActivity.java:430`). Unlinking the currently-active provider forces a logout dialog (`MyPageActivity.java:575-582`).

- **Withdrawal is a 2-step flow:** `joinCfm.do` (verify `custNm` + `hmpgPwd`) → `mbSced.do` (actual withdrawal) → `LogoutDao` (`W5/a.java:28-35`, `W5/c.java:48-56`). Note `joinCfm.do` sends the password as **RAW CLEARTEXT** from the EditText (`W5/a.java:32`), unlike the AES-encrypted `Login` endpoint, and **both** `joinCfm.do` and `mbSced.do` omit the `Key` field entirely (`LoginService.java:34,38`).

- **DynaPath anti-macro protection.** `ExecuteDao.java:27` hardcodes exactly 6 protected paths (loop bound at line 31); `/classes/com.korail.mobile.login.Login` is one of them. When `I4.a.IS_MACRO_ACTIVE` is true (set from CommonCode `isMacroEnable == "Y"`, `IntroActivity.java:663`), the client attaches header `x-dynapath-m-token: <DynaPathMobileSDK.Companion.generate()>` (`ExecuteDao.java:44-47`; SDK init `IntroActivity.java:584`). Rejection is HTTP 403 with header `DynaPath-Result`; if its integer value `< 0` the JSON body `message` is shown as a macro-block dialog (`BaseDaoHelper.java:54-92`). None of the other endpoints in this domain are DynaPath-protected.

- **`poppCfmRec.do` side-channel.** Triggered when `LoginResponse.notiTpCd ∈ {MC, MM, MS}` and the user confirms the warning dialog built in `S4/u.java:57-92`. It is the **only endpoint bypassing the BaseDao framework** — executed on a bare `new ExecuteDao().getService(IndependentService.class)` with no loading dialog, error handling, or NetfunnelDao (`S4/C0811k.java:36-56`). *Nuance:* the `MM` branch in `S4/u.java:68-77` builds a map with `notiTpCd="MM"` but discards it and reuses the first map (`"MC"`), so a `notiTpCd=="MM"` response actually transmits `"MC"`; only `"MC"` and `"MS"` are ever sent, and the `MM` guard at `C0811k.java:50` is effectively dead code.

- **Session propagation.** After a successful login, `LoginDao.executeDao()` calls `KTApplication.getInstance().setSessionId()` (`LoginDao.java:241`), copying the JSESSIONID cookie into the Android WebView `CookieManager` (`KTApplication.java:105-131`) so WebView flows share the session.

- **Account-integration redirect** fires only when login is **not** successful **and** `strRedirectUrl` is non-null — the Korail↔integrated-account migration flow. It POSTs the full login response (including the `encrypt*` fields) to `getSSLHost()+strRedirectUrl` inside `IntegrationWebViewActivity` (`S4/u.java:22-49`).

- **Social-linkage STATE is not read from any auth endpoint.** It comes from `KorailPointInquiryDao` response fields `h_logn_tp_cd1` (Naver), `h_logn_tp_cd2` (Kakao), `h_logn_tp_cd4` (Google), `h_logn_tp_cd5` (Onepass), each `"Y"`/`"N"` (`analysis/jadx/sources/com/korail/talk/network/dao/xPoint/KorailPointInquiryDao.java:20-23`; consumed `MyPageActivity.java:214-235`).

- **Non-member "registration" makes no network call** — name/phone/5-digit password are stored purely in the local `b5.h` singleton (`analysis/jadx/sources/com/korail/talk/ui/login/nonMember/NonMemberRegisterActivity.java:66-76`). The endpoint that actually resolves a non-member identity (gift-ticket recipients, ticket delivery, companion-mileage lookups) is `login.userCheck`.

- **`userCheck` `acept` discriminator.** Exactly one of `memNum`/`txtCpNo`/`txtEmailNo` is populated per call: in gifticket/delivery flows `"1"`=member no, `"3"`=mobile, `"2"`=email (`GifticketBookingActivity.java:112-119`, `w5/ViewOnClickListenerC6622a.java:54-63`); in the companion-mileage flow `"4"`=member no, `"6"`=mobile, `"5"`=email (`CompanionMileageRequestActivity.java:80-87`).

- **Legacy vs unified find-ID/find-PW pages** are gated on the server-side feature flag `CommonCodeDao.Data.anyidhm`: `"Y"` routes to the unified `www.korail.com` portal (the only auth page not on `smart.letskorail.com`), otherwise the legacy `mk_check_member.do`/`mk_pass_member.do` pages are used (`K5/b.java:345-357,360-367`).

- **Transport security.** `network_security_config.xml` permits cleartext only for `1.255.59.22`, a naverncp bot host, `teapp.srail.kr` and `app.srail.kr` — none of the letskorail auth hosts — so every endpoint in this domain is **HTTPS-only** (`analysis/apktool/res/xml/network_security_config.xml`).

- **Obfuscation.** Retrofit interfaces and DAOs are **not** obfuscated (verified against `analysis/apktool/smali/com/korail/talk/network/dao/login/LoginService.smali`, which matches the jadx output). The UI/util layer **is** obfuscated: login fragments `K5/b.java` (base), `K5/a.java`, `K5/c.java`, `K5/d.java`; member-drop fragments `W5/a.java`, `W5/c.java`; helpers `S4/u.java`, `S4/C0811k.java`, `S4/z.java`, `S4/C0812l.java`, `F4/a.java`.


### 3.2 Secure Keypad (nFilter)

The "Secure Keypad (nFilter)" domain contains **exactly one endpoint** and is implemented by just two Java files (`NFilterService.java`, `NFilterCreateKeyDao.java`) under `/com/korail/talk/network/dao/nFilter/`. Despite the name, there is **no secure virtual-keypad component** in the APK: it ships **no `lib/` directory** and `assets/` contains no keypad module. The one endpoint is **unreachable dead code** in v6.5.0 — nothing constructs the DAO and its only domain-specific field is never read. The real login-password key comes from a different domain (`common.code.do`), documented as the second row below for reproduction purposes.

#### Endpoints

| Name | Method | Path | Key params | Response | Auth | Source |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| nFilter createKey (secure-keypad public-key issuance) | POST | `/classes/com.korail.mobile.nFilter.createKey.do` | `Device`="AD", `Version`="250601003", `Key`="korail1234567890" (all 3 are compile-time constants from `BaseRequest`; no request DTO) | `NFilterCreateKeyDao.NFilterCreateKeyResponse` extends `BaseResponse` → `publicKey:String` (+ envelope `h_msg_cd`, `h_msg_txt`, `strResult`). **`getPublicKey()` has zero callers.** | **None** — no token/session/signature; `x-dynapath-m-token` is NOT applied to this path | `NFilterService.java:10-12` (interface); `NFilterCreateKeyDao.java:22-27` (invocation); `NFilterService.smali` (annotations verified) |
| CommonCode — ACTUAL issuer of the login password-encryption key (cross-domain; canonical home is the **`common`** domain) | POST | `/classes/com.korail.mobile.common.code.do` | `Device`, `Version`, `Key`, `code:List<String>`, `deviceWidth`, `deviceHeight`, `departDate`, `arrivalDate`, `holidayYn`, `OSVersion` | `CommonCodeDao.CommonCodeResponse`; relevant block `login` → `CommonCodeDao.Login {idx, key, pwdAESCphd}`. Persisted to SharedPreferences as `"LOGIN_DATA"` | **None** — called at app start from `IntroActivity` before any login | `CommonService.java:30-32`; DTO `CommonCodeDao.java:668-687`; persisted `IntroActivity.java:630` |

Base URL for both is `https://smart.letskorail.com`, resolved by `z.getSSLHost()` (`S4/z.java:46-49`) with `SERVER_TYPE = EnumC5607a.fromCode("3") = REAL` hardcoded at `K4/g.java:90` (`d5/EnumC5607a.java`: DEV="0", STAGING="1", TEST="2", REAL="3").

#### Request field constants (`BaseRequest.java:7-18`)

| Field | Value | Source |
|---|---|---|
| `Device` | `"AD"` | `BaseRequest.ANDROID` (`:7,15`) |
| `Version` | `"250601003"` | `BaseRequest.VERSION` (`:9,16`) |
| `Key` | `"korail1234567890"` | `BaseRequest.APP_KEY` (`:8,17`) |

`NFilterCreateKeyDao` defines no request subclass — it reads a plain `BaseRequest` (`NFilterCreateKeyDao.java:25-26`), so these three defaults are always what would be transmitted. Content type is `application/x-www-form-urlencoded` (`@FormUrlEncoded`, `NFilterService.java:11`).

#### Notes

- **This is the only endpoint in the domain, and it is unreachable dead code in v6.5.0.** A repo-wide grep for `NFilterCreateKeyDao|NFilterService|NFilterCreateKeyResponse` across the full jadx tree hits only files inside the `nFilter` package; the same grep across the full apktool smali tree returns only the 3 self-referential smali files. No Activity, Fragment, or helper instantiates the DAO. `getPublicKey()` has zero callers, and the returned `publicKey` is never used for any client-side crypto. **To reproduce the login flow, skip this endpoint entirely.** (Docs additionally record that a live probe returns HTTP 404 — complementary to the static "dead code" finding.)

- **No RSA / public-key crypto is applied to the login password anywhere.** The name "nFilter" implies a secure keypad + PKI key exchange, but neither exists in the app. The `publicKey` field is inert.

- **The real login-key prerequisite is `common.code.do`, not this endpoint.** Reproduction chain:
  1. `POST /classes/com.korail.mobile.common.code.do` → read `response.login {idx, key, pwdAESCphd}` (`CommonCodeDao.java:668-687`), persisted as `"LOGIN_DATA"` (`IntroActivity.java:630`).
  2. Encrypt the password (`BaseActivity.java:145-161` `L()`, and identical `k5/b.java:124-140` `E0()`): if `pwdAESCphd == "Y"` then `txtPwd = Base64(AES(key, plaintext))` with an **outer Base64** again (double Base64); else `txtPwd = Base64(plaintext)`. AES is **AES/CBC/PKCS5Padding**, `SecretKeySpec` from `key.getBytes()`, **IV = first 16 chars of the same key** (`S4/C0812l.java:18-24`).
  3. `POST /classes/com.korail.mobile.login.Login` with `txtPwd` and `idx = login.idx` (`LoginService.java:17-19`). Note: `login.Login` IS in the DynaPath anti-macro whitelist, so it may require the `x-dynapath-m-token` header.

- **DynaPath anti-macro header does not apply here.** `x-dynapath-m-token` (`ExecuteDao.java:45-47`) is added only when `I4.a.IS_MACRO_ACTIVE` is true AND the URL matches one of 6 hardcoded paths (`ExecuteDao.java:27`): `certification.TicketReservation`, `nonMember.NonMemTicket`, `seatMovie.ScheduleView`, `seatMovie.ScheduleViewSpecial`, `trn.prcFare.do`, `login.Login`. The nFilter path is not among them.

- **Naming trap:** `nf.letskorail.com` (`KTApplication.java:78-86`) is **NetFunnel** (Naver traffic-queueing), NOT nFilter. Do not conflate the two.

- **Transport & session.** HTTPS only for `smart.letskorail.com`; it is not in the cleartext allowlist of `network_security_config.xml` (which permits cleartext only for 4 other domains) and no certificate pinning is configured. Session state rides on cookies via a process-wide `java.net.CookieManager` (`CookiePolicy.ACCEPT_ALL`) installed at `KTApplication.java:72-76`; no explicit `Cookie` header annotation appears.

- **Networking stack.** Legacy **Retrofit 1.x** (`retrofit.http.*`, `RestAdapter`, `UrlConnectionClient`, `GsonConverter`) over `java.net.HttpURLConnection` — not Retrofit 2 / OkHttp. Calls are synchronous (blocking return type) run on an AsyncTask (`BaseDaoHelper.java:43-46`); connect and read timeouts are both 60000 ms (`ExecuteDao.java:23-24`).

- **DAO behavior details.** `NFilterCreateKeyDao` overrides `isPending()` to return `false` (`:34-37`), so no loading spinner would show — consistent with a silent background bootstrap call. Its dispatch id is `f.dao_n_filter_create_key = 2131362311 / 0x7f0a0207` (`G4/f.java:386`, `R.java:2831`, `res/values/public.xml`), referenced only inside its own `getId()`.

- **Obfuscation.** The `nFilter` classes are NOT obfuscated (full class/field names and `@POST`/`@Field` string constants survive in smali). The surrounding utility layer IS obfuscated: `S4.z` (host resolution), `S4.C0812l` (AES), `F4.a` (Base64/AES prefs), `K4.g` (constants), `G4.f` (R.id mirror), `k5.b` (login fragment).

- Every nFilter response inherits the standard `BaseResponse` envelope (`BaseResponse.java:7-31`): `h_msg_cd`, `h_msg_txt`, `strResult` (`"SUCC"` on success, `"FAIL"` on failure), mapped via Gson `@SerializedName`.


### 3.3 Train Search / Schedule (seat availability)

This domain covers timetable browsing, live seat/car availability re-search, the operating-date calendar, and the discount-card / commuter-pass / trip-change lookups that share the `com.korail.mobile.research.*` and `com.korail.mobile.seatMovie.*` namespaces. It is split across four Retrofit **1.x** service interfaces: `SeatMovieService` (the three schedule views), `ResearchService` (car/seat re-search + N-card + commuter + merge-seat + trip-change), `CalendarService` (operating-date calendar), and one member of `TrainsInfoService` (`actualTrainSchedule.do`, which lives in the `research.*` namespace).

**Transport common to every endpoint below**

- Networking is **Retrofit 1.x** (`retrofit.http.*`, `RestAdapter`) over `UrlConnectionClient` (`java.net.HttpURLConnection`), Gson converter, 60 s connect/read timeouts — `analysis/jadx/sources/com/korail/talk/network/ExecuteDao.java:11-24`. This is **not** OkHttp/Retrofit2.
- Host is per-build via `S4.z.getSSLHost()`: REAL = `https://smart.letskorail.com`, TEST = `https://dev3.letskorail.com`, STAGING = `https://dev2.letskorail.com`, else `https://mobiledev.letskorail.com` — `analysis/jadx/sources/S4/z.java:46-49`, wired at `ExecuteDao.java:60`. Paths in the table are relative to that host. HTTPS only; no certificate pinning (`analysis/apktool/res/xml/network_security_config.xml`).
- Every request carries static app identifiers from `BaseRequest`: `Device="AD"`, `Version="250601003"`, and (where present) `Key="korail1234567890"` — `analysis/jadx/sources/com/korail/talk/network/BaseRequest.java:6-42`. These are app identifiers, **not** user auth.
- User session auth is a `JSESSIONID` cookie held by a global `java.net.CookieManager` (`CookiePolicy.ACCEPT_ALL`) installed as the default `CookieHandler` — `analysis/jadx/sources/com/korail/talk/application/KTApplication.java:72-75,103-115`. There is no `Authorization` header or bearer token anywhere in this domain.
- No `@Header`/`@Headers` annotations exist on any method here. `Content-Type: application/x-www-form-urlencoded` comes implicitly from `@FormUrlEncoded` (POST only); the `Cookie` comes from the `CookieHandler`; the conditional `x-dynapath-m-token` is attached by `ExecuteDao` (see notes).
- Every response extends `BaseResponse` — envelope fields `strResult` (`"SUCC"`/`"FAIL"`), `h_msg_cd`, `h_msg_txt` — `analysis/jadx/sources/com/korail/talk/network/BaseResponse.java:7-30`.

An exhaustiveness sweep (smali-wide grep of `com.korail.mobile.{research,seatMovie,schedule}.*` under `com/korail/talk`) returns exactly these 16 endpoints — no others exist in v6.5.0.

- **`Sid` anti-automation token** (sent on ScheduleView, LimousineScheduleView, TrainResearch, TResidualSeatsResearch): AES/CBC/PKCS5Padding, hardcoded key `"2485dd54d9deaa36"`, IV = first 16 bytes of that same key, Base64-encoded; plaintext = `"AD" + System.currentTimeMillis()` — `analysis/jadx/sources/S4/C0812l.java:18-50` (`getSid()` at :43-50). Note ScheduleViewSpecial and assignScheduleView do **not** send `Sid`.
- **DynaPath anti-macro protection**: when `I4.a.IS_MACRO_ACTIVE` is true, an `x-dynapath-m-token` header (from `DynaPathMobileSDK.Companion.generate()`) is attached only to a hardcoded URL allowlist that, in this domain, includes exactly `ScheduleView` and `ScheduleViewSpecial` (plus non-domain paths certification.TicketReservation, nonMember.NonMemTicket, trn.prcFare.do, login.Login) — `analysis/jadx/sources/com/korail/talk/network/ExecuteDao.java:26-53`. **LimousineScheduleView is not protected.** Rejection surfaces as HTTP 403 with a `DynaPath-Result` header (negative ⇒ block) and the JSON `message` is shown to the user — `analysis/jadx/sources/com/korail/talk/network/BaseDaoHelper.java:54-92`.
- **NetFunnel virtual-waiting-room** handshake (`nf.letskorail.com:443`, SSL) runs before the main ScheduleView/ScheduleViewSpecial search, with distinct action IDs for normal / product / peak-season traffic — `KTApplication.java:78-84`, `analysis/jadx/sources/B5/c.java:429-445` (dao wired at :112). Dao selection is done in `B5/c.java:101` (`ProductTrainInquiryRequest` → ScheduleViewSpecial, else ScheduleView).
- **`getRsvLimousineInquiry` is dead code** in v6.5.0: a smali-wide cross-reference finds no reference to `LimousineTrainInquiryDao` outside its own class file, unlike `TrainInquiryDao`/`ProductTrainInquiryDao` which are referenced from `B5/c.smali`.
- **`getTrainCalendar`** fires from `IntroActivity.v0()` at app start (`analysis/jadx/sources/com/korail/talk/ui/intro/IntroActivity.java:388-392`); its Dao `isPending()` returns false, so no loading dialog is shown — `analysis/jadx/sources/com/korail/talk/network/dao/schedule/TrainCalendarDao.java:106-119`. Interpretation helpers on the DTO: `isForSaleDate()` when `saleDdDvCd` is `"5"` or the account-register constant; `isPeakSeason()` when `bizDdStgCd=="5"`; `isHoliday()` when `hldyDvCd` non-empty.
- **`setNCardExtension` is a mutating action performed over GET**, and `tkRetPwd` (ticket return password) travels in the URL query string (cleartext over TLS). `getNCardHistory` and `setNCardExtension` are declared on `ResearchService` despite living in the `ticket.*` / `reservation.*` namespaces.
- **`getTrainSchedule` (actualTrainSchedule.do)** is declared on `TrainsInfoService`, not `ResearchService`, but lives in the `research.*` namespace so it belongs to this domain — `TrainsInfoService.java:40-42`.
- **Two wire/DTO name mismatches** to preserve for reimplementation: `custTripInfo.do` sends `regSqno` while the DTO property is `reqSqno`; `mergeSeatsC.do` sends `runDt` sourced from DTO property `runtDt`.
- **`getCarList` (TrainResearch) has no `.do` suffix**, unlike every other `research.*` endpoint. `isArrow` on `getSeatList` is hardcoded `true`; `sidTest` is `null` on REAL builds and `"Y"` otherwise (`SearchCarListDao.java:88`, `SearchSeatListDao.java:137`).
- **Name-collision warning**: `/classes/com.korail.mobile.lms.TResidualSeatsResearch.do` is a **bus** reservation endpoint (`BusReservationService.java:31`), not this train seat-search, despite the identical class-name suffix.


### 3.4 Train Info / Fare / Consist

All six endpoints of this domain are declared in a single Retrofit 1.x interface, `TrainsInfoService` (`analysis/jadx/sources/com/korail/talk/network/dao/trainsInfo/TrainsInfoService.java:19-43`). Every method is `@POST` + `@FormUrlEncoded`, so the wire `Content-Type` is always `application/x-www-form-urlencoded`; there are no JSON bodies, `@Headers`, or `@Multipart` in this domain.

- **Networking stack** — Retrofit 1.x (`retrofit.http.*`, `retrofit.RestAdapter`, `retrofit.client.UrlConnectionClient`) with a Gson converter, built in `analysis/jadx/sources/com/korail/talk/network/ExecuteDao.java:19`; connect/read timeouts are 60000 ms (`ExecuteDao.java:23-24`).
- **Base host** — chosen at runtime by `S4/z.java:46-49` `getSSLHost()`: production `https://smart.letskorail.com`; other build modes `https://dev3.letskorail.com` / `https://dev2.letskorail.com` / `https://mobiledev.letskorail.com`. Wired in `ExecuteDao.java:60` (`setEndpoint(z.getSSLHost())`).
- **Auth model** — no bearer token and no per-request signature. Every request carries the three static app-identity fields from `BaseRequest` (`analysis/jadx/sources/com/korail/talk/network/BaseRequest.java:7-18`): `Device=AD`, `Version=250601003`, `Key=korail1234567890` (a hard-coded `APP_KEY`, not a user secret). Session state, when it exists, rides the JVM-wide cookie jar via the `JSESSIONID` cookie — `CookieHandler.setDefault(new CookieManager(ACCEPT_ALL))` at `analysis/jadx/sources/com/korail/talk/application/KTApplication.java:72-76` (cookie handling `:105-116`). None of the 6 endpoints require login by construction; they are called from unauthenticated inquiry screens.
- **Transport / encryption** — plain HTTPS only. No per-field or per-body encryption is used in this domain (the generic `com.korail.mobile.common.encrypt.do` / `decrypt.do` endpoints are never invoked from any `trainsInfo` DAO). `network_security_config.xml` does **not** grant cleartext to `letskorail.com`, and no certificate pinning is configured (`analysis/apktool/res/xml/network_security_config.xml:3-8`).
- **DynaPath anti-macro signing** — applies to exactly one endpoint of this domain, `prcFare.do`. Only when the server-driven flag `I4.a.IS_MACRO_ACTIVE` is true (set from the common-code response at `analysis/jadx/sources/com/korail/talk/ui/intro/IntroActivity.java:663`, `isMacroEnable == "Y"`) **and** the request URL matches one of 6 hard-coded paths (`ExecuteDao.java:27`) is the header `x-dynapath-m-token` attached, produced by `DynaPathMobileSDK.Companion.generate()` (`ExecuteDao.java:45-47`; SDK init at `IntroActivity.java:584`). Rejection surfaces as HTTP 403 `Forbidden` with response header `DynaPath-Result` (negative integer) and a body `message` shown as the macro-block dialog (`analysis/jadx/sources/com/korail/talk/network/BaseDaoHelper.java:54-92`).
- **Execution** — all 6 DAOs run through `BaseDaoHelper.HttpTask` (AsyncTask) with a 100 ms `Thread.sleep` before each call (`BaseDaoHelper.java:43-46`). No Netfunnel (queue) gate is attached to any `trainsInfo` DAO.
- **Response envelope** — every response extends `BaseResponse` (`analysis/jadx/sources/com/korail/talk/network/BaseResponse.java:8-19`), carrying the only `@SerializedName`-annotated fields in the domain: `h_msg_cd`, `h_msg_txt`, `strResult` (values `SUCC` / `FAIL`). All other DTO fields map 1:1 to their Java field names via plain Gson.

#### Endpoint catalog

| Name | Method | Path | Key params | Response | Auth | Source |
|------|--------|------|------------|----------|------|--------|

Invoked from a WebView bridge; session on shared cookie jar. Not DynaPath-covered | `TrainsInfoService.java:40-42`; DAO `TrainScheduleDao.java:157-167`; caller `ui/web/TrainServiceInfoWebViewActivity.java:136-147` |

#### Notable details

- **`getTrainSchedule` is the only method that omits `Key`** — it sends only `Device`, `Version`, `runDt`, `trnNo` (`TrainsInfoService.java:42`); all other five send `Device`+`Version`+`Key`. Despite its `research.` path prefix it is declared in `TrainsInfoService` (not `ResearchService`), and it overlaps the delay/research domain. Its request field names are `txt*` locally (`txtRunDt`/`txtTrnNo`, `TrainScheduleDao.java:92-114`) but the wire names are `runDt`/`trnNo`; the caller zero-pads `trnNo` to 5 digits (`TrainServiceInfoWebViewActivity.java:142`, `N.addZero(5, ...)`). Per-stop timeline in `TimeInfo` (`TrainScheduleDao.java:12-90`) carries scheduled vs. actual times and delay minutes (`actArvDlayTnum:int`, `expnDptDlayTnum`, `expnArvDlayTnum`, `rgulFlg`, `saodFlg`).

- **`getPriceFare` (`TrainCharge`) is dead code in v6.5.0.** No caller exists in jadx sources nor in apktool smali — only `PriceFareDao.java` / `PriceFareDao*.smali` reference these classes. It is the legacy predecessor of `prcFare.do`; its nested response (`prc_fare_list.jrny_info[].prc_fare[]`) and `h_*`-prefixed field names reflect the older backend schema, unlike the flat camelCase `prcList` of `prcFare.do`. Note the confusingly-named live screen `ui/price/PriceFareActivity.java` actually drives `Price2FareDao`/`prcFare.do`, not `PriceFareDao`.

- **`setTrnCnt` self-assignment bug (shipped code).** `Price2FareRequest.setTrnCnt(String str)` executes `this.trnCnt = this.trnCnt;` (`Price2FareDao.java:170-172`). Although `PriceFareActivity.java:64` calls `setTrnCnt(...)`, the field stays `null`, so Retrofit 1 omits the `trnCnt` form field entirely on the wire. The real journey count reaches the server only via `chtnDvCd`.

- **`prcFare.do` multi-leg encoding.** For a transfer (2-leg) query the 8 `Price2FareParams` values are NOT repeated keys — each becomes a single **comma-joined** `leg0,leg1` string (`PriceFareActivity.java:91-98`); single-leg queries send bare values (`:81-88`). Observed values: `chtnDvCd` = `1` (직통/direct) or `2` (환승/transfer) from enum `K4/d.java:5-6`, passed via intent extra `JRNY_COUNT` (`A5/u.java:278`); `txtMenuId` defaults to `"11"` (`A5/k.java:92`) and is overridden per-flow by `ReservationRequest.getMenuId()` (`c5/a.java:49`).

- **Indirected Retrofit param-name constants** (resolved to wire names): `Price2FareDao.Price2Fare.trnNoString` = `"trnNo"` (`Price2FareDao.java:21`); `b5.C1262b.ARV_RS_STN_CD` = `"arvRsStnCd"` (`b5/C1262b.java:16`); `OJrny.TRN_GP_CD` = `"txtTrnGpCd"` (`OJrny.java:23`); `OSeat.SEAT_ATT_CD4` = `"txtSeatAttCd4"` (`OSeat.java:12`).

- **`fresScar.do` UX linkage.** Only `fresScarNo` is rendered by the UI — shown in the "자유석 정보" dialog (`A5/u.java:910-928`, string `common_free_car_info` at `analysis/apktool/res/values/strings.xml:257`); `fresTtl`/`fresCont` are parsed but unused there. Separately, the fare dialog toggles a free-seat row based on `TrainInfo.h_free_sracar_cnt > 0` (`PriceFareActivity.java:133-134`, string `dialog_service_fare_info` at `strings.xml:679`).

- **`chtnStn.do` synthetic "전체" entry.** The response `chtnList` is prepended client-side with a synthetic "all" entry `TrainSelectStationDao.getAllTransferStationInfo()` (`TrainSelectStationDao.java:63-65`, defaults `chtnRsStnCd=""` / `chtnRsStnNm="전체"`, `:48-49`) at `L4/h.java:298-309`.

- **`TourTrainSpecialRoom` request** transmits only `txtTrnGpCd` (sourced from the WebView URL query param `trnGpCd`, `IntegrationWebViewActivity.java:135`); `title`/`startStn`/`arrivalStn`/`jobDv` on `TourTrainInfoRequest` are client-side carry-through fields populated from WebView query params (`IntegrationWebViewActivity.java:136-139`) and forwarded as intent extras. The response `SeatInfos` is handed to `DiscountTourTrainBookingActivity.java:43` as serializable extra `TOUR_TRAIN_DATA`.

- **Upstream param source.** Every request param in this domain originates from `RsvInquiryResponse.TrainInfo` (`analysis/jadx/sources/com/korail/talk/network/response/seatMovie/RsvInquiryResponse.java:65`): e.g. `h_run_dt`→`runDt`, `h_trn_no`→`trnNo`, `h_dpt_stn_cons_ordr`→`dptStnConsOrdr`, `h_arv_stn_cons_ordr`→`arvStnConsOrdr`, `h_dpt_stn_run_ordr`→`dptStnRunOrdr`, `h_arv_stn_run_ordr`→`arvStnRunOrdr`, `h_trn_gp_cd`→`trnGpCd`, `h_trn_clsf_cd`→`stlbTrnClsfCd`, `h_seat_att_cd`→`rqSeatAttCd`, `txtGdNo`→`gdNo`, and `h_free_sracar_cnt` (free-car count).

- **Obfuscation note.** The app's own `com/korail/talk/**` classes (DAOs, services, DTOs) keep original readable names; the callers are obfuscated single-letter classes (`A5/u.java` inquiry-list base for fresScar, `L4/h.java` dialog for chtnStn, `A5/k.java`/`c5/a.java` menu-id providers). Helper utils are also obfuscated (`S4.z` host provider, `I4.a` constants).


### 3.5 Reservation (booking)

Endpoints that create, view, and mutate reservations: member/non-member ticket issue, single-reservation lookup, reserved-trip listing, seat assignment, seat-condition guidance, trip-change processing, and the reservation waitlist. All paths are relative to a single base URL resolved at call time from the build's `SERVER_TYPE` enum (`REAL=https://smart.letskorail.com`, `TEST=https://dev3.letskorail.com`, `STAGING=https://dev2.letskorail.com`, else `https://mobiledev.letskorail.com` — `analysis/jadx/sources/S4/z.java:46-49`, applied via `RestAdapter.setEndpoint` in `analysis/jadx/sources/com/korail/talk/network/ExecuteDao.java:59-61`).
