#### Notes

- **Two ticket-issuing calls, four overloads.** `TicketReservation` (member) and `NonMemTicket` (non-member) are the only calls that actually issue tickets. Each has a "full-field" overload and a Bixby "map-only" overload. Overload selection is purely client-side: `ReservationDao` picks `NonMemTicket` when both `custNm` and `cpNo` are non-null, otherwise `TicketReservation` (`ReservationDao.java:17`); `BixbyReservationDao` applies the same rule but additionally requires `custPw` (`BixbyReservationDao.java:34`). Non-member booking is blocked client-side when `txtMenuId=="41"`, `txtJobId=="1102"`, or a special-room seat code is selected (`ReservationRequest.java:105-119`) — a client-side-only restriction. `LReservationDao` is a limousine variant reusing the same `TicketReservation`/`NonMemTicket` branch under a different DAO id (`f.dao_l_reservation`).

- **DynaPath anti-macro token.** An `x-dynapath-m-token` header (from `DynaPathMobileSDK.Companion.generate()`) is attached ONLY when the request URL matches a hardcoded 6-entry allowlist, which includes both ticket-issuing paths (`com.korail.mobile.certification.TicketReservation`, `com.korail.mobile.nonMember.NonMemTicket`) plus `seatMovie.ScheduleView`, `seatMovie.ScheduleViewSpecial`, `trn.prcFare.do`, `login.Login` (`ExecuteDao.java:26-52`). The gate flag `I4.a.IS_MACRO_ACTIVE` is hardcoded `false` in this build (`I4/a.java:14`), so the token is not sent unless flipped at runtime. Server-side rejection surfaces as HTTP 403 with a `DynaPath-Result` header; a negative value causes the app to parse the JSON body's `message` field (`BaseDaoHelper.java:54-94`). None of the other reservation paths are in the allowlist.

- **Auth model.** No bearer token or signature anywhere. Every request carries three constant app-identifier params (`Device="AD"`, `Version="250601003"`, `Key="korail1234567890"` — `BaseRequest.java:7-18`). Actual authentication is the JSESSIONID cookie held by a process-wide `java.net.CookieManager` (`CookiePolicy.ACCEPT_ALL`) installed as the default `CookieHandler` (`KTApplication.java:72-76,105-116`). `ReservationView` returns the reservation list scoped entirely by the cookie — no customer identifier is sent (`TicketRsvHistoryDao.java:162-167`). Non-member `txtCustPw` is transmitted as a raw form field (`CertificationService.java:50,58`).

- **Two DTO generations, not interchangeable.** The LEGACY `O*` maps (`OJrny`/`OPsg`/`OSeat`/`OSrcar`, keys prefixed `txt*`) are used by the ticket-issuing calls; the NEWER `R*` maps (`RJrny`/`RSrcar`/`RSeat`/`RPsg`/`ROrtg`/`RDscp`, keys like `jrnySqno_1`, `seatPsrmClCd_1_1`) are used by `seatAssign.do`, `tripChgPrsC.do`, and `reservationChange.do`. Wire format is a flattened repeated-group encoding (prefix + 1- or 2-level indices), all `application/x-www-form-urlencoded` over TLS with Gson-parsed JSON responses. No body encryption or HMAC.

- **Same-path overloads on `ReservationList`.** `com.korail.mobile.certification.ReservationList` has two overloads with completely different parameter sets: `inquiryTicketRsv` (`hidPnrNo` only → full `ReservationResponse`) and `applyDisabilityCertification` (`hidPnrNo` + 6 QueryMaps → bare `BaseResponse`). The server presumably branches on the presence of `txtPsgDisc0019Cnt`. `applyDisabilityCertification` is a boundary endpoint belonging to the discount-certification domain, listed here only as the 2nd `ReservationList` overload.

- **Waitlist is registration-only.** There is exactly one waitlist endpoint (`reservationWait.ReservationWait`). No waitlist query or waitlist-cancel API exists; `ReservationWaitActivity` cancels through the reservation-cancel domain (`reservationCancel.ReservationCancel`, `ReservationWaitActivity.java:121-153`).

- **Domain-boundary / dedupe notes.** `reservationChange.do` carries a `reservation.` path prefix but is declared in `ReservationCancelService` and `BusReservationService`, not `ReservationService` — included here for completeness because it reuses the reservation `R*` DTOs and mutates a reservation. Other trip-change stages live elsewhere: `reservation.tripChgDate.do` (`TicketService.java:58`), `research.tripChgOgtk.do` (`ResearchService.java:61`), `ticket.tripChgHndgCnc.do` (`TicketService.java:98`); `reservation.dcntCrdExtn.do` (`ResearchService.java:65`) is discount-card, not booking. Only `tripChgPrsC.do` is the trip-change *processing* call owned by this domain.

- **Quirks in code.** (1) `TCReservationDao.TCReservationRequest` holds an `orgRDcp` (`RDscp`) field that is cloned in `clone()` but never transmitted — only `rDscp` reaches the service call (`TCReservationDao.java:36,212,222`; note field-name typo `orgRDcp` vs getter `getOrgRDscp()`). (2) Class-name typo `GudieSeatCndRequest` (for "Guide") is present in the app (`GuideSeatCndDao.java:11`). (3) `SeatAssignReservationResponse` is an empty subclass of `ReservationResponse` (`SeatAssignReservationDao.java:131-134`).

- **Transport.** Legacy square Retrofit 1.x (`retrofit.http.*`, `RestAdapter`, `GsonConverter`, `UrlConnectionClient`) over `HttpURLConnection`; every call is synchronous on an AsyncTask (`BaseDaoHelper.HttpTask`); connect/read timeouts both 60000 ms (`ExecuteDao.java:23-24`). `network_security_config.xml` permits cleartext only for a handful of non-letskorail hosts, so the reservation hosts are HTTPS-only; no certificate pinning is configured. The `com.korail.talk.network.**` layer (services, DAOs, request/response DTOs) is NOT obfuscated — Gson relies on the surviving field names matching the wire JSON.


### 3.6 Reservation Cancel / Change

예약취소 · 예약변경 domain of `com.korail.talk` v6.5.0. Covers cancelling unpaid reservations, rebooking (예약변경) onto a different date/train/seat, and the ticket-change (승차권 변경) hold/rollback calls.

**Domain-wide facts (apply to every row below):**

- **Host:** resolved at runtime by `S4.z.getSSLHost()` (`analysis/jadx/sources/S4/z.java:46-49`). `K4.g.SERVER_TYPE = EnumC5607a.fromCode("3") = REAL` (`analysis/jadx/sources/K4/g.java:91`, `analysis/jadx/sources/d5/EnumC5607a.java:6-9`), so the production host for all endpoints in this domain is **`https://smart.letskorail.com`**. Other branches: TEST=`https://dev3.letskorail.com`, STAGING=`https://dev2.letskorail.com`, DEV=`https://mobiledev.letskorail.com`.
- **Transport:** Retrofit 1.x (`retrofit.RestAdapter` + `retrofit.http.*`) over `java.net.HttpURLConnection` (`UrlConnectionClient`) with Gson conversion; connect/read timeouts 60000 ms (`analysis/jadx/sources/com/korail/talk/network/ExecuteDao.java:18-65`). Not OkHttp/Retrofit2.
- **Content type:** every endpoint except `productCancel` (the name-collision case) is `@FormUrlEncoded @POST`, i.e. `application/x-www-form-urlencoded`. No custom `@Header`/`@Headers` are set anywhere in the DAO/service layer for this domain.
- **Auth:** session-cookie based. Every request carries app-identifier form fields `Device=AD`, `Version=250601003`, `Key=korail1234567890` (constants in `analysis/jadx/sources/com/korail/talk/network/BaseRequest.java:7-18`) — these are **not** credentials. The user session is the `JSESSIONID` cookie, auto-attached by a global `java.net.CookieManager` (`CookiePolicy.ACCEPT_ALL`) installed as the default `CookieHandler` (`analysis/jadx/sources/com/korail/talk/application/KTApplication.java:73-75`, read back by name at `:110`).
- **No signing / no payload encryption** for any cancel/change endpoint. TLS only. The DynaPath token (`x-dynapath-m-token`) is applied only when `I4.a.IS_MACRO_ACTIVE` and the URL is one of 6 hard-coded paths (TicketReservation, NonMemTicket, ScheduleView, ScheduleViewSpecial, trn.prcFare.do, login.Login — `ExecuteDao.java:26-53`); none of the cancel/change paths are in that list.

#### Core endpoints

| Name | Method | Path | Key params | Response | Auth | Source |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| `reservationCancel` — 미결제 예약 취소 (initiate cancel) | POST | `/classes/com.korail.mobile.reservationCancel.ReservationCancel` | `Device`,`Version`,`Key`; `txtPnrNo`; `txtJrnySqno` (`OJrny.JRNY_SQ_NO`, callers send `"0001"`); `txtJrnyCnt` (`OJrny.JRNY_CNT`); `hidRsvChgNo` (callers send `"000"`) | `BaseResponse` (`h_msg_cd`,`h_msg_txt`,`strResult` SUCC/FAIL) | JSESSIONID cookie | `ReservationCancelService.java:15-17` |
| `reservationCancelCheck` — 예약취소 확정/완료 (confirm/complete) | POST | `/classes/com.korail.mobile.reservationCancel.ReservationCancelChk` | Identical to `reservationCancel`: `Device`,`Version`,`Key`,`txtPnrNo`,`txtJrnySqno`,`txtJrnyCnt`,`hidRsvChgNo` | `BaseResponse` | JSESSIONID cookie | `ReservationCancelService.java:19-21` |
| `reservationChange` — 예약 변경 (rebook onto new date/train/seat) | POST | `/classes/com.korail.mobile.reservation.reservationChange.do` | `Device`,`Version`,`Key`; `pnrNo`; `chgTno` (`=h_rsv_chg_no`); `totPrnb`; `stndFlg`/`evntWctFlg`/`wctHndgCncDvCd`/`lrgCrgFlg` (all `"N"`); `psgCnt` (`RPsg.PSG_CNT`); **5× `@FieldMap`** — RJrny, RSrcar, RSeat, RPsg, RDscp (index-suffixed keys) | `ReservationChangeDao.ReservationChangeResponse` (`jrnyList:List<JrnyInfo{lumpStlTgtNo}>` + BaseResponse) | JSESSIONID cookie | `ReservationCancelService.java:23-25` |
| `getTripChgDate` — 변경 가능 날짜 조회 (list legal new dates) | POST | `/classes/com.korail.mobile.reservation.tripChgDate.do` | `Device`,`Version`,`Key`; `tripChgDate` (original ticket `h_dpt_dt`, yyyyMMdd) | `TripChgInfoDao.TripChgInfoDaoResponse` (`lastRunDt`, `tripChgDate`, `tripChgDates:List<String>` + BaseResponse) | JSESSIONID cookie | `TicketService.java:58-60` |
| `ticketChangeCancel` (tripChgHndgCnc) — 변경 진행 취소 / 일괄결제 홀드 해제 | POST | `/classes/com.korail.mobile.ticket.tripChgHndgCnc.do` | `Device`,`Version`,`Key`; `lumpStlCnt` (caller sends `"1"`); **`@FieldMap`** with keys `lumpStlTgtNo_N` (value from `jrny_info[0].lumpStlTgtNo`) | `BaseResponse` | JSESSIONID cookie | `TicketService.java:98-100` |

All paths above verified against apktool smali (`grep -rhno 'com\.korail\.mobile\.[A-Za-z0-9._]*' smali*/`): the jadx path set is complete; no hidden or dynamically-built endpoints exist in this domain.

#### Adjacent / boundary endpoints (trip-change call chain, primary ownership elsewhere)

Included so the full 승차권 변경 chain `tripChgOgtk → tripChgDate → tripChgPrsC → tripChgHndgCnc` is traceable. `tripChgPrsC` lives on `ReservationService` (Reservation/Booking domain); `tripChgOgtk` lives on `ResearchService` (Ticket Inquiry domain).

| Name | Method | Path | Key params | Response | Auth | Source |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| `getTicketChangeReservation` (tripChgPrsC) — 변경 예약 생성 | POST | `/classes/com.korail.mobile.reservation.tripChgPrsC.do` | `Device`,`Version`,`Key`; `trvlKndCd`,`totPrnb`,`isePrnb`,`stndSeatFlg`,`intgTktIseFlg`,`prcFareReCalcFlg`,`tmpJobSqno`,`alcSeatDmnPsDvCd`,`jrny2Cnt`,`psg2Cnt`,`ctlDvCd`,`frcSaleRsnCont`; 6× `@FieldMap` | `ReservationResponse` (extends BaseResponse) | JSESSIONID cookie | `ReservationService.java:24-26` |
| `getTicketOriginalInquiry` (tripChgOgtk) — 변경 대상 원권 조회 | POST | `/classes/com.korail.mobile.research.tripChgOgtk.do` | `Device`,`Version`,`Key`; `tkCnt:int`; `@FieldMap` (original-ticket key block) | `OgTkInquiryDao.OgTkInquiryResponse` (`getOrgTkList()` → `getJrnyList()`) | JSESSIONID cookie | `ResearchService.java:61-63` |

#### Excluded — name collision only (NOT part of this domain)

| Name | Method | Path | Key params | Response | Auth | Source |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| `productCancel` — 여행상품 예약 취소 | **GET** | `/classes/com.korail.mobile.product.ReservationCancel` | `@Query`: `Device`,`Version`,`Key`,`txtVrRsNo`,`txtGdSqno` | `BaseResponse` | JSESSIONID cookie | `ProductService.java:21-22` |

This cancels a **travel-product (여행상품)** order — GET + `@Query`, keyed by `txtVrRsNo`/`txtGdSqno` (not PNR). Its final path segment `ReservationCancel` collides with `com.korail.mobile.reservationCancel.ReservationCancel`; do not conflate the two.

#### Notable

- **Cancel is a two-step flow, and the ordering is: `ReservationCancel` FIRST, `ReservationCancelChk` SECOND.** The two endpoints take identical parameter lists and both return a bare `BaseResponse`, but they are NOT "pre-check then execute". Verified in `DReservationConfirmActivity.onReceive` (`analysis/jadx/sources/com/korail/talk/ui/reservation/confirm/activity/DReservationConfirmActivity.java:408-437`): the `dao_rsv_cancel` (= `ReservationCancel`) response arrives first and triggers the "취소하시겠습니까?" dialog (`common_reservation_cancel_message`, `:414`); on confirm, `G0()` → `D0()` (`:118-142`) fires `ReservationCancelChk`; the `dao_rsv_cancel_check` response then shows the "취소 완료" dialog (`common_reservation_cancel_complete_message`, `:425`). So `ReservationCancel` = step 1 (initiate) and `ReservationCancelChk` = step 2 (confirm/complete). Same pattern in `ReservationWaitActivity` (~`:250-264`) and `LimousineActivity` (~`:374`+). This matches `docs/deep-dive/agent-reports/10-refund-delay-compensate-cancel.md` (lines 141-146).
- **Hard-coded caller values (single-leg case):** `txtJrnySqno="0001"`, `hidRsvChgNo="000"` at `DReservationConfirmActivity.java:274-275`, `ReservationWaitActivity.java:123-124`, `LimousineActivity.java:139-140`, `LimousineSelectSeatActivity.java:324-325`, `DirectInquiryActivity.java:233-234`,`:246-247`. Only `ReservedTicketActivity.java:285-286` passes them as variables (multi-leg / already-changed reservations).
- **Dead-code dedupe (confirmed):** `BusReservationService` declares byte-identical copies of `reservationCancelCheck` (`analysis/jadx/sources/com/korail/talk/network/dao/certification/BusReservationService.java:19-21`) and `reservationChange` (`:23-25`). These are never invoked — `BusReservationService` is instantiated only at `BusReservationSeatListDao.java:272` and `BusReservationListDao.java:329`, which call `reservationSeatList`/`reservationList`. All live cancel/change traffic goes through `ReservationCancelService`. Note: `docs/api-endpoints.md` rows 61-62 cite the dead `BusReservationService` copies as the source ref.
- **`AutoRsvCancelDao` / `AutoRsvCancelCheckDao` are not separate endpoints.** They subclass `RsvCancelDao`/`RsvCancelCheckDao`, add only a client-side `trainInfo` field that is never serialized, and override `getId()` for UI-callback routing (`AutoRsvCancelDao.java:26-29`, `AutoRsvCancelCheckDao.java:26-29`). They hit the exact same two cancel paths — used by the auto-rebooking flow in `DirectInquiryActivity.java:228-247`.
- **`reservationChange.do` wire format:** the 5 `@FieldMap`s are `LinkedHashMap` subclasses (`RJrny`,`RSrcar`,`RSeat`,`RPsg`,`RDscp`) whose keys already carry `_N` / `_N_M` index suffixes, so the body flattens to e.g. `jrnySqno_1`, `trnNo_1` (zero-padded 5 digits), `seatPsrmClCd_1_1`, `psgTpDvCd_1`, `dcntKndCd_1_1`. Request built by `w4.a.getReservationChangeRequest(ReservationResponse, m5.e)` (`analysis/jadx/sources/w4/a.java:120-242`; package declares `W4`). `chgTno` = `jrny_info[0].h_rsv_chg_no` (`w4/a.java:136`). Observed discount codes: `dcntKndCd` 321=child-accompany, 131=senior, 111=severe disability, 112=mild disability; `psgTpDvCd` 1=adult/senior/disabled, 3=child (`w4/a.java:169-232`). Fixed `"N"` flags: `stndFlg`,`evntWctFlg`,`wctHndgCncDvCd`,`lrgCrgFlg` (`w4/a.java:131-134`). Field-name source: `analysis/jadx/sources/com/korail/talk/network/data/reservation/{RJrny,RSrcar,RSeat,RPsg,RDscp}.java`; `RDscp` constant names say `OGRK` but the wire keys are `Ogtk*`.
- **`ReservationCancel` / `ReservationCancelChk` field names** come from `OJrny`: `@Field(OJrny.JRNY_SQ_NO)="txtJrnySqno"`, `@Field(OJrny.JRNY_CNT)="txtJrnyCnt"` (`analysis/jadx/sources/com/korail/talk/network/data/reservation/old/OJrny.java:18-19`).
- **`tripChgDate.do` is a read-only lookup**, not a mutation — it returns legal new departure dates. Called from `TCBookingActivity.java:187-191` with the original ticket's `h_dpt_dt`; `response.tripChgDates` drives the calendar at `:276`. Declared on `TicketService` despite the `com.korail.mobile.reservation.*` prefix. Response getter for `tripChgDate` is misspelled `geTtripChgDate()` (`TripChgInfoDao.java:37`).
- **`tripChgHndgCnc.do` rolls back an in-progress ticket-change hold**, silently. Fired on back-press when `reservationType == TICKET_CHANGE` (`DReservationConfirmActivity.java:314`,`:361`) with `lumpStlCnt="1"` and `lumpStlTgtNo_1` from `jrny_info[0].lumpStlTgtNo` (`:282-290`). Dispatched with `setNotShowDialog(true)` (`:288`), i.e. errors are swallowed (fire-and-forget). The `lumpStlTgtNo` it consumes is produced upstream by `reservationChange.do`'s `JrnyInfo.lumpStlTgtNo`.
- **No Netfunnel (virtual waiting-room) gating** on this domain. `NetfunnelDao` exists and host `nf.letskorail.com` is configured (`KTApplication.java:81`), but no DAO in this domain references it.
- **Cleartext policy:** `res/xml/network_security_config.xml` permits cleartext only for `1.255.59.22`, `teapp.srail.kr`, `app.srail.kr` — none serve this domain, so `letskorail.com` hosts fall under the default TLS-required policy. No certificate pinning is declared.
- **Client-side coverage** (`src/korail_mobile_api`): only `tripChgDate.do` is live — `client.get_trip_change_dates()` with `{Device,Version,Key,tripChgDate}`, whitelisted as an allowed read in `safety.py`. `build_unpaid_reservation_cancel_form()` exists but is not wired (latent). `ReservationCancelChk`, `reservationChange.do`, `tripChgHndgCnc.do`, `tripChgPrsC.do`, `tripChgOgtk.do` are not implemented.


### 3.7 Payment

The Payment domain covers reservation/cart/pass payment, PG & easy-pay (간편결제) integrations, settlement-key & bank-account management, cash receipts, ticket/delay receipts, and the card-crypto helper endpoints used to build a payment body.

**Common characteristics (apply to every endpoint below unless noted):**

- **Transport:** Retrofit **1.x** (`retrofit.http.*`, `retrofit.RestAdapter`, `UrlConnectionClient`, `GsonConverter`), connect/read timeout 60000 ms — `analysis/jadx/sources/com/korail/talk/network/ExecuteDao.java:18-65`.
- **Base URL:** `S4.z.getSSLHost()` → production `https://smart.letskorail.com` (dev builds: `dev3`/`dev2`/`mobiledev.letskorail.com`) — `analysis/jadx/sources/S4/z.java:46-49`; host constants `analysis/jadx/sources/K4/g.java:20-25`.
- **Encoding:** every payment call is `@FormUrlEncoded` POST (`Content-Type: application/x-www-form-urlencoded`), except `getAutoCharge` and `paymentCheck` which are GET with `@Query` params.
- **Auth:** implicit **session cookie** (JSESSIONID from `login.Login`), auto-attached by the global `CookieManager` (`CookiePolicy.ACCEPT_ALL`) installed in `analysis/jadx/sources/com/korail/talk/application/KTApplication.java:72-75`. No Bearer/OAuth token, no per-request auth header. The anti-macro `x-dynapath-m-token` header is **not** applied to any payment URL (`ExecuteDao.java:27-52`).
- **App-level credentials on every call:** `Device="AD"`, `Version="250601003"`, `Key="korail1234567890"` — injected from `BaseRequest` (`analysis/jadx/sources/com/korail/talk/network/BaseRequest.java:7-18`). Static, not per-user.
- **Every response extends `BaseResponse`** — `strResult` ("SUCC"/"FAIL"), `h_msg_cd`, `h_msg_txt` (`analysis/jadx/sources/com/korail/talk/network/BaseResponse.java:7-30`).
- **NetFUNNEL gate:** the actual payment calls (`ReservationPayment`, `intgStl`, `passPayIssue`, `passOtrPayIssue`) are gated by the virtual-waiting-room action `act_18` (`NETFUNNEL_ACTION_PAY_ID`, `analysis/jadx/sources/K4/g.java:45`) before the pay button proceeds — `analysis/jadx/sources/B6/AbstractC1269e.java:1046`, `analysis/jadx/sources/B6/C1270f.java:232`. NetFUNNEL host `nf.letskorail.com` (`KTApplication.java:81-82`).

Source paths in the tables below are relative to `analysis/jadx/sources/`.

#### 3.7.2 The `PaymentMethod` settlement body

The generic pay calls (`ReservationPayment`, `intgStl`, `passPayIssue`, `passOtrPayIssue`) carry an open key/value `@FieldMap` supplied by `PaymentMethod extends LinkedHashMap<String,String>` (`com/korail/talk/network/request/payment/PaymentMethod.java:6-139`), populated by the request builder `v4/a.java:21-345`. Keys are indexed per settlement means (`{n}`):

- Row control: `hidInrecmnsGridcnt`, `hidStlMnsSqno{n}`, `hidStlMnsCd{n}`, `hidMnsStlAmt{n}`, `hidCrdInpWayCd{n}`.
- Card: `hidStlCrCrdNo{n}`, `hidVanPwd{n}`, `hidCrdVlidTrm{n}`, `hidIsmtMnthNum{n}`, `hidAthnDvCd{n}`, `hidAthnVal{n}`; Shinhan FAN: `shStlCrCrdNo{n}`, `shCrdVlidTrm{n}`.
- Point: `hidPontDvCd{n}`, `hidPontInpDvCd{n}`, `hidPontCrdPwd{n}`.
- Discount / deferred payment: `hidDscpMgNo{n}`, `hidDfpyDscpNo{n}`, `hidDfpySrtCd{n}`.
- Member/non-member: `hiduserYn`, `hidMbCrdNo`.
- Easy-pay: `spayDvCd_1_1`/`spayDvCd_2_1`, `spayCphdDatVal_1_1`/`spayCphdDatVal_2_1`, `stSpayGridcnt_1`/`stSpayGridcnt_2`.

`IPaymentRequest.getEasyPaymentMethod()` returns `paymentMethod.get("spayDvCd_1_1")` (`.../request/payment/IPaymentRequest.java:4-6`), implemented by all four generic pay request DTOs.

**`hidStlMnsCd` (settlement means code):** `02`=credit card, `03`=congress/deferred payment (dfpy), `12`=point, `13`=RailPlus, `14`=account/e-money (`v4/a.java:26,61,120,144,167,286`).

**`spayDvCd` (easy-pay method code), from `v4/a.java:69-216`:** RailPlus=`00`, KakaoPay(card)=`01`, PAYCO(`mainPgCode==31`)=`02` / PAYCO(other)=`07`, Settlebank(stbk)=`03`, ZeroPay=`04`, NaverPay=`05`, KakaoPay Money=`08`, Samsung/point path=`09`, stbkAcnt(내통장결제)=`10`, PayBooc=`11`, TossPay(card)=`12`, TossPay TOSS_MONEY=`13`, KB Pay=`15`, NaverPay Money=`16`, Monimo=`19`. Shinhan FAN uses no `spayDvCd` (populates `shStlCrCrdNo`/`shCrdVlidTrm` instead, `v4/a.java:76-87`).

#### 3.7.3 Notable details

- **Field order & non-transmitted fields:** `ReservationPayment` sends `hidPnrNo, wctNo, jobSqNo1, jobSqNo2, hidRsvChgNo` then the `PaymentMethod` map (`RsvPaymentDao.java:131`; builder `v4/b.java:36-42`). `intgStl` holds `hidRsvChgNo` in its request object but does **not** transmit it (`IntgStlDao.java:76`). `SpayCphdDatVal` transmits only `spayDvCd`+`data` (`mainPgCode`/`paymentMethodType`/`type` are local-only, `SpayCphdDatValDao.java:83`). `SpayOdrNo.isMonimo` is local-only (set true when `spayDvCd=="19"`, `AbstractC1269e.java:817-819`). `Decrypt.easyPayType`/`mAutoChargeRequestType` are local-only routing hints (`DecryptDao.java:80`).
- **Amount-tampering guard:** `encTotTxnAmt` on `spayOrdNo` is `Base64(AES/CBC/PKCS5Padding(loginKey, amount))` with IV = first 16 bytes of the per-session login key, applied only when `login.pwdAESCphd=="Y"` (else plain `Base64(amount)`) — `S4/C0812l.java:18-41`, called at `B6/AbstractC1269e.java:822`.
- **Card crypto pipeline:** PANs/passwords are encrypted **server-side** before payment via `shinhan.Encrypt.do` (SEED) and `common.encrypt.do`; the secure-keypad public key comes from `nFilter.createKey.do`, which is fed to the nFilter keypad before the value enters `PaymentMethod` (`hidVanPwd`/`hidAthnVal`). Payment bodies themselves carry no request signing.
- **`SpayCphdDatVal.data` contents by PG** (`AbstractC1269e.java:773-799`): PAYCO=`[reserveOrderNo, sellerOrderReferenceKey, paymentCertifyToken, cardBin]`; Kakao=`[spayTid, prprNo, pg_token]`; PayBooc=`[appIdauTno, cardBinNo, tknTid]`.
- **PAYCO/Toss-auto redirect:** `getPaycoResult` nests the PG URL at `recvData.result.orderSheetUrl`, loaded in `EasyPayWebViewActivity`, which intercepts custom schemes and a `type=tossauto` query param (`.../ui/web/EasyPayWebViewActivity.java:28-46`); `tossautoC.checkoutAndroidUri` uses the same web activity.
- **Settlebank `jobDvCd` constants:** `ACCOUNT_NAME_CHECK="1"`, `ARS_CERTIFICATION="2"`, `ARS_RESULT_CONFIRM="3"`, `ACCOUNT_REGISTER="4"`, `CHANGE_PASSWORD="5"`, `DELETE_ACCOUNT="9"` (`StbkAcntDao.java:10-15`), used across `.../ui/stbk/*`.
- **Toss settlement-key callers:** `stlKeyQry` is always called with `spayDvCd="12"` (`AbstractC1269e.java:853`, `TossAutoSettingActivity.java:187`); `stlKeyPrs` is called with `jobDvCd="4"` (ACCOUNT_REGISTER) for delete/register of a saved key (`TossAutoSettingActivity.java:174`).
- **Cash receipt is auto-fired** after payment success as a `HashMap` FieldMap of `lumpStlTgtNo_1..N`; `isPending()` is overridden to false so it runs silently, and `vltIsuFlg="N"` when the user requested a receipt, `"Y"` (자진발급) otherwise (`CashReceiptIssueDao.java:84-87`; orchestration `AbstractC1269e.java:603-643`).
- **Naver Pay Money reuses the Naver Pay response type** — `NaverPayMoneyRsvDao` declares no own Response class (returns `NaverPayRsvDao.NaverPayRsvResponse`). Subsequent payment uses `spayDvCd="16"` (money) vs `"05"` (card) — `v4/a.java:193,201`.
- **Pass payments return `PassPaymentDao.MainInfo`** — `CommPaymentResponse.main_info` reuses `PassPaymentDao.MainInfo`, not an inner class of its own (`CommPaymentDao.java:55,65`).
- **Security observations:** No certificate pinning for letskorail hosts — `network_security_config.xml` only whitelists cleartext for `1.255.59.22`, a Naver NCP bot host, `teapp.srail.kr`, `app.srail.kr` (`analysis/apktool/res/xml/network_security_config.xml`). The RailPlus prepaid card number (`prepCrdNo`) travels in the **URL query string** of `getAutoCharge` (GET). `getEncrypt` and `getKBPayEncrypt` share the same path `common.encrypt.do`, differentiated only by the `type` value and the response DTO used. The `stbkRegBank` DTO getter for `regPsbList` is misnamed `getRegPsbLists()` (`StbkRegBankDao.java:71`); the `seedEncrypt` request field is singular `value` (not `values`).
- **Obfuscation:** DAOs/DTOs under `com/korail/talk/network/**` keep full names, but payment UI/orchestration is obfuscated — fragment base `B6.AbstractC1269e` / subclass `B6.C1270f`, request builders `v4.a`/`v4.b`, crypto helper `S4.C0812l`, host resolver `S4.z`, constants `K4.g`, NetFUNNEL wrapper `T6.g`/`T6.h`.


### 3.8 Ticket / My Ticket / Check-in

Post-purchase ticket lifecycle: listing owned/purchased tickets, seat/room upgrades, duplicate checks, platform lookup, trip-change and self seat-change info, self check-in (register / cancel / info / possibility), guardian relief SMS, ticket delivery (recipient lookup, history, PBP accept/withdraw), and MaaS add-service cancel/detail. Two service interfaces cover the domain: `TicketService` (19 endpoints) and `MyTicketService` (3 endpoints) = **22 endpoints total**.

**Transport / conventions common to every endpoint below:**

- **Stack:** Retrofit **1.x** (`retrofit.http` annotations) over `retrofit.client.UrlConnectionClient` (plain `java.net.HttpURLConnection`), **not** OkHttp/Retrofit2. All service methods are **synchronous** (return the response object directly). See `analysis/jadx/sources/com/korail/talk/network/ExecuteDao.java:11-19`.
- **Base host** is chosen at runtime by a server-type enum: `REAL=https://smart.letskorail.com`, `TEST=https://dev3.letskorail.com`, `STAGING=https://dev2.letskorail.com`, fallback `https://mobiledev.letskorail.com` (`analysis/jadx/sources/S4/z.java:46-49`; injected at `ExecuteDao.java:60`). Paths in the table below are relative to that host; production is `smart.letskorail.com`.
- **Constant params on every request** (from `BaseRequest`): `Device="AD"`, `Version="250601003"`, `Key="korail1234567890"` (hardcoded static app key) — `analysis/jadx/sources/com/korail/talk/network/BaseRequest.java:7-18`. **Three endpoints omit `Key`** (`addService.cancelPay.do`, `addService.coptCnc.do`, `copt.gdReqQry.do`). These constants are left out of the "Key params" column below for brevity.
- **Auth = JSESSIONID session cookie**, not a token/header. A global `java.net.CookieManager` (`ACCEPT_ALL`) is installed as `CookieHandler.setDefault()` in `KTApplication.f()` (`analysis/jadx/sources/com/korail/talk/application/KTApplication.java:72-76`, read back at `:105-116`); `HttpURLConnection` attaches it automatically. **No `Authorization` header exists on any endpoint in this domain.**
- **No per-request encryption or signing** anywhere in this domain. The anti-macro `x-dynapath-m-token` header is applied only to a 6-URL whitelist (none in this domain) at `ExecuteDao.java:27-52`.
- All POST endpoints are `@FormUrlEncoded` ⇒ implicit `Content-Type: application/x-www-form-urlencoded`. The two upgrade-seat endpoints are `@GET` with `@Query` params and carry sensitive data in the URL query string.
- Connect/read timeouts are both 60000 ms; HTTP keep-alive is globally disabled (`ExecuteDao.java:23-24`, `KTApplication.java:92-94`). Netfunnel queueing is **not** attached to any DAO in this domain.

Every response extends `BaseResponse { h_msg_cd, h_msg_txt, strResult }` (SUCCESS=`"SUCC"`, FAIL=`"FAIL"`) — `analysis/jadx/sources/com/korail/talk/network/BaseResponse.java:7-19`. In the Response column, `BaseResponse` means only those three fields are returned.

#### Notable points

- **Path typo (bug):** `getMaasCancel` is annotated `@POST("/classes//com.korail.mobile.addService.cancelPay.do")` — a literal double slash after `/classes` (`TicketService.java:38`). Reproduced verbatim in the table.
- **`Key`-omitting endpoints:** `addService.cancelPay.do`, `addService.coptCnc.do`, and `copt.gdReqQry.do` send only `Device`/`Version`, not the `Key="korail1234567890"` app key (`TicketService.java:40,44,52`).
- **Sensitive data in GET query strings:** the two `MyTicketService` upgrade endpoints are `@GET` with `@Query`. `requestUpgradeSeat` puts the original-ticket password `ogtkRetPwd` in the URL; `procUpgrade` puts payment amounts and card-input codes (`crdInpWayCd`, `mnsStlAmt`, `totTxnAmt`, …) in the URL. No encryption; TLS only.
- **`saleDt` vs `saleDd` confusion:** two distinct classes both named `SelfCheckinPossibleRequest` are imported side-by-side in `ui/ticket/confirm/TicketSelfCheckinStatusActivity.java:26-28`. `SelfCheckinInfoDao`'s version uses field **`saleDt`** (→ `checkin.info.do`, `checkin.cnc.do`) while `SelfCheckinPossibleDao`'s version uses **`saleDd`** (→ `checkin.psbFlg.do`, and `checkin.reg.do` also uses `saleDd`). Bolded in the Key-params column above.
- **Dual-mode ticket-list auth:** members send `hiduserYn="Y"` only; non-members send `hiduserYn="N"` plus `hidName`/`hidTeleNo`/`hidPwd` (name + phone + 5-digit password). `txtIndex="1"` = current tickets, `"2"` = purchase history (`ui/ticket/confirm/TicketListActivity.java:942-950`, `ui/ticket/history/TicketPurchaseHistoryActivity.java:282-289`). `tsRsStnCd` is declared on the request DTO but unset in the two observed flows.
- **`deviceReset` is dead code in v6.5.0.** `DeviceResetDao` (POST `tk.dvcInfoInit.do`) is referenced only by its own DAO file and `TicketService`, with **no UI caller** anywhere under `com/korail/talk`. It would send `teln`, `custNm`, `nonMbPwd`, and device geolocation (`latitude`/`longitude`). The related response flag `dvcInfoSmnsFlg` on `TicketListDao.TrainInfo` (`myTicket/TicketListDao.java:207`) is likewise never read.
- **`@FieldMap` endpoint:** `ticketChangeCancel` is the only `@FieldMap` request in the domain — a `HashMap<String,String>` with indexed keys built as `"lumpStlTgtNo_"+i` (`ticket/TCCancelDao.java:32-34`).
- **Repeated / typed form fields:** `plfNo` and `pbpAcepSpec` send `tkRetNo` as a repeated form field (`List<String>`); `pbpTkWdrw` repeats `pbpRsvNo` and `pnrNo`. `pbpAcepSpec.tkCnt` and `pbpTkWdrw.pbpCnt` are the only `int`-typed `@Field`s here. `PbpTkWdrwRequest.position` is a client-side list index and is **not** transmitted.
- **PII in cleartext responses (TLS only, no field encryption):** `dlvRcvCust` and `rcntDlvHst` return recipient names, phone numbers, and membership card numbers; `pbpAcepSpec` returns recipient name/phone per journey.
- **Copy/paste artifacts:** `UpdatePlatformDao`'s response getter is named `getAcepList()` but returns `tkList` (`ticket/UpdatePlatformDao.java:55-57`); `MaasServiceDetailListDao`'s model classes are misspelled `MaasServivceDetail…` in source; `GuardianReliefSmsDao`'s request fields are `public` (unusual for this codebase).
- **Field-name indirection:** several params resolve through obfuscated constants — `Price2FareDao.Price2Fare.trnNoString="trnNo"`/`.jrnySqnoString="jrnySqno"` (`trainsInfo/Price2FareDao.java:16,21`) and `b5.C1262b.ARV_RS_STN_CD="arvRsStnCd"`, `DPT_DT="dptDt"`, `DPT_TM="dptTm"` (`analysis/jadx/sources/b5/C1262b.java:16-19`). Resolved to their wire names in the table.
- **`getTripChgDate` is declared on `TicketService`** despite its `reservation.*` path prefix.
- **MaaS cancel flow:** `getMaasServiceCancelFee` (`maas.cncFee.do`) output `cncRetFee` feeds the `cncRetFee` input of `getMaasServiceCancel` (`addService.coptCnc.do`). `getMaasServiceDetailList` returns a server-supplied `rsvSpecUrl` opened in a WebView.


### 3.9 Refund

The Refund domain covers online (member) ticket return and offline (paper-ticket, no-login) refund. All five JSON APIs are declared in a single Retrofit interface, `RefundService`, and are `POST` + `@FormUrlEncoded` (`application/x-www-form-urlencoded`). Base host is resolved at runtime from `K4.g.SERVER_TYPE` (hard-set to code `"3"` => `REAL`) via `S4/z.java:46-49` `getSSLHost()`, yielding `https://smart.letskorail.com`. Every request also carries three fixed `BaseRequest` form fields — `Device="AD"`, `Version="250601003"`, `Key="korail1234567890"` (static app key; `network/BaseRequest.java:6-42`).

Auth marked "Session" means a member `JSESSIONID` cookie is required. That cookie is taken from `java.net.CookieManager` (`KTApplication.getCookie`, `application/KTApplication.java:105-116`) and reused by Retrofit's default `CookieHandler`. Auth marked "Ticket data" means no login is required; the request authenticates by paper-ticket number + passenger name (or by data echoed from a prior verify call).

#### Endpoint catalog

| Name | Method | Path | Key params | Response | Auth | Source |
|------|--------|------|-----------|----------|------|--------|
| getTicketDetail (SelTicketInfo) | POST | `/classes/com.korail.mobile.refunds.SelTicketInfo` | `Device`, `Version`, `Key`, `h_orgtk_ret_sale_dt`, `h_orgtk_wct_no`, `h_orgtk_sale_sqno`, `h_orgtk_ret_pwd`, `h_purchase_history` | `TicketDetailDao.TicketDetailResponse` (extends `BaseResponse`; ~55 fields) | Session (JSESSIONID) | `RefundService.java:23-25`; DTO `TicketDetailDao.java:176-225` (req), `:227-500` (resp) |
| getTicketCommission (CommissionView) | POST | `/classes/com.korail.mobile.refunds.CommissionView` | `Device`, `Version`, `Key`, `h_orgtk_ret_sale_dt`, `h_orgtk_wct_no`, `h_orgtk_sale_sqno`, `h_orgtk_ret_pwd`, `h_comp_nm`, `h_comp_cert_no` | `RefundCommissionDao.RefundCommissionResponse` (`ret_amt`, `ret_fee`, `prg_psb_flg`, `use_psb_mlg_num`, `tk_ret_tms_dv_cd`, `h_msg_cd2`, `h_msg_txt2`) | Session (JSESSIONID) | `RefundService.java:19-21`; DTO `RefundCommissionDao.java:11-110`; caller `ui/ticket/ticketReturn/a.java:349-358` (H0) |
| returnTicket (RefundsRequest) | POST | `/classes/com.korail.mobile.refunds.RefundsRequest` | `Device`, `Version`, `Key`, `txtPnrNo`, `h_orgtk_sale_dt`, `h_orgtk_sale_wct_no`, `h_orgtk_sale_sqno`, `h_orgtk_ret_pwd`, `h_mlg_stl`, `tk_ret_tms_dv_cd`, `trnNo`, `pbpAcepTgtFlg`, `latitude`, `longitude` | `RefundDao.RefundResponse` (`stlList: List<StlList>{stl_mns_cd}`) | Session (JSESSIONID) | `RefundService.java:27-29`; DTO `RefundDao.java:13-138`; caller `ui/ticket/ticketReturn/a.java:370-438` (J0) |
| verifyOnlineRefunds | POST | `/classes/com.korail.mobile.refunds.verifyOnlineRefunds` | `Device`, `Version`, `Key`, `retNo1`, `retNo2`, `retNo3`, `retNo4`, `strName` | `RefundVerifyTicketDao.RefundVerifyTicketResponse` (`ret_amt`, `ret_fee`, `rcvd_amt`, `poppMsg`, `orgtkinfo_list`) | Ticket data (retNo1-4 + name; no login) | `RefundService.java:31-33`; DTO `RefundVerifyTicketDao.java:11-211`; caller `s5/c.java:64-73` (A0) |
| executeOnlineRefunds | POST | `/classes/com.korail.mobile.refunds.executeOnlineRefunds` | `Device`, `Version`, `Key`, `pnrNo`, `tkKndCd`, `retDvCd`, `retRsnCd`, `ogtkSaleDt`, `ogtkSaleWctNo`, `ogtkSaleSqno`, `ogtkRetPwd`, `retAmt`, `retFee`, `custTeln`, `acepCustNm` | `RefundExecuteTicketRefundDao.RefundExecuteTicketRefundResponse` (`h_ret_dv_cd`) | Ticket data (echoed from verify; no login) | `RefundService.java:15-17`; DTO `RefundExecuteTicketRefundDao.java:11-134`; caller `s5/h.java:111-128` (y0) |
| Refund policy / commission page (WebView) | GET | `/returninfo/html/returnchginfo.html` (Limousine: `?tabId=4`) | `tabId` (query, optional; `=4` for limousine) | Static HTML (refund fee/commission policy) | None (static HTML) | `K4/g.java:104-105` (`COMMISSION_URL`, `LIMOUSINE_COMMISSION_URL`); openers `view/base/BaseActivity.java:92,105`; `X5/f.java:515`; `K6/C5956a.java:80` |

#### Notes

- **Two flows.** *Online refund (member)*: `SelTicketInfo` (ticket detail) -> `CommissionView` (fee/refundable-amount preview) -> `RefundsRequest` (executes the return, sends GPS `latitude`/`longitude` captured via a `LocationListener` in `ticketReturn/a.java`). *Offline refund (paper ticket, no login)*: `verifyOnlineRefunds` (lookup/verify) -> `executeOnlineRefunds` (execute).
- **`executeOnlineRefunds` does not re-send `retNo1-4`/name.** It authenticates via `pnrNo` + `ogtk*` fields, and echoes `retAmt`/`retFee` from the `verifyOnlineRefunds` response back into the request (`s5/h.java`). Only `verifyOnlineRefunds` takes `retNo1-4` + `strName`.
- **No signing or encryption on any refund endpoint.** Transport is HTTPS only; connect/read timeout 60000 ms (`ExecuteDao.java:23-24`). The anti-macro `x-dynapath-m-token` header is added only for 6 macro URLs (`ExecuteDao.java:27`: TicketReservation, NonMemTicket, ScheduleView, ScheduleViewSpecial, prcFare, Login) and only when `a.IS_MACRO_ACTIVE` is also true (`ExecuteDao.java:26`) — no refund endpoint is on that list. All requests are parsed by `GsonConverter` (`ExecuteDao.java:19`).
- **Response envelope.** Every response extends `BaseResponse` (`network/BaseResponse.java:6-31`): `h_msg_cd` (`hMsgCd`), `h_msg_txt` (`hMsgTxt`), `strResult` (`SUCC`/`FAIL`).
- **`returnTicket` wire-param quirk.** `RefundDao.RefundRequest` stores the WCT number in field `h_orgtk_wct_no`, but it is serialized on the wire as `@Field("h_orgtk_sale_wct_no")` (`RefundService.java:29`). The `trnNo` field is keyed via the constant `Price2FareDao.Price2Fare.trnNoString = "trnNo"` (`network/dao/trainsInfo/Price2FareDao.java:21`). Response `stl_mns_cd == "13"` triggers a RailPlus sync (`ticketReturn/a.java:533-543`).
- **Commission preview logic.** In `CommissionView` handling, `prg_psb_flg == "M"` together with `use_psb_mlg_num` vs. the commission drives a mileage-refund dialog (`ticketReturn/a.java:185-189`, repeated `:214-218`).
- **`executeOnlineRefunds` result branching.** `h_ret_dv_cd == "02"` produces a "refund complete" message; otherwise a "refund success" message (`s5/h.java:187`).
- **NetFUNNEL gate `act_22` is defined but unused.** `K4/g.java:47` declares `NETFUNNEL_ACTION_REFUND_ID = "act_22"`, but neither the constant nor the literal `"act_22"` is referenced anywhere else in the jadx sources or apktool smali of v6.5.0 — the refund NetFUNNEL gate is defined-but-unwired.
- **Refund policy/commission/belongings pages are WebView static HTML, not APIs.** `COMMISSION_URL=/returninfo/html/returnchginfo.html`, `LIMOUSINE_COMMISSION_URL=/returninfo/html/returnchginfo.html?tabId=4`, `BELONGINGS_URL=/returninfo/html/luggageinfo.html` (`K4/g.java:104-106`), served from `getWebHost()` (`https://smart.letskorail.com` for REAL, `S4/z.java:51-54`) and opened via the `WEB_POST_URL` intent extra.
- **Non-REAL hosts** (for reference): `SERVER_TYPE` variants other than REAL resolve to `dev3` / `dev2` / `mobiledev.letskorail.com` (`K4/g.java`, `S4/z.java`).
- **Client status.** These endpoints are documented but intentionally not implemented in the `korail_mobile_api` client — `refund` is listed in `EXCLUDED_API_DOMAINS` (`src/.../safety.py:11-20`) as a state-mutating domain; grepping the client for the endpoint paths returns zero hits. (The `refund_*` fields in `read_models.py:207-210` belong to an unrelated receipt read model.)


### 3.10 Delay Compensation & Ticket Return

This domain covers train-delay certificates, delay-fare refunds (cash and to original payment method), delay-PNR acceptance, and train-stoppage compensation refunds. All 12 endpoints are Retrofit (`retrofit.http`) `@POST` `@FormUrlEncoded` interfaces split across two service interfaces in `com.korail.talk.network.dao`:

- `delay.DelayService` — 9 endpoints (`DelayService.java:18-52`)
- `compensate.CompensateService` — 3 endpoints (`CompensateService.java:12-22`)

No additional endpoints exist in these three path families (`dlay.*`, `delay.*`, `compensate.*`) anywhere in the app package.

**Host.** All 12 endpoints target `https://smart.letskorail.com`, set once via `RestAdapter.setEndpoint(z.getSSLHost())` (`ExecuteDao.java:60`). `z.getSSLHost()` returns `https://smart.letskorail.com` because `SERVER_TYPE = EnumC5607a.fromCode("3") = REAL` (`K4/g.java:90-91`; `S4/z.java:46-48`; `d5/EnumC5607a.java:9`). Full URL = host + the `@POST` path shown below.

**Auth / encryption (uniform across the domain).** No per-user token or signature is added by any of these endpoints. Every request carries three static app-level form fields injected by `BaseRequest` defaults: `Device=AD`, `Version=250601003`, `Key=korail1234567890` (`BaseRequest.java:7-9,15-17`; duplicated as `COMMON_PARAMETER` at `K4/g.java:11`). User/ticket context is conveyed via plaintext business fields (`saleWctNo`/`saleDd`/`saleSqno`/`tkRetPwd`, etc.). All bodies are plaintext `application/x-www-form-urlencoded`; the only request signing in the stack is the DynaPath anti-macro header `x-dynapath-m-token`, added ONLY for 6 hardcoded macro-protected URLs (login, ticket reservation, non-member ticket, two schedule-view URLs, prcFare) at `ExecuteDao.java:27,45-47` — **none** of the delay/compensate endpoints are in that list. Responses are parsed with Gson (`GsonConverter`, `ExecuteDao.java:19`). Connect/read timeouts are 60000 ms (`ExecuteDao.java:23-24`; `K4/g.java:12,72`).

In the tables below, the three static fields (`Device`, `Version`, `Key`) are the shared auth column value and are not repeated in "Key params". Auth is "App static (Device/Version/Key)" and Source cites the `DelayService`/`CompensateService` line plus the DAO for every row.

| executeCompensateRefundList / compensate.ticketList (compensation ticket list) | POST | `/classes/com.korail.mobile.compensate.ticketList.do` | `nowPgNo: int`, `dptDtFrom`, `dptDtTo` | `CompensateRefundListResponse` (extends `RefundResponse`): `ticketList: List<TicketList>`, `whlPgNum: int` | App static | `CompensateService.java:20-22`; `CompensateRefundListDao.java:50-54` |

#### Related WebView notice (NOT a REST/JSON API)

| Name | Method | Path | Key params | Response | Auth | Source |
|------|--------|------|------------|----------|------|--------|
| DELAY_REFUND_DETAIL_INFO_URL (WebView notice page) | POST (WebView form post) | `/ebizmk/notice/detail.do` | `bbrdId=000000000033`, `OffSet=0`, `ptwtSqno=51` (static body) | HTML page (WebView content); not modeled as a DTO | None (public notice page) | `K4/g.java:14-15,102` |

Host resolves via `z.getWebHost()` (REAL) → `https://smart.letskorail.com`. Listed for completeness — it is an `ebizmk` notice/guidance page about delay-refund detail info referenced by the scout hint, not a `classes/*.do` API. The POST body is the static constant `DELAY_REFUND_DETAIL_INFO_POST_DATA = "bbrdId=000000000033&OffSet=0&ptwtSqno=51"` (`K4/g.java:14`).

#### Notes

- **Three distinct naming families, not legacy/new duplication.** `dlay.*` is the delay-certificate + cash-refund side flow (`athnIsu`, `cashRfn`, `pymtRcet`, `dptnBank`); `delay.*` is the delay-fare PNR/acceptance/list/detail/return flow; `compensate.*` is the train-stoppage compensation list/detail/return flow. Each maps to distinct backend `.do` class names.
- **compensate.* vs delay.* are separate programs, not a rename.** The compensation detail/return requests carry two EXTRA list fields — `trnStpRsStnCd` and `jrnyStpTkFlg` — that the parallel `delay.*` requests lack, and `compensate.ticketReturn` omits `dlayFarePymtMtdCd` (which `delay.ticketReturn` carries). Both families' `ticketList` endpoints share the same response base (`RefundResponse`).
- **Envelope-only responses.** `delay.acptPrs`, `delay.ticketReturn`, `delay.ticketDetail`, `compensate.ticketReturn`, and `compensate.ticketDetail` have Retrofit methods returning `BaseResponse`, so only the envelope fields (`h_msg_cd`, `h_msg_txt`, `strResult`) are modeled in the decompiled code; any richer server payload is not captured.
- **`cashRfn.dmnPrsDvCd`** constants `A`/`B`/`I` are defined at `CashRfnDao.java:12-18` (per existing UI-flow notes these map to ticket-query / account-query / refund-request in `DelayAccountRefundActivity`, `docs/deep-dive/agent-reports/10-refund-delay-compensate-cancel.md:100`).
- **`delay.ticketReturn.dlayFarePymtMtdCd`** is hardcoded to `"01"` at execution time (`G6/C5683c.java`, per `docs/deep-dive/agent-reports/10-refund-delay-compensate-cancel.md:89`).
- **PNR-accept `jobDvCd`** constants (`RESERVATION='0'`, `TICKET_CHANGE='1'`) are at `DelayPNRAcceptDao.java:11-12`.
- **`athnIsu.trnNo`** field name is referenced indirectly via `Price2FareDao.Price2Fare.trnNoString`, which resolves to `"trnNo"` (`Price2FareDao.java:21`). Field names in this domain are otherwise not obfuscated (plaintext Korean-rail column codes).
- **Shared list DTO (`RefundResponse`, `RefundResponse.java:9-162`)** — used by `delay.ticketList`, `compensate.ticketList`, and `dptnBank` — carries `ticketList: List<TicketList>` and `whlPgNum: int`. `TicketList` fields include `arvDt`, `arvRsStnNm`, `dlayFare: int`, `dptRsStnNm`, `jrnyOrdr`, `jrnyStpTkFlg`, `jrnyTpCd`, `rcVdAmt: int`, `rcvdAmt: int`, `refundAmount`, `refundInfo`, `refundJrny`, `saleDd`, `saleSqNo`, `saleWctNo`, `stlList: List<StlList>{stlMnsCd}`, `stlbTrnClsfNm`, `tkRetPwd`, `trnNo: int`, `trnRunStpCpstAmt: int`, `trnStpRsStnCd`, `trnStpRsStnCdNm`.
- **Client coverage.** Only 1 of the 12 endpoints is implemented in the read-only Python client: `get_deposit_banks()` POSTs `dlay.dptnBank.do` with `include_dynapath=False` and parses `dptnBank[]{dptnBankCd, dptnBankNm}` (`src/korail_mobile_api/client.py:383-392`; `read_parsers.py:620-639`; allowlisted at `safety.py:43,117`). The other 11 endpoints are state-mutating and deliberately not implemented. (`client.py:394-409 get_delay_discount_tickets()` targets `passCard.DelayDiscountView`, a different endpoint outside this domain's path scope.)
- **Scope note.** Two other delay-adjacent endpoints exist under a different path family and are correctly excluded here by path scope: `PassCardService.addDelayTicket` (`passCard.DelayDiscountCheck`) and `getDelayTicketList` (`passCard.DelayDiscountView`) — see `docs/api-endpoints.md:214,216`.


### 3.11 Discount Certification / Eligibility

This domain covers the endpoints that establish a passenger's eligibility for a fare
discount (disabled, national-merit, National-Assembly member, government mobile-ID /
PBEP, senior/matched targets), register and list discount coupons (N-card / delay
coupons), and recompute a reservation price once a discount is applied.

**Transport & host.** Every endpoint is a Retrofit v1 service method (`retrofit.http.*`)
on a single production host resolved at runtime by `getSSLHost()` →
`https://smart.letskorail.com` (env-switchable to `dev3/dev2/mobiledev.letskorail.com`),
`S4/z.java:46-48`, wired into the adapter at `ExecuteDao.java:59-61`. Transport is
HTTPS/TLS; responses are Gson-deserialized (`GsonConverter`, `ExecuteDao.java:19`).

**App-identity params.** Nearly every request carries three static constants — `Device`
= `AD`, `Version` = `250601003`, `Key` (APP_KEY) = `korail1234567890` — defined in
`BaseRequest.java:7-9,14-18`. GET methods pass them as `@Query`, POST methods as
`@Field` (form-url-encoded). Exception: the two PBEP GET calls send only `Device` +
`Version` (no `Key`).

**Auth model.** Auth is session-cookie based, not header/token based. A global
`java.net.CookieManager` is installed as the default `CookieHandler`
(`KTApplication.java:73-75`), so the `JSESSIONID` cookie established at login
(`KTApplication.java:110`) is attached automatically to every certification/passCard/cust
call. `Key` is a fixed shared app key, not a user credential. There is no per-request
bearer/signature header for the pure lookups.

**Anti-macro signing (DynaPath).** Only for a fixed allowlist of endpoints is a DynaPath
token header `x-dynapath-m-token` attached via `DynaPathMobileSDK.Companion.generate()`
when `a.IS_MACRO_ACTIVE` (`ExecuteDao.java:26-52`). In this domain the allowlist covers
**only** `certification.TicketReservation` and `nonMember.NonMemTicket`
(`ExecuteDao.java:27`). The pure discount-cert / eligibility lookups (`disabled.do`,
`assemblyCert`, `MeritCert`, `PriceReCalculation`, `pbep.*`, `mchdDcntTgt.do`,
`passCard.*`) are **not** signed.

**Response envelope.** All response models extend `BaseResponse`, carrying `h_msg_cd`,
`h_msg_txt` (`@SerializedName`) and `strResult` (success sentinel `SUCC`, failure `FAIL`),
`BaseResponse.java:6-31`. (In the decompiled artifact the annotation is R8-shaded to
`@S3.c(...)`, semantically identical to Gson's `@SerializedName`.)

#### Notes

- **Shared path, two methods.** `/classes/com.korail.mobile.certification.ReservationList`
  is bound to **two** distinct Retrofit GET methods on the same interface:
  `applyDisabilityCertification` (full `@QueryMap` set, `CertificationService.java:22-23`)
  and `inquiryTicketRsv` (minimal `hidPnrNo` only, `:45-46`). This path reuse is
  intentional; params/response differ.

- **Shared endpoint, two DAOs.** `passCard.DiscountCheck` is used by both
  `DCCouponCertDao` and `DCEmployeeCouponCertDao` (employee coupon). The latter
  subclasses the former and overrides only the local resource id — it hits the **same**
  URL with the **same** fields (`DCEmployeeCouponCertDao.java:6-10`).

- **PBEP = government mobile-ID (모바일 신분증) flow.** A verifiable-presentation
  eligibility flow split into two GET steps. Step1 `toknCre.do` returns
  `app` (JSON with `sp_did`, `service_code`, `callback_url`, `nonce`, `encrypt_type`,
  `sessionId`) + `csrfToken`; the app then launches the external government app via intent
  `kr.go.id.bmc.VERIFY_VP` / URI `bmc://verify_vp?...`
  (`GovernmentCertificationActivity.java:141-164`). Step2 `sttChck.do` (carrying
  `csrfToken`) returns `pbepInfo`, which is fed back as the `@Field("pbepInfo")` on
  `certification.TicketReservation` (`GovernmentCertificationActivity.java:131-133`;
  `CertificationService.java:54`). Note: `GovernmentCertificationStep1Request` still
  declares unused `regNum`/`hdcpGrade` fields — only `Device`/`Version` are transmitted.

- **Plaintext PII.** `disabledCertification` sends `regNum` (resident-registration number,
  populated from the `RESIDENT_REGISTRATION_NUMBER` bundle key,
  `DisabilityCertificationActivity.java:216`) and `hdcpGrade` as plaintext `@Query` — no
  field-level encryption, HTTPS only. `certMerit` likewise sends `txtJuminNo7` (7-digit
  resident-reg) as a plaintext `@Field`.

- **Obfuscated field name.** The `mchdDcntTgt.do` field `dptDt` is referenced in the
  service interface via the obfuscated constant `b5.C1262b.DPT_DT` (= `"dptDt"`,
  `b5/C1262b.java:17`); the DAO request property is also `dptDt` (`MchdDcntTgtDao.java:67`).

- **TicketReservation / NonMemTicket scope.** Both are primarily reservation/ticketing
  endpoints but are listed here because they live in `CertificationService` and consume
  this domain's eligibility outputs — the `pbepInfo` token (`@Field`, TicketReservation)
  and the `hidFreeFlg` free/discount flag. They are the only two endpoints in this domain
  that are DynaPath-signed (`ExecuteDao.java:27`).

- **Paging.** `getDiscountCoupon` supports paging via `h_page_no` / `h_tot_page_cnt`
  (`isMore()` at `DCCouponListDao.java:155-159`); `txtSelPage` selects the page.

- **`ReservationResponse` (shared model)** backs `getDiscountPrice`, `inquiryTicketRsv`,
  `reservation` (TicketReservation), and `reservation` (NonMemTicket). It carries the full
  fare/passenger/seat breakdown — top-level totals (`h_pnr_no`, `h_tot_fare`, `h_tot_prc`,
  `h_tot_dcnt_amt`, …), and collections `psg_infos`, `psgDiscAdd_infos`, `jrny_infos`
  (with nested `seat_infos`), `tkList`, `dfpyList`, `stopStnList`
  (`ReservationResponse.java:8-575`).

- **Client coverage.** The read-only subset is implemented in `src/korail_mobile_api` and
  matches this analysis exactly: `mchdDcntTgt.do` (called with `include_dynapath=False`,
  corroborating the "not signed" finding), `DelayDiscountView`, and `CouponView`. The
  cert/write endpoints (`disabled.do`, `assemblyCert`, `MeritCert`, `PriceReCalculation`,
  `pbep.*`, `DiscountCheck`, `DelayDiscountCheck`, `certification.ReservationList`) are not
  implemented (the client is read-focused). `TicketReservation`/`NonMemTicket` appear in
  the client only as the DynaPath macro allowlist (`constants.py:37-38`), not as callable
  endpoints.


### 3.12 Pass (정기권/자유여행패스)

This domain covers Korail rail passes / commuter passes (정기권), the 자유여행패스 ("OTR" free-travel pass) variants, and the adjacent **PassCard** discount-card / coupon surface (할인쿠폰·지연할인권). It is defined by exactly two legacy-Retrofit (square retrofit v1) service interfaces:

- `PassService` — 8 endpoints (`.../network/dao/pass/PassService.java:18-50`)
- `PassCardService` — 4 endpoints (`.../network/dao/passCard/PassCardService.java:11-27`)

All 12 endpoint path strings found in the sources match these 12 declarations exactly — no other pass/passCard endpoints exist.

#### Transport & common conventions (apply to every row below)

- **Host:** all endpoints are `POST` + `@FormUrlEncoded` to the production host `https://smart.letskorail.com` (`SERVER_TYPE.REAL`), resolved at runtime via `z.getSSLHost()` (`S4/z.java:46-49`) and applied as the Retrofit endpoint in `ExecuteDao.java:60`. Non-prod hosts are `dev3` / `dev2` / `mobiledev.letskorail.com`.
- **Common fields:** every request carries 3 fields from `BaseRequest` — `Device='AD'`, `Version='250601003'`, `Key='korail1234567890'` (`BaseRequest.java:6-42`). **Exception:** `tripMenu` sends only `Device` + `Version`, NOT `Key` (`PassService.java:47-49`).
- **Response base:** every response extends `BaseResponse` (`BaseResponse.java:7-31`), supplying `strResult` (`SUCC`/`FAIL`), `h_msg_cd`, `h_msg_txt` via Gson aliases.
- **Encryption/signing:** none. Bodies are plaintext `application/x-www-form-urlencoded`. No per-request encryption or signature is applied to any pass/passCard endpoint.
- **`x-dynapath-m-token`:** the DynaPath anti-macro token (`ExecuteDao.java:26-53`) is added only when the `IS_MACRO_ACTIVE` runtime flag is set AND the URL is one of 6 hardcoded macro-sensitive URLs (TicketReservation, NonMemTicket, ScheduleView, ScheduleViewSpecial, `trn.prcFare.do`, `login.Login`). No pass/passCard endpoint is in that list, so the token is never applied here (independently corroborated by the Python client using `include_dynapath=False` on every pass endpoint).
- **Auth model:** session/cookie based — the member-login session cookie is set globally on `z.getSSLHost()` via `CookieManager` (`KTApplication.java:130`). The constant `Key` field is a static app key, not per-user auth. In the tables below, "Yes (session)" means the endpoint functionally requires an authenticated member-login session (reserve/pay/coupon); "Partial" means only the static `Key` is required for a session-scoped query; "No" means a public menu.

#### PassService — 정기권 / 자유여행패스 (8 endpoints)

| Name | Method | Path | Key params | Response | Auth | Source |
|------|--------|------|------------|----------|------|--------|

| Name | Method | Path | Key params | Response | Auth | Source |

| `certDCCoupon` (할인쿠폰 인증) | POST | `/classes/com.korail.mobile.passCard.DiscountCheck` | `Device`, `Version`, `Key`, `txtCertNo`, `txtCertPwd` | `BaseResponse` (no dedicated DTO) | Yes (session) | `PassCardService.java:16-18`; `DCCouponCertDao.java:48`; req model `DCCouponCertDao.java:11-42` |
| `getDelayTicketList` (지연할인권 목록) | POST | `/classes/com.korail.mobile.passCard.DelayDiscountView` | `Device`, `Version`, `Key`, `dptDtTo` | `DelayTicketListDao.DelayTicketListResponse` → `disc_infos.disc_info[]DelayCoupon{h_dlay_fare,h_orgtk_ret_pwd,h_orgtk_ret_sale_dt,h_orgtk_sale_sqno,h_orgtk_wct_no,h_use_psb_dt}` + `BaseResponse` | Yes (session) | `PassCardService.java:20-22`; `DelayTicketListDao.java:114`; models `DelayTicketListDao.java:12-108` |
| `getDiscountCoupon` (할인쿠폰 목록) | POST | `/classes/com.korail.mobile.passCard.CouponView` | `Device`, `Version`, `Key`, `txtSelPage`, `pnrNo` (wire name; DTO field is `pnr`) | `DCCouponListDao.DCCouponListResponse` → `coupon_infos.coupon_info[]DiscountCoupon{...}`, `h_page_no`, `h_tot_page_cnt` + `BaseResponse` | Yes (session) | `PassCardService.java:24-26`; `DCCouponListDao.java:147`; models `DCCouponListDao.java:13-159` |

#### Notes

- **Two payment endpoints send two `@FieldMap` maps.** `commPayment` (passPayIssue) and `passPayment` (passOtrPayIssue) each flatten a free-form payment map plus a `PaymentMethod` map into the form body. `PaymentMethod` (`PaymentMethod.java:6`) **extends `LinkedHashMap<String,String>` only** — it does not implement `IPaymentRequest`. It carries ~30 `hidXxx` card/point/easy-pay keys (`hidStlMnsSqno{n}`, `hidStlMnsCd{n}`, `hidMnsStlAmt{n}`, `hidStlCrCrdNo{n}`, `hidVanPwd{n}`, `hidIsmtMnthNum{n}`, `hidPontDvCd{n}`, `spayDvCd_1_1`, `hidDscpMgNo{n}`, …; `PaymentMethod.java:6-139`). It is the request DTOs `CommPaymentRequest` (`CommPaymentDao.java:28-31`) and `PassPaymentRequest` (`PassPaymentDao.java:37-40`) that implement `IPaymentRequest`, exposing `getEasyPaymentMethod()` = `paymentMethod.get("spayDvCd_1_1")`.
- **`tripMenu` is the only endpoint that omits `Key`.** Its signature is `tripMenu(@Field Device, @Field Version)` only (`PassService.java:47-49`) — corroborated by the client whitelist allowing only `{Device, Version}` for this path.
- **`DCEmployeeCouponCertDao` reuses `certDCCoupon`.** It extends `DCCouponCertDao` and calls the exact same endpoint/request (`DiscountCheck`, `DCCouponCertRequest`); only its dao id differs (`DCEmployeeCouponCertDao.java:6-11`). There is no separate employee endpoint.
- **`getCommRsvInquiry` defines `txtCmtrUtlTrmNm` but does not send it.** The request DTO has a getter for `txtCmtrUtlTrmNm`, but `executeDao` omits it from the wire (only 15 fields sent; `CommRsvInquiryDao.java:208`).
- **`getDiscountCoupon` renames on the wire.** The DTO field/getter is `pnr` but it is sent as `@Field("pnrNo")`. This endpoint paginates via `h_page_no` / `h_tot_page_cnt` (`isMore()`, `DCCouponListDao.java:155-159`).
- **`getEnableDate` naming vs. path.** The Retrofit method is `getEnableDate` but the path is `passInfoList`; it returns available open-dates plus WCT info.
- **`commPayment` dead code.** `CommPaymentDao` declares an unused inner `MainInfo` class (`CommPaymentDao.java:65-74`); the actual `CommPaymentResponse.main_info` references `PassPaymentDao.MainInfo` (`CommPaymentDao.java:55`).
- **Client coverage.** The Python client (`src/korail_mobile_api`) implements 6 of 12 — the read/menu endpoints (`passInfoList`, `passScheduleInfoList`, `passMenu.do`, `trGdMenuLt.do`, `DelayDiscountView`, `CouponView`). The 6 write endpoints (both pay-issue, both reserve, `DelayDiscountCheck`, `DiscountCheck`) are not implemented. No path/param conflict — just unimplemented.
- **Obfuscation.** DAO ids use obfuscated refs (`G4.f.dao_*`) and the host resolver is obfuscated (`S4.z`), but all endpoint paths, params, and model field names are plaintext.


### 3.13 Mileage / Points / Loyalty

This domain covers KORAIL membership mileage (accrual notice / save / detail for companions),
X-Point views + OK Cashbag certification, the general mileage amount spec plus L.Point (lpot)
authentication, and the RailPlus prepaid-card auto-charge check. There are **exactly 9 endpoints**
spread across 3 Square-Retrofit-v1 service interfaces: `MileageService` (3 POST),
`XPointService` (5 POST) and `RailPlusService` (1 GET). A package-wide grep for the
`com.korail.mobile.(mileage|mlg|xPoint|railplus)` literals returned precisely these 9 paths — no
hidden or additional endpoints.

All paths below are relative to the runtime-selected host. In production (REAL) that host is
`https://smart.letskorail.com`, resolved by `S4/z.java:46-49` `getSSLHost()` from
`K4.g.SERVER_TYPE` (REAL=`smart.letskorail.com`, TEST=`dev3.letskorail.com`,
STAGING=`dev2.letskorail.com`, else `mobiledev.letskorail.com`) and applied at
`ExecuteDao.java:60`.

#### Endpoint catalog

| Name | Method | Path | Key params | Response | Auth | Source |
|------|--------|------|------------|----------|------|--------|
| acpnMlgNoti (companion/accrual mileage notice) | POST | `/classes/com.korail.mobile.mileage.acpnMlgNoti.do` | `saleWctNo`, `saleDt`, `saleSqno`, `retPwd`, `rcvPsHndyTeln` (+ `Device`/`Version`/`Key`) | `BaseResponse` (`h_msg_cd`, `h_msg_txt`, `strResult`) — no typed DTO | Session cookie + app id fields | `MileageService.java:11-13`; DAO `AcpnMlgNotiDao.java:11-67` |
| acpnMlgSave (save companion/accrual mileage) | POST | `/classes/com.korail.mobile.mileage.acpnMlgSave.do` | `rsvMbCrdNo`, `custNm`, `mlgAcmMbCrdNo`, `saleWctNo`, `saleDd`, `saleSqno`, `tkRetPwd` | `BaseResponse` — no typed DTO | Session cookie + app id fields | `MileageService.java:15-17`; DAO `AcpnMlgSaveDao.java:11-91`; caller `AccumulatingKTXMileageActivity.java:153-167` |
| acpnMlgSpec (companion mileage detail/spec by PNR) | POST | `/classes/com.korail.mobile.mileage.acpnMlgSpec.do` | `pnrNo` | `AcpnMlgSpecResponse` (`tkList:List<Ticket>`) | Session cookie + app id fields | `MileageService.java:19-21`; DAO `AcpnMlgSpecDao.java:11-168`; caller `CompanionMileageDetailActivity.java:184-188` |
| certifyOKCashbag (OK Cashbag card certification) | POST | `/classes/com.korail.mobile.xPoint.OkCashbagCertView` | `cp_no` (Java `cpNo`) | `BaseResponse` — no typed DTO | Session cookie + app id fields | `XPointService.java:14-16`; DAO `OKCashbagCertDao.java:9-37` |
| getKorailPoint (My X-Point / KORAIL point view) | POST | `/classes/com.korail.mobile.xPoint.MyXPointView` | `point_dv_cd` (hardcoded `'0'`) | `KorailPointInquiryResponse` (14 `h_*` String fields, incl. `h_korail_point`) | Session cookie + app id fields | `XPointService.java:18-20`; DAO `KorailPointInquiryDao.java:9-98` |
| getLPoint (L.Point / lpot authentication) | POST | `/classes/com.korail.mobile.mlg.lpotAthn.do` | `pontPwd` (only field sent per signature; request DTO also holds `custRcgnNoVal`, `jobDvCd`) | `LPointInquiryResponse` (`custRcgnNoVal`, `extrPontAmt`, `prsCnqeVal`, `pwdErrTno`) | Session cookie + app id fields; `pontPwd` user-supplied | `XPointService.java:22-24`; DAO `LPointDao.java:9-81` |
| getMileage (general mileage amount spec / history) | POST | `/classes/com.korail.mobile.mlg.amtSpec.do` | `pontTpVal`, `qryDvVal`, `qryStDt`, `qryClsDt`, `pgPrCnt`, `nowPgNo` | `MileageInquiryResponse` (totals + `specList:List<SpecList>`) | Session cookie + app id fields | `XPointService.java:26-28`; DAO `MileageInquiryDao.java:10-180` |

| getAutoCharge (RailPlus prepaid card auto-charge check) | GET | `/classes/com.korail.mobile.railplus.autoCharge.do` | `jobDvCd`, `prepCrdNo` (+ `Device`/`Version`/`Key` as **@Query**) | `AutoChargeResponse` (`psbFlg:String`) | Session cookie + app id query params | `RailPlusService.java:9-10`; DAO `AutoChargeDao.java:9-56`; caller `RailPlusActivity.java:92-97` |

All source paths above are under
`analysis/jadx/sources/com/korail/talk/network/dao/`
(sub-packages `mileage/`, `xPoint/`, `railplus/`).

#### Notes

- **Transport / identity.** The stack is Square Retrofit v1 (`retrofit.http.*` + `RestAdapter`)
  with `GsonConverter` over a `UrlConnectionClient` (`java.net.HttpURLConnection`), **not**
  retrofit2/OkHttp (`ExecuteDao.java:11-65`, connect/read timeouts 60000ms at
  `ExecuteDao.java:23-24`). Every request carries three app-level identity params from
  `BaseRequest.java:7-18` — `Device='AD'` (ANDROID), `Version='250601003'` (build constant, distinct
  from the marketing version 6.5.0), `Key='korail1234567890'` (hardcoded APP_KEY). For the 8 POST
  endpoints these are `@Field` form params; for the RailPlus GET they are `@Query` params.
- **Auth is session-cookie based.** A global `java.net.CookieManager` with
  `CookiePolicy.ACCEPT_ALL` is installed as the default `CookieHandler` in
  `KTApplication.java:73-75`, so the login session cookie is sent automatically on every request.
  There is **no** per-request `Authorization` header or bearer token. These are member/loyalty
  endpoints requiring a logged-in session (e.g. `AccumulatingKTXMileageActivity` first calls
  `MemberCertDao` before `acpnMlgSave`).
- **No body encryption or signing.** Transport is HTTPS/TLS only (`getSSLHost` returns an `https://`
  origin). The anti-macro DynaPath `x-dynapath-m-token` header (`ExecuteDao.java:44-47`) is added
  **only** for a hardcoded macro-URL allowlist (login/reservation/schedule/fare,
  `ExecuteDao.java:27`); **none** of the 9 endpoints in this domain are in that list, so no DynaPath
  token is sent here.
- **Wire vs Java field names.** Reported params use the actual `@Field`/`@Query` annotation strings,
  which differ from the Java DTO field names: `getPoint` sends `inp_dv_cd`/`point_dv_cd`/`xpoint_no`/
  `xpoint_pwd`/`stl_crd_valid_trm`; `certifyOKCashbag` sends `cp_no` for `cpNo`;
  `getKorailPoint`/`getPoint` send `point_dv_cd`.
- **Response shapes.** All response DTOs extend `BaseResponse` (`BaseResponse.java:7-31`), which via
  Gson `@c` aliases carries `h_msg_cd`, `h_msg_txt`, `strResult` (constants `SUCCESS='SUCC'`,
  `FAIL='FAIL'`). Three endpoints return a **bare** `BaseResponse` with no typed DTO
  (`acpnMlgNoti`, `acpnMlgSave`, `certifyOKCashbag`). `getKorailPoint` builds a plain `BaseRequest`
  (no dedicated request DTO) and hardcodes `point_dv_cd='0'` (`KorailPointInquiryDao.java:91`).
  `PointInquiryResponse` fields are `int`; every other typed response DTO in this domain uses
  `String`.
- **`.do` suffix quirk.** The three xPoint *View* endpoints (`MyXPointView`, `XPointView`,
  `OkCashbagCertView`) carry **no** `.do` suffix, whereas `acpnMlg*`, `lpotAthn`, `amtSpec`, and
  `autoCharge` all end in `.do`.
- **Companion-mileage UIs** reuse `acpnMlgSpec`/`acpnMlgSave` — they add no extra endpoint.
- **Client parity.** None of these 9 endpoints are implemented in the Python client under
  `src/` (only incidental string fields such as
  `railplus_cardinfo`, an `app.menu.railpoint` menu id, and a `points-mileage` category label
  exist — none call these APIs). This domain is greenfield reference material on the client side.


### 3.14 Gift / Gift Ticket

The **Gift / Gift Ticket** domain covers two distinct capabilities that share a security profile: (1) gifting a purchased ticket to another member/person (`GiftSend`, in `GiftInfoService`), and (2) the gift-goods (선물권) lifecycle — reserve, list, usage spec/history, and return (`gdRsv`/`gdLst`/`gdUseSpec`/`gdRet`, in `GifticketService`). The domain has **exactly 5 endpoints**, confirmed complete by grepping all `com.korail.mobile.gift*` strings across both jadx and apktool smali (same 5 strings, no others).

All 5 endpoints are **HTTP POST** with `Content-Type: application/x-www-form-urlencoded` (retrofit1 `@POST` + `@FormUrlEncoded`). All require an authenticated **member login session**; there is no per-request bearer/token/signature header at the retrofit interface level (see security note below).

#### Endpoint catalog

| Name | Method | Path | Key params | Response | Auth | Source |
|------|--------|------|------------|----------|------|--------|
| presentTicket (gift ticket send to a member — GiftSend) | POST | `/classes/com.korail.mobile.giftInfo.GiftSend` | `hidAcepPsNm`, `hidAcepPsTeln`, `hidPbpAcepPsMbFlg`, `hidPbpAcepPsCustMgNo`, `hidPnrNo`, `hidTotNewStlAmt`, `hidRsvChgNo`, `hidInfoInpDvCd` (O=default/K=kakao/S=sms), `hidSaleCnt`, `hidAcepPwd` (plaintext), `@FieldMap ticketPresentParams` (indexed `hidSaleWctNo`/`hidSaleDt`/`hidSaleSqno`/`hidtkRetPwd`) | `TicketPresentResponse`: `chgePbpRsvNo` + `strResult`/`h_msg_cd`/`h_msg_txt` | Yes — member session cookie + static `Key` | `GiftInfoService.java:12-14`; `giftInfo/TicketPresentDao.java:59-176` |
| bookingGifticket (gift-goods reserve — gdRsv) | POST | `/classes/com.korail.mobile.gift.gdRsv.do` | `itmCnt`, `mrkAmt` (wire `mrkAmt_1`), `prnbCnt`, `mbCrdNo` (wire `mbCrdNo_1`), `gdUtlPsNm` (wire `gdUtlPsNm_1`) | `GifticketBookingResponse`: `lumpStlTgtNo`, `prsCnqeVal`, `rcvdAmt` + base | Yes — member session cookie + static `Key` | `gifticket/GifticketService.java:13-15`; `gifticket/GifticketBookingDao.java:11-88` |
| getGifticketList (gift-goods list — gdLst) | POST | `/classes/com.korail.mobile.gift.gdLst.do` | `qryDvCd`, `qryVal`, `abrdDtFrom`, `abrdDtTo`, `usePsbFlg`, `qryNumNext`, `fllwQryFlg`, `trnOprBzDvCd` | `GifticketListResponse`: `gdList: List<GifticketInfo>`, `qryCnt`, `qryNumNext` + base | Yes — member session cookie + static `Key` | `gifticket/GifticketService.java:17-19`; `gifticket/GifticketListDao.java:11-175` |
| historyGifticket (gift-goods usage spec/history — gdUseSpec) | POST | `/classes/com.korail.mobile.gift.gdUseSpec.do` | `tkId` (only `Device`/`Version`/`Key`/`tkId` sent) | `GifticketHistoryResponse`: `txnList: List<GifticketDetailData>`, `qryCnt`, `fllwQryFlg` + base | Yes — member session cookie + static `Key` | `gifticket/GifticketService.java:21-23`; `gifticket/GifticketHistoryDao.java:11-92` |
| returnGifticket (gift-goods return — gdRet) | POST | `/classes/com.korail.mobile.gift.gdRet.do` | `tkId` | `GifticketReturnResponse`: `prsFlg` + base | Yes — member session cookie + static `Key` | `gifticket/GifticketService.java:25-27`; `gifticket/GifticketReturnDao.java:11-47` |

> Path prefix note: `Path` is the retrofit `@POST` suffix. The full URL is `host + path`, where `host` is resolved at runtime by `getSSLHost()` (`S4/z.java:46-49`) from `SERVER_TYPE`: `REAL`(production)=`https://smart.letskorail.com`, `TEST`=`https://dev3.letskorail.com`, `STAGING`=`https://dev2.letskorail.com`, else(dev)=`https://mobiledev.letskorail.com`. Wired via `ExecuteDao.getRestAdapterBuilder().setEndpoint(z.getSSLHost())` (`ExecuteDao.java:59-60`). Source paths are under `analysis/jadx/sources/com/korail/talk/network/dao/`.

#### Common request/response envelope

Every gift request DTO extends **`BaseRequest`** (`BaseRequest.java:7-18`), which auto-includes 3 constant fields on the wire:

- `Device` = `AD` (ANDROID)
- `Version` = `250601003`
- `Key` = `korail1234567890` — a **static app key**, NOT a user token.

Every gift response DTO extends **`BaseResponse`** (`BaseResponse.java:7-19`): `strResult` (`SUCC`/`FAIL`), `h_msg_cd` (`hMsgCd`), `h_msg_txt` (`hMsgTxt`). (Obfuscation nuance: the Gson annotation is the renamed `@S3.c("...")`, semantically `@SerializedName`.)

#### Auth & security profile (applies to all 5 endpoints)

- **Auth is session-cookie based.** A process-wide `java.net.CookieManager` with `CookiePolicy.ACCEPT_ALL` is installed in `KTApplication.java:73-75`, so the login session cookie (e.g. `JSESSIONID`) is attached automatically by the `UrlConnectionClient`. There is **no** per-request bearer/token/signature header on gift endpoints. The static app `Key` is sent but is not a per-user credential.
- **No encryption or signing** is applied to any gift endpoint (TLS only). A grep for `encrypt`/`cipher`/`AES`/`RSA`/`SHA`/`base64`/`hash`/`sign` in both gift DAO packages found nothing. The accept password `hidAcepPwd` is sent **plaintext** (set from a raw string in `DeliveryActivity.java:205`, inside the non-member delivery path `executeDeliveryNonMember(...)`).
- **No DynaPath anti-macro token.** The `x-dynapath-m-token` header (`ExecuteDao.java:19-55`) is added only when `IS_MACRO_ACTIVE` is on **and** the URL is on a hardcoded 6-URL whitelist (`TicketReservation`, `NonMemTicket`, `ScheduleView`, `ScheduleViewSpecial`, `trn.prcFare.do`, `login.Login`). None of the 5 gift endpoints are on that list, so gift requests carry no DynaPath token.

#### Notable details

- **`presentTicket` uses a `@FieldMap` of variable/indexed keys** (`TicketPresentParams`, a `LinkedHashMap`, `TicketPresentDao.java:17-57`) in addition to its fixed `@Field` params. It holds `hidSaleWctNo`, `hidSaleDt`, `hidSaleSqno`, `hidtkRetPwd` per sold ticket — the **first entry has no numeric suffix**, and subsequent entries are suffixed `1`, `2`, `3`, …. `hidInfoInpDvCd` picks the delivery channel: `O`=DEFAULT, `K`=KAKAO, `S`=SMS (`TicketPresentDao.java:13-15`).
- **Wire field names differ from DTO field names for `gdRsv`.** The retrofit `@Field` names carry a `_1` suffix — `mrkAmt_1`, `mbCrdNo_1`, `gdUtlPsNm_1` — although the DTO fields are `mrkAmt`/`mbCrdNo`/`gdUtlPsNm` (`GifticketService.java:15` vs `GifticketBookingDao.java:12-16`).
- **`gdUseSpec` has a `qryNum` field that is never sent.** The request DTO exposes `setQryNum`, but `executeDao` only passes `Device`/`Version`/`Key`/`tkId` on the wire (`GifticketHistoryDao.java:82-87`).
- **Decompiled-source oddity in `gdRet`.** `GifticketReturnDao.getId()` returns the same resource id `f.dao_gifticket_reservation` as `GifticketBookingDao` (`GifticketBookingDao.java:92`, `GifticketReturnDao.java:46`) — a likely copy/paste in the decompiled/original code. The existing repo doc (`agent-reports/12-...:383`) notes the caller (`B6/b.java:295-302`) expects `dao_gifticket_return`, framing it as a genuine source mismatch. The endpoint path/model facts are unaffected either way. (Obfuscation note: DAO ids come from obfuscated resource holder `G4.f`; helper/host classes are single-letter obfuscated, e.g. `S4.z`, `K4.g`.)
- **Client implementation status.** Only `gdLst` is wired as a callable read op in the Python client (`src/.../client.py:732` `get_gift_ticket_list()` → POST `/classes/com.korail.mobile.gift.gdLst.do`, parsed by `parse_gift_ticket_list_response`). Its safety allow-list (`safety.py:369`) sends a conservative subset `{Device,Version,Key,qryDvCd,qryVal,abrdDtFrom,abrdDtTo,usePsbFlg}`, omitting `qryNumNext`/`fllwQryFlg`/`trnOprBzDvCd` — client narrowing, not a contract mismatch. The other 4 gift endpoints are not implemented (read-only/safety-gated client; `read_payloads.py:637` explicitly raises "gift-ticket history mode is not supported").


### 3.15 MaaS / Add-on Services / Cart

This domain covers the multimodal / add-on ("MaaS") product surface of `com.korail.talk` v6.5.0: add-service reserve/buy/cancel, the mobility-help ("help service") flows, the shopping cart, MaaS reservation status/cancel/fee, the coupon-goods (`copt`) menu and query endpoints, the MaaS station list, and the limousine-bus (`lmu` / `lms`) schedule and seat-inventory lookups.

**Transport & common envelope (applies to every endpoint below).** All 16 endpoints are `POST` + `@FormUrlEncoded` (plaintext `application/x-www-form-urlencoded` over TLS); none carry `@Header` annotations. The networking stack is Retrofit v1 (`retrofit.RestAdapter` + `GsonConverter` + `UrlConnectionClient` over `java.net.HttpURLConnection`), connect/read timeouts 60 s (`ExecuteDao.java:23-24`, class body `ExecuteDao.java:17-66`). The base host is resolved at runtime by `S4/z.getSSLHost()` (`ExecuteDao.java:60`): production (`SERVER_TYPE=PRODUCTION`) is `https://smart.letskorail.com`; dev fallbacks are `https://dev3.letskorail.com`, `https://dev2.letskorail.com`, `https://mobiledev.letskorail.com` (`S4/z.java:46-49`). One endpoint (`getMaasStationList`) uses the `/ebizmaas/` path prefix on the same host; all others use `/classes/`. There is **no request signing / anti-macro token** in this domain — the `x-dynapath-m-token` header's hardcoded URL whitelist (`ExecuteDao.java:27-53`) includes none of these paths — and **no per-request body encryption** (the separate `common.encrypt.do`/`decrypt.do` field-crypto service is never invoked here). Every response wraps the `BaseResponse` envelope `{ strResult ("SUCC"/"FAIL"), h_msg_cd, h_msg_txt }` (`BaseResponse.java:7-31`).

**Static request fields vs. real auth.** By default `BaseRequest` injects three static fields on the wire: `Device="AD"` (Android), `Version="250601003"`, `Key="korail1234567890"` (`APP_KEY`) — `BaseRequest.java:6-42`. `Key` is a hardcoded **app** key, not a per-user token; real user authentication for endpoints carrying personal identifiers (`pnrNo`, `saleWctNo`, `custMgNo`, `lumpStlTgtNo`, …) is via the login **session cookie**. Note that several MaaS endpoints deliberately **omit** `Key` on the wire — `cancelPay.do`, `coptCnc.do`, `gdReqQry.do`, `gdMenuLt.do` send only `Device`+`Version` (+ business params) — and `EbizMaasStationList.do` sends **only** `addSrvDvCd` (no `Device`/`Version`/`Key` at all).
