**Notes**

- **CONFIRMED path bug (double slash):** `getMaasCancel` is declared `@POST("/classes//com.korail.mobile.addService.cancelPay.do")` — a literal double slash after `/classes` (`TicketService.java:38`). Server-side normalization is unverified; this is independently flagged in `docs/deep-dive/agent-reports/20-doc-quality-gap-audit.md:116` and catalogued at `docs/deep-dive/webview-and-url-catalog.md:144`.
- **`cancelPay.do` has two DAO paths.** It is reachable via `getMaasCancel` (`custMgNo`/`lumpStlTgtNo`) and wired through `MaasCancelDao` (`MaasCancelDao.java:9-46`). `coptCnc.do` is driven by `MaasServiceCancelDao`.
- **Copy-paste artifact in `reservationList`.** `BusReservationListDao.executeDao()` calls `busReservationService.reservationList(...)` **twice** with identical args, discarding the first result (`BusReservationListDao.java:331-332`).
- **`addCart` / `getCartList` paths have no trailing `.do`** (`cart.addCartList`, `cart.showCartList`). `AddProductDao` reuses the exact same request/endpoint as `AddCartDao` — only the DAO id differs (`dao_add_product` vs `dao_add_cart`) — so `addProduct` is not a distinct wire endpoint (`AddProductDao.java:6-11`).
- **Field-name obfuscation / wire-vs-DTO mismatches.** Several `@Field` names come from constant classes: `jrnySqno=Price2Fare.jrnySqnoString`, `trnNo=Price2Fare.trnNoString`, `dptDt=C1262b.DPT_DT`, `arvRsStnCd=C1262b.ARV_RS_STN_CD`, `dptTm=C1262b.DPT_TM`. Also: `additionalService` sends `@Field("jobDvCd")` but the DTO getter is `getJobDbCd()`; `reservationList` sends `@Field("tmGpCd")` fed from `getTrnGpCd()`.
- **Bare-envelope endpoints (no typed body):** `dealCarBuy`, `addCart`, `verifyMaasStatus`, `getMaasCancel`, `getMaasServiceCancel` return only the `BaseResponse` envelope.
- **`getMaasMenuList` response also returns navigation URLs** for arrival/departure (parking lot, elevator, BIS info, baggage-transfer rebate, lead-navi). `getMaasStationList` is the **only** endpoint on the `/ebizmaas/` path and shares `StationDataResponse` with `getStationData`.
- **Client parity (read-only posture).** The Python client (`src/korail_mobile_api/`) implements the read subset and matches exactly: `get_cart_list`→`showCartList`, `get_maas_service_details`→`copt.gdReqQry.do` (Device+Version only), `get_maas_menu_list`→`copt.gdMenuLt.do` (no Key), `get_maas_station_data`→`EbizMaasStationList.do` (`addSrvDvCd` only), `get_limousine_schedules`→`lmu.scdlQry.do` (wire name `tmGpCd`), `get_limousine_seat_inventory`→`lms.TResidualSeatsResearch.do`. Write/mutation endpoints (`additionalService`, `dealCarBuy`, `helpSrvCust`, `helpSrvTk`, `addCart`, `verifyMaasStatus`, `getMaasCancel`, `getMaasServiceCancel`, `getMaasServiceCancelFee`) and the `reserveList` read are deliberately absent, consistent with the client's read-only safety allowlists (`src/korail_mobile_api/safety.py`).


### 3.16 Travel Products (여행상품)

Packaged travel / tour products: reservation **list** & **detail**, pre-payment (settlement) **check**, and **cancel**, plus a set of product-browsing / voucher / receipt **WebViews** under `/ebizprd/*` and `/ebizmk/prd/*`.

**Host resolution.** The active server is `REAL`: `K4.g.SERVER_TYPE = EnumC5607a.fromCode("3")` (REAL) — `K4/g.java:90` + `d5/EnumC5607a.java:9`. Therefore every REST call resolves against `z.getSSLHost()` = `https://smart.letskorail.com` (`S4/z.java:46-49`, `ExecuteDao.java:60`) and every product WebView URL against `z.getWebHost()` = `https://smart.letskorail.com` (`S4/z.java:51-54`).

**REST surface.** Exactly **4** REST endpoints, all declared on the Retrofit interface `ProductService` (`com/korail/talk/network/dao/product/ProductService.java:11-23`). All are **HTTP GET with query params only — no request body.** Every request carries the 3 static credential query params from `BaseRequest` defaults: `Device=AD`, `Version=250601003`, `Key=korail1234567890` (`BaseRequest.java:7-18`). `Key` is a hardcoded static **app** key, not a per-session token.

**Auth model.** Session-cookie based (`JSESSIONID`). `KTApplication` installs a global `java.net.CookieManager` (`CookiePolicy.ACCEPT_ALL`) as the default `CookieHandler` (`KTApplication.java:73-75`), so the Retrofit `UrlConnectionClient` auto-sends `JSESSIONID`; the same cookie is mirrored into the WebView via `android.webkit.CookieManager.setCookie(z.getSSLHost(), …)` (`KTApplication.java:130-131`). List/detail are per-member and require a logged-in session.

**No signing, no encryption.** TLS only. The DynaPath anti-macro token (`x-dynapath-m-token`, `ExecuteDao.java:44-47`) is applied **only** to the 6 macro-protected URLs listed at `ExecuteDao.java:27` (login / reservation / schedule / prcFare). The `/classes/com.korail.mobile.product.*` paths are **not** in that list, so no token is attached.

#### 3.16.1 REST endpoints

| Name | Method | Path | Key params | Response | Auth | Source |
|------|--------|------|------------|----------|------|--------|
| getProductList (예약목록) | GET | `/classes/com.korail.mobile.product.ReservationList` | `Device=AD`, `Version=250601003`, `Key=korail1234567890`, `txtSelPage` (int, 1-based), `txtCntPerPage` (int, =10 from caller) | `ProductListResponse` → `mainInfo{ strTotCnt, entity: List<ReservationProduct>{ strGdNm, strRsvSttCd, strRsvSttNm, strStlDlnDt, strStlSttCd:EnumC5608b, strVrRsvNo } }` | JSESSIONID cookie + static Key | `ProductService.java:15-16`; `ProductListDao.java:29-105`; caller `TripBookingListActivity.java:142-149` |
| getProductDetail (예약상세) | GET | `/classes/com.korail.mobile.product.ReservationDetail` | `Device/Version/Key`, **`txtVrRsNo`** (bound from DTO `txtVrRsvNo`), `txtVrRsvSqNo` | `ProductDetailResponse` → `mainInfo:ProductInfo{ entityOne:List<EntityOne>{strGdConsItmNm}, strCncDlnDt, strCncRetAmt, strCncRetFee, strGdNm, strGdSqno, strInt11, strRcvdAmt, strRsvSttNm, strStlSttCd:EnumC5608b, strTotStlAmt, strUtlTrmCont, strVrRsvNo }` | JSESSIONID cookie | `ProductService.java:12-13`; `ProductDetailDao.java:24-135`; caller `TripBookingDetailActivity.java:116-124` |
| paymentCheck (payInfo) | GET | `/classes/com.korail.mobile.product.payInfo` | `Device/Version/Key`, `txtVrRsNo`, `txtRsvGdSqno` | `ProductPaymentCheckResponse` → `mainInfo{ strLumpStlTgtNo, strMrkAmtSum:int }` | JSESSIONID cookie | `ProductService.java:18-19`; `ProductPaymentCheckDao.java:11-67`; callers `TripBookingDetailActivity.java:106-114,157-168`, `IntegrationWebViewActivity.java:120-128,167-179` |
| productCancel (예약취소) | GET | `/classes/com.korail.mobile.product.ReservationCancel` | `Device/Version/Key`, `txtVrRsNo`, `txtGdSqno` | `BaseResponse{ strResult, h_msg_cd, h_msg_txt }` | JSESSIONID cookie | `ProductService.java:21-22`; `ProductCancelDao.java:11-45`; caller `TripBookingDetailActivity.java:57-65,126-131` |

Notes (REST):

- **Query-name quirk:** `getProductDetail`'s first data param is `@Query("txtVrRsNo")` — missing the second `v` — even though the source DTO field is `txtVrRsvNo` (`ProductService.java:13` vs `ProductDetailDao.java:25`). `paymentCheck` and `productCancel` also use the short `txtVrRsNo`. This quirk is faithfully reproduced in the read-only client (`src/.../read_payloads.py:467`).
- **Field reuse chain:** `getProductDetail` returns `strGdSqno`, which is captured and re-sent as `txtGdSqno` to `productCancel` and (as `txtRsvGdSqno`) to `paymentCheck` (`TripBookingDetailActivity.java:126-131`).
- **Payment is out-of-domain:** `paymentCheck` is a *pre-payment check only*; its `strLumpStlTgtNo` is handed to `V4.b.getIntgStlRequest(...)` → `PaymentActivity` with `IS_TRAVEL_PACKAGES=true` (`TripBookingDetailActivity.java:157-168`, `IntegrationWebViewActivity.java:167-179`). Actual settlement runs through the shared **payment** domain, not a product endpoint. `paymentCheck` is also invoked from the deep-link scheme `korailtalk://payment?strVrRsNo=&strGdSqno=` (`IntegrationWebViewActivity.java:120-128`).
- **Obfuscated settlement status:** `strStlSttCd` is typed as `d5.EnumC5608b` whose constants are fully obfuscated (`f31451`, `f31452`, `f31450`, `f31449`) with **no** `@SerializedName`/`@c` annotations (`d5/EnumC5608b.java:5-10`), so the real server status codes cannot be recovered from the decompile — Gson maps by enum name only. `f31449` is used as the "paid" gate for showing the voucher (`TripBookingDetailActivity.java`).
- **DAO ids:** `f.dao_reservation_product` (list), `f.dao_reservation_product_detail` (detail), `f.dao_product_payment_check` (payInfo), `f.dao_reservation_product_cancel` (cancel).
- **Client status:** the read-only Python client implements only the two non-money read endpoints — `get_product_reservations` → `ReservationList` (`client.py:524-541`) and `get_product_detail` → `ReservationDetail` (`client.py:543-562`), both with `include_common=True`, `include_dynapath=False`; `safety.py:58-59` allowlists only those two GETs. `paymentCheck` and `productCancel` are deliberately not implemented (money / state-changing).

#### 3.16.2 Product WebView endpoints (POST, form-urlencoded)

WebView endpoints use `WebView.postUrl(url, body.getBytes())` with plaintext form-urlencoded bodies (`BaseWebViewActivity.java:1049,1087`). When `IS_WEB_DEFAULT_PARAMETER` is set (default `true`, `BaseWebViewActivity.java:1029-1040`) the body is prefixed with `COMMON_PARAMETER = "Device=AD&Version=250601003&Key=korail1234567890"` (`K4/g.java:11`). The WebView carries the same `JSESSIONID` cookie synced from the native session.

| TOUR_PACKAGE_URL — travel package main (여행상품 메인) | POST | `/ebizmk/prd/main.do` | `COMMON_PARAMETER` only | HTML (IntegrationWebViewActivity) | WebView session cookie | `K4/g.java:95`; launch `TripBookingListActivity.java:202-208`; post `BaseWebViewActivity.java:1049` |
| VOUCHER_URL — product voucher / ticket | POST | `/ebizmk/prd/voucher.do` | `COMMON_PARAMETER` + `txtVrRsvNo=<VR_RSV_NO>` | HTML (IntegrationWebViewActivity) | WebView JSESSIONID | `K4/g.java:99`; launch `TripBookingDetailActivity.java:171-176,185-191`; post `BaseWebViewActivity.java:1049` |
| PRODUCT_MAAS_RECEIPT_URL — MAAS / add-service product receipt | POST | `/ebizprd/EbizPrdTicketPr13500w_pr13571_new.do` | `COMMON_PARAMETER` + `qry_dv_val=M&add_srv_req_no=<addSrvReqNo>` (PNR variant uses `qry_dv_val=E`) | HTML receipt (ExtraProductWebViewActivity, `IS_SCREEN_CAPTURE=true`) | WebView JSESSIONID | `K4/g.java:109,60,61`; callers `TicketListActivity.java:1178-1192`, `TicketPurchaseHistoryActivity.java:627-631` |
| PRODUCT_RECEIPT_URL — product receipt (**DEFINED BUT UNUSED / dead in v6.5.0**) | POST | `/ebizprd/EbizPrdTicketPr13500w_pr13571_new.do` | intended `COMMON_PARAMETER` + `qry_dv_val=B&` (`PRODUCT_RECEIPT_POST_DATA`) — never assembled | HTML (unused) | N/A (no caller) | `K4/g.java:108,64,65` |

Notes (WebView):

- **Two receipt constants, one path.** `PRODUCT_MAAS_RECEIPT_URL` (live) and `PRODUCT_RECEIPT_URL` (dead) both resolve to `/ebizprd/EbizPrdTicketPr13500w_pr13571_new.do`; they differ only by the leading POST-data flag `qry_dv_val`: live receipts use `M` (add-service) or `E` (PNR variant); the dead `PRODUCT_RECEIPT_URL` body would be `qry_dv_val=B&`. `PRODUCT_RECEIPT_URL`/`PRODUCT_RECEIPT_POST_DATA` have **zero references** in jadx or smali — reported for completeness only, not wired up.
- **Voucher gating.** `VOUCHER_URL` is shown only when the settlement state equals the "paid" enum constant `EnumC5608b.f31449` (`TripBookingDetailActivity.java:171-176`).
- **PRODUCT_URL cart lineage.** The product-browse WebView is launched from `DReservationConfirmActivity` on the `dao_add_product` DAO (`AddProductDao extends cart.AddCartDao` — cart domain), i.e. it is entered from a ticket-reservation flow, not from the travel-package list.

#### 3.16.3 NetFunnel gating (scope clarification)

`NETFUNNEL_ACTION_PRODUCT_ID = "act_6"` (`K4/g.java:46`) is **not** attached to any of the four `ProductService` travel-package DAOs (ReservationList / Detail / payInfo / Cancel) — those never set `mNetfunnelDao`, so there is no `act_6` queue gate on this domain's REST calls. However, `act_6` **is** a live NetFunnel gate elsewhere: it is selected when `this.f837Z instanceof ProductTrainInquiryRequest` and passed to `g.BEGIN(NETFUNNEL_SERVER_ID, str, …)` (`b5/c.java:439,450`; smali `apktool/smali/B5/c.smali:3227`) — i.e. it gates the 관광열차 / tour-train seat-inquiry flow (`ProductTrainInquiryDao`, launched from `DiscountGoodsBookingActivity`), a different "product" surface. (This matches the existing repo docs: `docs/deep-dive/agent-reports/05-train-search-schedule.md:34`, `docs/IMPLEMENTATION_PROGRESS.md:153`.)

#### 3.16.4 Scope boundary — not part of this domain

The Retrofit interface `AddService` (`com/korail/talk/network/dao/addService/AddService.java`) exposes the add-on-service (부가서비스) endpoints — `additionalService`, `dealCarBuy`, `getExtraProductList` (paths `/classes/com.korail.mobile.addService.*`), plus `helpSrvCust` and `helpSrvTk` (paths `/classes/com.korail.mobile.addSrv.helpSrvCust.do` / `…addSrv.helpSrvTk.do`, `AddService.java:16-34`). Despite the "ExtraProduct" naming, these attach add-on services to **tickets** and are **not** the 여행상품 travel-package domain — excluded here.

## 4. Data models

This section catalogs the key request/response DTOs and their supporting data classes, grouped by functional domain. Conventions used throughout:

- Every model lives under `com.korail.talk.network.*` in the decompiled tree (`analysis/jadx/sources/...`). Obfuscated helper classes (e.g. `S4.z`, `d5.EnumC5607a`) are named as they appear after jadx renaming and are flagged **(OBFUSCATED name)**.
- **Requests** are POSTed as `application/x-www-form-urlencoded`. Field-to-form mapping is done by Retrofit 1.x `@Field(...)` / `@FieldMap` on the corresponding service interface, so a Java field name (e.g. `loginId`) and its wire name (e.g. `txtMemberNo`) frequently differ. Where they differ it is noted `field -> "wire_name"`.
- **All field types are `String` unless otherwise stated.** Non-`String` types (`int`, `boolean`, `float`, `List<…>`, `HashMap`, `LinkedHashMap`, `android.text.Spanned`, nested DTOs) are called out explicitly.
- Fields marked **client-only** are populated/consumed on the device and never serialized onto the wire.

### 4.0 Common envelope types (shared by all domains)

Every request DTO extends `BaseRequest` and every response DTO extends `BaseResponse`. These are documented once here; per-domain models below inherit them via `+ inherited …`.

**`BaseRequest`** (implements `Serializable`) — supplies the three auth/version form fields present on every call.

| Field | Wire name | Type | Value / notes |
|---|---|---|---|
| `device` | `Device` | String | Defaulted to `ANDROID = "AD"` in the constructor |
| `version` | `Version` | String | Defaulted to `VERSION = "250601003"` in the constructor |
| `key` | `Key` | String | Defaulted to `APP_KEY = "korail1234567890"` in the constructor |

Static constants: `ANDROID = "AD"`, `APP_KEY = "korail1234567890"`, `VERSION = "250601003"`.
Source: `analysis/jadx/sources/com/korail/talk/network/BaseRequest.java:6-43`

**`BaseResponse`** (implements `Serializable`) — the response envelope. Field names are mapped by Gson `@SerializedName` (obfuscated as `S3.c`).

| Field | `@SerializedName` | Type | Notes |
|---|---|---|---|
| `hMsgCd` | `h_msg_cd` | String | Server message code |
| `hMsgTxt` | `h_msg_txt` | String | Server message text |
| `strResult` | `strResult` | String | `SUCCESS = "SUCC"` / `FAIL = "FAIL"` |

Source: `analysis/jadx/sources/com/korail/talk/network/BaseResponse.java:7-31`

---

### 4.2 Secure Keypad (nFilter)

The nFilter DTO is a near-vestigial pair: the server issues a `publicKey` that the app **never reads**. The real password-encryption key comes from `CommonCodeDao.Login` (§4.1). Supporting infra classes are listed at the end for completeness.

**`NFilterCreateKeyDao`** (extends `com.korail.talk.network.BaseDao`) — no instance fields. Key methods: `executeDao()` resolves `NFilterService` and calls `createKey(device, version, key)` (`:22-27`); `getId()` returns `R.id.dao_n_filter_create_key = 0x7f0a0207` (`:29-32`); `isPending()` overridden to `false`, suppressing the loading dialog (`:34-37`).
Source: `analysis/jadx/sources/com/korail/talk/network/dao/nFilter/NFilterCreateKeyDao.java:9-38`

**`NFilterCreateKeyDao.NFilterCreateKeyResponse`** (inner class, extends `BaseResponse`).

| Field | Type | Notes |
|---|---|---|
| `publicKey` | String | Private; getter `getPublicKey()` (`:17-19`). The **only** domain-specific field, and **never read anywhere in the app** |
| `hMsgCd` / `hMsgTxt` / `strResult` | String | Inherited envelope |
| `this$0` | `NFilterCreateKeyDao` | Synthetic outer ref (non-static inner class; confirmed in smali) |

Source: `analysis/jadx/sources/com/korail/talk/network/dao/nFilter/NFilterCreateKeyDao.java:11-20`; types confirmed in `analysis/apktool/smali/com/korail/talk/network/dao/nFilter/NFilterCreateKeyDao$NFilterCreateKeyResponse.smali`

**`NFilterService`** (Retrofit 1.x interface) — single method:
`@POST("/classes/com.korail.mobile.nFilter.createKey.do") @FormUrlEncoded createKey(@Field("Device") String, @Field("Version") String, @Field("Key") String) : NFilterCreateKeyResponse`. Synchronous (no `Callback`/`Observable`); executed on an AsyncTask by `BaseDaoHelper`.
Source: `analysis/jadx/sources/com/korail/talk/network/dao/nFilter/NFilterService.java:9-13`

**Supporting non-DTO infra (verified, not data models):**

- **`S4.C0812l`** *(OBFUSCATED name)* — password-crypto helper. `encryptAES(key, plaintext)` uses `AES/CBC/PKCS5Padding`, `SecretKeySpec` from `key.getBytes()`, IV from `key.substring(0,16).getBytes()`, returns Base64 (`:18-24`). `getAmountEncrypt(login, value)`: if `login.pwdAESCphd=="Y"` returns `Base64(encryptAES(login.key, value))`, else `Base64(value)` (`:26-41`). `getSid()` uses a **hardcoded** key `"2485dd54d9deaa36"` over `"AD"+currentTimeMillis` (`:43-50`). Source: `analysis/jadx/sources/S4/C0812l.java:17-51`
- **`ExecuteDao`** — Retrofit 1.x `RestAdapter.Builder` factory (Gson converter, `UrlConnectionClient`, 60 s connect/read timeouts). Anti-macro hook adds header `x-dynapath-m-token` for 6 hardcoded paths — the nFilter path is **not** among them. Source: `analysis/jadx/sources/com/korail/talk/network/ExecuteDao.java:17-66`
- **`S4.z`** *(OBFUSCATED name)* — host resolver. `getSSLHost()`: REAL→`https://smart.letskorail.com`, TEST→`https://dev3.letskorail.com`, STAGING→`https://dev2.letskorail.com`, else→`https://mobiledev.letskorail.com`. Source: `analysis/jadx/sources/S4/z.java:36-54`
- **`d5.EnumC5607a`** *(OBFUSCATED name)* — server-environment enum: `DEV("0")`, `STAGING("1")`, `TEST("2")`, `REAL("3")`. Bound at `K4/g.java:90` as `SERVER_TYPE = fromCode("3")` ⇒ REAL ⇒ `https://smart.letskorail.com`. Source: `analysis/jadx/sources/d5/EnumC5607a.java`; binding at `analysis/jadx/sources/K4/g.java:74,89-91`

### 4.4 Train Info / Fare / Consist

Six DAOs covering fare lookup, free-car notice, station selection, tour-train info, and per-stop schedule. All requests derive their params from `RsvInquiryResponse.TrainInfo` (§4.3).

**`Price2FareDao.Price2FareRequest`** — `txtMenuId`, `chtnDvCd` (`"1"` direct / `"2"` transfer), `trnCnt` (never set — setter self-assigns), `price2FareParams: Price2FareParams`; client-only: `mSelectIndex: int`, `mTrainInfo: RsvInquiryResponse.TrainInfo[]`.
Source: `analysis/jadx/sources/com/korail/talk/network/dao/trainsInfo/Price2FareDao.java:127-177`

**`Price2FareDao.Price2FareParams`** (`LinkedHashMap<String,String>` as `@FieldMap`) — keys: `trnGpCd`, `stlbTrnClsfCd`, `dptRsStnCd`, `arvRsStnCd` (from `C1262b.ARV_RS_STN_CD`), `runDt`, `trnNo`, `gdNo`, `rqSeatAttCd`.
Source: `Price2FareDao.java:81-125`

**`Price2FareDao.Price2Fare`** (fare row; also holds wire key-name constants) — `trnNo` (const `trnNoString="trnNo"`), `jrnySqno` (`"jrnySqno"`), `psrmClNm` (`"psrmClNm"`), `rcvdPrc` (`"rcvdPrc"`), `rcvdFare` (`"rcvdFare"`), `sumAmt` (`"sumAmt"`).
Source: `Price2FareDao.java:15-79`

**`Price2FareDao.Price2FareResponse`** — `prcList: List<Price2Fare>` + inherited envelope.
Source: `Price2FareDao.java:179-188`

**`FresScarDao.FresScarRequest`** (free-car lookup) — `runDt`, `trnNo`, `dptStnConsOrdr`, `arvStnConsOrdr`, `dptStnRunOrdr`, `arvStnRunOrdr`.
Source: `analysis/jadx/sources/com/korail/talk/network/dao/trainsInfo/FresScarDao.java:11-69`

**`FresScarDao.FresScarResponse`** — `fresTtl` (notice title), `fresScarNo` (free-car number(s) — the only field rendered, `A5/u.java:918`), `fresCont` (notice content) + inherited envelope.

**`PriceFareDao` family (dead code)** — retained for completeness. `PriceFareRequest{ txtMenuId, txtRtnDvCd, txtChtrDvCd1, txtSeatAttCd4: String; priceFareParams: PriceFareParams; mSelectIndex: int (client-only); mTrainInfo: RsvInquiryResponse.TrainInfo (client-only) }`; `PriceFareParams` (`LinkedHashMap` `@FieldMap`) with paired keys `txtTrnClsfCd1`/`_1`, `txtDptRsStnCd1`/`_1`, `txtArvRsStnCd1`/`_1`, `txtRunDt1`/`_1`, `txtTrnNo1`/`_1`, `txtTrnGpCd1`/`_1`; `PriceFareResponse{ prc_fare_list: PrcFareList }` → `PrcFareList{ jrny_info: List<JrnyInfo> }` → `JrnyInfo{ prc_fare: List<PriceFare> }` → `PriceFare{ h_psg_tp_nm, h_psrm_cl_nm, h_rg_rcvd_amt: String }`.
Source: `analysis/jadx/sources/com/korail/talk/network/dao/trainsInfo/PriceFareDao.java:14-177`

**`TrainSelectStationDao`** (transfer-station picker) — `TrainSelectStationRequest{ dptRsStnCd, arvRsStnCd }`; `TrainSelectStationResponse{ chtnList: List<TransferStationInfo> }`; `TransferStationInfo{ chtnRsStnCd (default ""), chtnRsStnNm (default "전체") }` + inherited envelope.

#### New-style `@FieldMap` carriers (`R*`)

These `LinkedHashMap<String,String>` subclasses flatten indexed journey/seat/passenger data into form fields. Index placeholders: `{i}` = leg/journey index, `{j}` = seat index within a leg. Detailed key-name constants are given in §4.6 (same classes; reused verbatim by `reservationChange.do`).

| Class | Flattened keys (abridged) |
|---|---|
| `RJrny` | `jrnyCnt`, and per-leg `jrnySqno_{i}`, `jrnyTpCd_{i}`, `trnGpCd_{i}`, `stlbTrnClsfCd_{i}`, `trnNo_{i}`, `runDt_{i}`, `dptDt_{i}`, `dptTm_{i}`, `dptRsStnCd_{i}`, `dptStnConsOrdr_{i}`, `dptStnRunOrdr_{i}`, `arvDt_{i}`, `arvTm_{i}`, `arvRsStnCd_{i}`, `arvStnConsOrdr_{i}`, `arvStnRunOrdr_{i}`, `chgFlg_{i}` |
| `RSrcar` | `srcarCnt_{i}`, `scarCnt_{i}`, `scarNo_{i}_{j}` (setter `setSrcarNo`), `seatNo_{i}_{j}` |
| `RSeat` | `seatCnt_{i}`, `seatPsrmClCd_{i}_{j}`, `rqSeatAttCd_{i}_{j}`, `smkSeatAttCd_{i}_{j}`, `dirSeatAttCd_{i}_{j}`, `locSeatAttCd_{i}_{j}`, `etcSeatAttCd_{i}_{j}`, `roomClsfCd_{i}_{j}` |
| `RPsg` | `psgCnt`, `psgInfoPerPrnb_{i}`, `psgTpDvCd_{i}` |
| `ROrtg` | `ortgCnt`, `ogtkSaleWctNo_{i}`, `ogtkSaleDd_{i}`, `ogtkSaleSqno_{i}`, `ogtkRetPwd_{i}`, `cmtrDvCd_{i}`, `retNoMnlInpFlg_{i}` |

## 5. Local storage

This section documents how `com.korail.talk` v6.5.0 persists data on-device. Three distinct mechanisms are used; there is **no** Room, no raw `SQLiteOpenHelper`, no Jetpack DataStore, and no `getSharedPreferences(name, …)` with a custom file name anywhere under `com/korail/talk`:

| Mechanism | Technology | Used for | Wrapper class |
|-----------|-----------|----------|---------------|
| Structured DB | OrmLite (`com.j256.ormlite`) over SQLite | stations, favorites, cards, ticket/issue cache, popups, SMS | `J4.a` (helper), `J4.b` (manager) |
| Key/value prefs | Android **default** `SharedPreferences` (`PreferenceManager.getDefaultSharedPreferences`) | login credentials, flags, cached JSON config | `S4.H` |
| File caches | files under `getExternalCacheDir()` (fallback `getFilesDir()`) | images, receipts, QR codes | `S4.C0813m`, constants in `K4.a` |

The DB helper (`J4.a`), DB manager (`J4.b`), prefs wrapper (`S4.H`), file-cache util (`S4.C0813m`), constants (`K4.a`), login fragment (`k5.b`) and session holder (`b5.h`) are obfuscated single-letter class names; the entity model classes (`com.korail.talk.database.model.*`) and their getters/setters are **not** obfuscated.

### 5.1 SQLite database (OrmLite)

- **DB name:** `korailtalk.sqlite`, **schema version 15** — `super(context, "korailtalk.sqlite", null, 15)` (`analysis/jadx/sources/j4/a.java:55-57`).
- **Helper:** `J4.a extends com.j256.ormlite.android.apptools.OrmLiteSqliteOpenHelper` (`j4/a.java:26`).
- **Manager:** `J4.b` is a singleton wrapper (`getInstance()` / `init(context)`), initialized from `KTApplication.d()` → `J4.b.init` at `analysis/jadx/sources/com/korail/talk/application/KTApplication.java:65`. It exposes typed CRUD helpers over the DAOs.
- `onCreate` (`j4/a.java:140-156`) creates 9 tables via `TableUtils.createTableIfNotExists`: `StationData`, `FavoriteStation`, `CreditCard`, `IssueList`, `TicketDetail`, `ZRecentStation`, `MainPopupData`, `DoNotLookAgain`, `SMSData`.
- **DAO accessors** (`j4/a.java:73-138`): `getStationListDao`, `getFavoriteSectionDao`, `getCreditCardDao`, `getOldCreditCardDao`, `getIssueListDao`, `getTicketDetailDao`, `getRecentSectionDao`, `getMainPopupDataDao`, `getDoNotLookAgainDataDao`, `getSMSData`.

#### Schema migration (`onUpgrade`, `j4/a.java:158-201`)

On any version change (`if (i9 != i10)`), the upgrade path:

1. **Drops and recreates** `StationData`, `MainPopupData`, `DoNotLookAgain` (dropped at `j4/a.java:163-165`; recreated when `onCreate` is re-invoked at `:182`). These three are treated as regenerable caches.
2. **Migrates** legacy `com.korail.talk.database.model.old.CreditCard` rows into the new `CreditCard` table: for each old row it copies `id`, `cardNickname`, `cardNumber`, `cardValidateMonth`, `cardValidateYear` and sets `cardType = "0"` (`j4/a.java:167-186`), then drops the old table (`:181`). Note the new-only field `businessNum` is **not** migrated — the legacy entity has no such field (`com/korail/talk/database/model/old/CreditCard.java:6-21`).
3. **Preserves** (never dropped) `FavoriteStation`, `IssueList`, `TicketDetail`, `ZRecentStation`, `SMSData` across the upgrade.

#### DB access-layer business rules (`J4.b`)

- **Recent stations are capped at 2 rows.** `insertRecentSection()` calls a trim routine `a()` that deletes all but the 2 newest `ZRecentStation` rows by timestamp (`analysis/jadx/sources/j4/b.java:35-46`, `:400-419`).
- Recent stations are ordered by `timestamp` desc; favorites and credit cards are ordered by `id` desc.
- `DoNotLookAgain` rows are queried by the composite `custMgNo` + `doNotLookAgainType` (`j4/b.java:245`).

#### Entities

All entities are POJOs annotated with OrmLite `@DatabaseField`; primary keys are declared with `id = true` or `generatedId = true`.

| Entity / Table | Key | Fields | Notes | Source |
|----------------|-----|--------|-------|--------|
| `StationData` | `id` (`id = true`) | `stnCd`, `stnNm`, `group`, `major`, `latitude`, `longitude`, `doNotLookADay`, `doNotLookAgain` (bool), `popupType` (int), `popupMessage`, `popupLinkTitle`, `popupLinkUrl` | Station master list; dropped+recreated on upgrade | `com/korail/talk/database/model/StationData.java:6-45` |
| `FavoriteStation` | `id` (`generatedId = true`) | `startStation`, `startStationCode`, `arrivalStation`, `arrivalStationCode` | Favorite O–D sections; preserved on upgrade | `com/korail/talk/database/model/FavoriteStation.java:6-21` |
| `ZRecentStation` | `id` (`generatedId = true`) | `startStation`, `startStationCode`, `arrivalStation`, `arrivalStationCode`, `timestamp` (long) | Recent O–D sections; **capped at 2 rows**, ordered by `timestamp` | `com/korail/talk/database/model/ZRecentStation.java:6-24`; cap logic `j4/b.java:35-46` |
| `CreditCard` | `id` (`generatedId = true`) | `cardNickname`, `cardNumber`, `cardType`, `cardValidateMonth`, `cardValidateYear`, `businessNum` | Saved cards; **no field-level encryption observed** for card data | `com/korail/talk/database/model/CreditCard.java:6-27` |
| `old.CreditCard` | `id` (`generatedId = true`) | `cardNickname`, `cardNumber`, `cardValidateMonth`, `cardValidateYear` | Legacy, migration-only; dropped after migration into `CreditCard` (`cardType` set to `"0"`) | `com/korail/talk/database/model/old/CreditCard.java:6-21`; migration `j4/a.java:167-186` |
| `IssueList` | `id` (`generatedId = true`) | `issueList` — **AES-encrypted JSON** of the issued-ticket list | Encrypted at `TicketListActivity.java:1402` | `com/korail/talk/database/model/IssueList.java:6-13` |
| `TicketDetail` | `id` (`generatedId = true`) | `pnrNo`, `ticketDetail` — **AES-encrypted JSON** | Encrypted at `TicketListActivity.java:341`; cached ticket detail keyed by PNR | `com/korail/talk/database/model/TicketDetail.java:6-15` |
| `MainPopupData` | `id` (`generatedId = true`) | `noticeId` (int), `checkType` (int; 0=HIDDEN, 1=SHOW), `isShow` (Y/N), `title`, `message`, `imageUrl`, `linkTitle`, `linkUrl`, `buttonType`, `clsBtn`, `size`, `voice`, `isExternalBrowser`, `confirmDate` | Main-screen popup/notice; dropped+recreated on upgrade | `com/korail/talk/database/model/MainPopupData.java:6-53` |
| `DoNotLookAgain` | `custMgNo` (`id = true`, `uniqueCombo = true`) | `doNotLookAgainType` (int, `uniqueCombo = true`), `confirmDate` | Per-user "do not show again"; composite key; dropped+recreated on upgrade | `com/korail/talk/database/model/DoNotLookAgain.java:6-15` |
| `SMSData` | `pnrNo` (`id = true`) | `phoneNumber` — **AES-encrypted**; `implements Serializable` | Guardian-relief SMS per PNR; encrypted at `GuardianReliefSmsActivity.java:131` | `com/korail/talk/database/model/SMSData.java:7-13` |

### 5.2 SharedPreferences

`S4.H` statically wraps `PreferenceManager.getDefaultSharedPreferences(context)`, so every key lives in the single default prefs file **`com.korail.talk_preferences.xml`**. It offers `getString/putString`, `getBoolean/putBoolean`, `getInt/putInt`, and `getIntList/putIntList` (int lists serialized as a JSON-array string). All writes use `commit()` (`analysis/jadx/sources/S4/H.java:12-78`).

#### Encryption of sensitive prefs

Sensitive values are AES-encrypted via `F4.a.encryptAES` / `decryptAES` before storage. That routine uses **`AES/ECB/PKCS5Padding`** with a 16-byte key derived from the device's `Settings.Secure.ANDROID_ID` (`UUID.nameUUIDFromBytes(android_id)`, truncated to 16 bytes) — see `analysis/jadx/sources/F4/a.java:13-41`. This is a *device-local* encryption scheme, distinct from the server-transmission login crypto (AES/CBC keyed on `LoginCryptoInfo`) implemented in the Python client.

Encrypted keys: `KEY_LOGIN_ID`, `KEY_LOGIN_PW`, `KEY_LOGIN_CUST_NO` (written in `k5/b.java:461-470`) and the veterans-number key `보훈번호` (`VeteransNoSettingActivity.java:74`, read at `:33`). `KEY_LOGIN_TYPE` and `KEY_LOGIN_INTEGRATION_*` are plaintext. **Logout** (`b5/h.java:182-197`, `initLoginData`) sets `KEY_AUTO_LOGIN = false` and `KEY_LOGIN_PW = ""` but does **not** clear `KEY_LOGIN_ID`.

#### Key catalog

Wrapper: `S4/H.java:12-78`; keys enumerated across the app (`k5/b.java`, `IntroActivity.java`, `a5/u.java`, `i5/C5785c.java`, `VeteransNoSettingActivity.java`, etc.).

| Key | Type | Meaning | Crypto | Source |
|-----|------|---------|--------|--------|
| `KEY_LOGIN_TYPE` | String | Login method type | plaintext | `k5/b.java:457` |
| `KEY_LOGIN_ID` | String | Login id | AES | `k5/b.java:461,465,468` |
| `KEY_LOGIN_PW` | String | Password; cleared to `""` on logout | AES | `k5/b.java:462`; `b5/h.java:185` |
| `KEY_LOGIN_CUST_NO` | String | Customer number | AES | `k5/b.java:470` |
| `KEY_AUTO_LOGIN` | Boolean | Auto-login enabled | — | `k5/b.java:460,464`; `b5/h.java:184` |
| `KEY_MEMBER_NUM` | Boolean | Save-member-number flag | — | `k5/b.java:458` |
| `KEY_LOGIN_INTEGRATION_FLAG` / `_MSG` / `_URL` | String | Account-integration flag/msg/url | plaintext | `k5/b.java:472-474` |
| `보훈번호` | String | Veterans/patriot number (literal Korean key) | AES | `VeteransNoSettingActivity.java:74,33` |
| `IMAGE_DOWN_LOAD_DATA` | String (JSON `CommonCodeDao.ImageDownLoadData`) | Image-download config | — | `IntroActivity.java:470-471` |
| `MAP_VERSION` | Int | Cached station-map data version | — | `IntroActivity.java:435,441` |
| `TrainType` | String | Last selected train-type name | — | `a5/u.java:791,853`; `S4/O.java:24` |
| `BOOKING_TOGGLE_OPTION` | IntList (JSON array) | Checked booking-toggle indices | — | `i5/C5785c.java:30`; `p5/C6244c.java:90` |
| `IS_ACCUMULATING_KTX_MILEGE` | Boolean | KTX mileage-accumulation flag | — | `a5/g.java:40,187` |
| `IS_TICKET_AUTO_REFRESH` | Boolean | Auto-refresh tickets (default true) | — | `SettingActivity` |
| `IS_DIALOG_AUTO_POPUP_CHATBOT` | Boolean | Chatbot auto-popup | — | `SettingActivity` / `MainBooking` |
| `IS_MULTI_LANGUAGE_TYPE` | String | Selected UI language | — | `BaseWebViewActivity` |
| `IS_DEVICE_POPUP_TODAY` | String (`yyyyMMdd`) | Date OS-version popup last shown | — | `MainBooking` |
| `is_exist_push_data` | String | Customer no whose push data exists | — | `TicketListActivity` |
| `KEY_SETTING_SECURE_ANDROID_ID` | Boolean | Secure `ANDROID_ID` setting | — | `SettingActivity` |
| `REQUIRED_PERMISSIONS` | Boolean | Runtime-permissions-requested flag | — | `IntroActivity` |
| `CONVENIENCE_SETTING_VISIBLE` | Boolean | "Buy now" convenience setting visible | — | `CommonCode` |
| `CONVENIENCE_SETTING_UPDATE` | Boolean | Convenience-setting-changed flag | — | `IntroActivity` / `Booking` |
| `TRAIN_NEAR_STATION` / `TRAIN_SRT_STATION` / `TRAIN_NEAR_SRT_STATION` | Boolean | Nearby-station toggles (default true) | — | app-wide |
| `KEY_LIMOUSINE_MAIN_MSG` / `KEY_LIMOUSINE_MSG` | String | Limousine messages | — | `CommonCode` / `AppData` |
| `KEY_RAIL_PLUS_CARD_INFO` | String | RailPlus card info | — | `AppData` |
| `DISABILITY_CERTIFICATION_MSG` | String | Disability-certification message | — | `AppData` |
| `NOTICE_DATA` | String (JSON) | Notice payload | — | app-wide |
| `ATHN`, `EASY_LOGIN`, `EASY_PAY_OPTION`, `HOLIDAY_POPUP_DATA`, `KORAIL_BOSS`, `LOGIN_DATA`, `LOST_ARTICLE`, `MENU_BIZ`, `MENU_RAIL_POINT`, `OREO_DATA`, `POINT_PAY_OPTION`, `REPORT_DATA`, `VAR_DATA`, `VIEW_VISIBILITY` | String | Cached JSON sub-objects of `CommonCodeResponse` (stored via `q.toJson`) | — | CommonCode handler |

> **Not SharedPreferences:** the tokens `AGE_TYPE`, `SEAT_NO`, `AMOUNT` are `android.os.Bundle` keys (`bundleH.getString`) at `analysis/jadx/sources/com/korail/talk/ui/ticket/change/TCSActivity.java:168-170`, not prefs keys.

### 5.3 File caches

Utility `S4.C0813m` writes files into subfolders of `getExternalCacheDir()` (fallback `getFilesDir()`); folder names and limits are constants in `K4.a` (`analysis/jadx/sources/K4/a.java:4-16`):

| Folder / constant | Contents | Filenames | Lifecycle |
|-------------------|----------|-----------|-----------|
| `volatility` (`VOLATILITY_FOLDER`) | Receipts, share images | `receipt.png` (`RECEIPT_IMAGE_NM`), `share.png` (`SHARE_IMAGE_NM`) | **Cleared on app start** |
| `qr` (`QR_FOLDER`) | QR-code images | — | **Cleared on app start** |
| `thumbs` (`THUMBS_FOLDER`) | Downloaded images | MD5-hashed name + `.tmp` (`getMD5File`) | Retained |
| `non_volatile` (`NON_VOLATILE_FOLDER`) | Persistent files | — | Retained |

Other constants: `MIN_STORAGE_SIZE = 10485760` (10 MB minimum free required before caching), `FILE_PROVIDER_AUTHORITIES = "com.korail.talk.fileprovider"`, `FONT_NAME = "fonts/yun.ttf"`.

- Image caching is driven by the `IMAGE_DOWN_LOAD_DATA` pref in `IntroActivity.getDownLoadImageData` (`IntroActivity.java:461-487`); downloaded images land in `thumbs` with MD5-hashed filenames.
- Receipt/share images are written to `volatility` (e.g. `TicketReceiptActivity.java:267`, `ticket/confirm/a.java:72`).
- **Cache clear-on-launch:** `KTApplication.b()` (`KTApplication.java:47-54`) deletes the entire `volatility` and `qr` folders on every app start; `thumbs` and `non_volatile` are retained.

Source: `analysis/jadx/sources/S4/C0813m.java:27-124`, `K4/a.java:4-16`, `KTApplication.java:47-54`.

### 5.4 Backup, export, and FileProvider

- **`android:allowBackup="false"`** on the `<application>` element (`analysis/apktool/AndroidManifest.xml:100`), so the SQLite DB and SharedPreferences are excluded from Android auto-backup.
- The manifest declares exactly **one** FileProvider: `androidx.core.content.FileProvider`, authority `com.korail.talk.fileprovider`, `exported="false"`, `grantUriPermissions="true"` (`AndroidManifest.xml:102`). Its `FILE_PROVIDER_PATHS` meta-data references **only** `@xml/provider_paths` (`AndroidManifest.xml:103`) — path config `res/xml/provider_paths.xml` (external-cache + external root).
- `res/xml/file_paths.xml` (external-cache `volatility/`) is referenced only in `com/korail/talk/R.java` with **no** manifest provider wiring — legacy/unused, not attached to `com.korail.talk.fileprovider`.
- `res/xml/image_share_filepaths.xml` (files-dir `image_provider/`) is a **Kakao SDK** resource (defined in `com.kakao.sdk.v2.*` R.java), belonging to a different SDK's provider — not `com.korail.talk.fileprovider`.

### 5.5 In-memory session state (not persisted)

`b5.h` is a login/session data holder carrying member name/number/birth/phone/email, `custNo`, encrypted member-card / customer numbers, `custClCd`, and `youthType`. These live **only in memory** and are reset on logout; only the credential/flag subset in §5.2 is persisted to SharedPreferences (`analysis/jadx/sources/b5/h.java`).


## 6. WebView & URL schemes

`com.korail.talk` is a WebView-heavy hybrid app. A large share of booking, payment,
identity-verification and partner flows run inside `WebView`s that talk to native code
through `@JavascriptInterface` bridges, and the app both consumes and emits a family of
custom `korailtalk://` (and partner) URL schemes. This section catalogs every JS bridge,
every `loadUrl`/`postUrl` target, and every deep link / intent-filter data scheme.

### 6.1 JS bridges (`@JavascriptInterface`)

An exhaustive `addJavascriptInterface` sweep of the decompiled sources (excluding
androidx / kotlin / okhttp / retrofit / google / gms / facebook / firebase / reactivex /
kakao / nhn / naver / zxing libraries) found exactly **one** bridge in the `com.korail`
app package (`BaseWebViewActivity.java:1103`), a second in the obfuscated `t5` package
(`t5/c.java:190`, MaumAI voice V2), and a third belonging to the vendored `ai.maum.m2u`
SDK (`ai/maum/m2u/cdk/CdkNative.java:468`). The only remaining bridges are inside Google
libraries (`gmaSdk`, `googleAdsJsInterface`) and are out of scope.

#### 6.1.1 Primary bridge — name `"korailtalk"` (`BaseWebViewActivity.c`)

The bridge object is the obfuscated inner class `c`
(`BaseWebViewActivity.java:299-503`), attached via
`webView.addJavascriptInterface(obj, g.PUSH_APPTYPE)` in `d1()`
(`BaseWebViewActivity.java:1103`). The bridge name is the constant
`g.PUSH_APPTYPE = "korailtalk"` (`K4/g.java:67`). **Every** WebView subclass reuses this
same bridge object via `d1(new BaseWebViewActivity.c(), ...)`:
`EasyPayWebViewActivity:58`, `ExtraProductWebViewActivity:67`,
`IntegrationWebViewActivity:195`, `OnepassWebViewActivity:107`,
`MaumAIWebViewActivity:138`, `TrainServiceInfoWebViewActivity:235`,
`TrainServiceWebViewActivity:52`.

The bridge exposes **26 `@JavascriptInterface` methods**. Most post a message to the inner
`Handler b` (`BaseWebViewActivity.java:147-296`) keyed by `message.what`; the native action
runs on that handler. Method names are **not** obfuscated (they must match the web
contract), so the JS API surface is fully readable.

| # | JS method (`korailtalk.…`) | Native action | `what` | Source |
| - | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| 1 | `appBack(String)` | `onBackPressed()` | 1 | `BaseWebViewActivity.java:304` |
| 2 | `cartlist()` | open `BasketTicketActivity` | 22 | `:311` |
| 3 | `certificationIdSuccess(String)` | `setResult` `USER_ID` (result 8) | 8 | `:318` |
| 4 | `certificationPwSuccess(String)` | `setResult` `USER_PW` (result 9) | 9 | `:326` |
| 5 | `changeLanguage(String)` | persist `IS_MULTI_LANGUAGE_TYPE` to prefs | 11 | `:334` |
| 6 | `clearHistory()` | `webView.clearHistory()` | 14 | `:342` |
| 7 | `fn_webViewOpenRsult(String)` | log only | – | `:349` |
| 8 | `fn_webViewSeatSetResult(String)` | toast + JSON parse | – | `:354` |
| 9 | `goHome()` | open `MainBookingActivity` | 12 | `:365` |
| 10 | `goMaasPayment(String)` | `PaymentActivity` `PAYMENT_MAAS` | 18 | `:372` |
| 11 | `goPayment(String)` | `PaymentActivity` `PAYMENT_PRODUCT` | 10 | `:380` |
| 12 | `goSelectFromDate()` | MAAS rent-car rent-date picker | 19 | `:388` |
| 13 | `goSelectToDate()` | MAAS rent-car return-date picker | 20 | `:395` |
| 14 | `hideLoadingDialog(String)` | dismiss loading | 6 | `:402` |
| 15 | `identityVerificationSuccess(String)` | `setResult` `IDENTITY_VERIFICATION_SUCCESS` (result 16) | 16 | `:409` |
| 16 | `login(String)` | `setResult` `USER_AUTHENTICATION` (result 3) | 3 | `:417` |
| 17 | `moveToTrainTime(String)` | open `TrainServiceInfoWebViewActivity` | 15 | `:425` |
| 18 | `nonmember()` | non-member flow | 31 | `:433` |
| 19 | `nonmemberResult(String,String,String)` | concat `a;b;c` | 32 | `:440` (concat `:443`) |
| 20 | `refreshCustTrip()` | `CONVENIENCE_SETTING_UPDATE` | 13 | `:448` |
| 21 | `sendCalendar(String)` | date picker | 7 | `:455` |
| 22 | `sessionExpired(String)` | re-login (auto) / `moveToLogin` | 4 | `:468` |
| 23 | `setTitle(String)` | `setAppTitle` | – | `:481` |
| 24 | `showLoadingDialog(String)` | show loading | 5 | `:486` |
| 25 | `showToast(String)` | `Toast` | – | `:493` |
| 26 | `windowClose()` | `finish()` | 2 | `:498` |

Security-sensitive behavior:

- **`goPayment(String)` / `goMaasPayment(String)`** launch `PaymentActivity` with the
  **raw JS-supplied string** placed into `PNR_NO_LIST[0]` / `PRODUCT_NO`
  (handler `BaseWebViewActivity.java:222-237`). Web content therefore initiates payment
  and controls the PNR / product number.
- **`login(String)`** returns the raw JS string to the launching activity as the
  `USER_AUTHENTICATION` result (result code 3, handler `:180-185`).
- **`certificationIdSuccess` / `certificationPwSuccess` / `identityVerificationSuccess`**
  return raw JS strings as `USER_ID` / `USER_PW` / `IDENTITY_VERIFICATION_SUCCESS`
  (result codes 8 / 9 / 16). These are the WebView return channels for the find-id/pw and
  identity-verification (본인인증) web flows.
- **`sessionExpired(String)`** re-authenticates silently if `KEY_AUTO_LOGIN` is set
  (`E()`), otherwise routes to `moveToLogin` (handler `:186-193`).
- **`changeLanguage(String)`** is persisted to shared prefs (`what=11`).

**App → JS callback injection** (`K0()`, `BaseWebViewActivity.java:977-991`): the native
side calls back into the page with
`loadUrl("javascript:<callback>('<json>')")`, where the `callback` function name is read
from the **web-supplied JSON `callback` field** — i.e. web content chooses which JS
function the app invokes back on it.

#### 6.1.2 Second `"korailtalk"` bridge — MaumAI voice V2 (`t5.c.C0414c`)

The MaumAI voice-avatar V2 feature registers a **separate** bridge object under the
**same** name `"korailtalk"` at `t5/c.java:190` (`addJavascriptInterface(new C0414c(), …)`).
`MaumAIV2WebViewActivity` extends `BaseViewActivity` (not `BaseWebViewActivity`) and hosts
the WebView inside fragment `t5.e` (`loadUrl` at `t5/e.java:104`). The bridge object
`C0414c` (`t5/c.java:136-160`) exposes only three methods:

| JS method | Native action | Source |
| --- | --- | --- |
| `appBack()` | `WebView.goBack()` or `Activity.finish()` | `t5/c.java:141` |
| `showToast(String)` | `Toast` | `t5/c.java:152` |
| `windowClose()` | `Activity.finish()` | `t5/c.java:157` |

Its `WebViewClient` (`t5/c.java:112-118`) only intercepts `tel:` URIs (→ `DIAL` intent);
everything else loads in-WebView. Note that this WebView **enables** contents debugging:
`setWebContentsDebuggingEnabled(true)` when `IS_DEBUG_LOG` (`t5/c.java:191-192`).

#### 6.1.3 Third bridge — name `"m2uWebViewNative"` (vendored MaumAI SDK, third-party)

The vendored `ai.maum.m2u.cdk` SDK (package `ai.maum`, **not** `com.korail.talk`)
registers a `JavaScriptReceiver` under the name `"m2uWebViewNative"` at
`ai/maum/m2u/cdk/CdkNative.java:468`. Its `@JavascriptInterface` methods
(`ai/maum/m2u/cdk/JavaScriptReceiver.java`):

| JS method (`m2uWebViewNative.…`) | Effect | Source |
| --- | --- | --- |
| `closeMicrophone()` | stop mic | `JavaScriptReceiver.java:26` |
| `closeSpeaker()` | stop speaker | `:35` |
| `getPhoneNumber() : String` | **returns the device phone number** to web content | `:44` |
| `openMicrophone(String)` | start mic | `:53` |
| `sendEvent(String)` | forward event | `:62` |
| `setMapSetting(String)` | map config | `:92` |

This bridge lets web content read the device phone number and control the microphone /
speaker.

### 6.2 WebView configuration & security posture

Configured in `d1()` (`BaseWebViewActivity.java:1108-1132`):

- JS fully enabled: `setJavaScriptEnabled(true)` (`:1120`), `setDomStorageEnabled(true)`
  (`:1124`), `setGeolocationEnabled(true)` (`:1121`), `setDatabaseEnabled(true)` (`:1122`),
  `setSupportMultipleWindows(true)` (`:1126`).
- `setMixedContentMode(2)` = `MIXED_CONTENT_ALWAYS_ALLOW` (`:1128`) — mixed HTTP content is
  loaded over HTTPS pages.
- **Geolocation prompts auto-granted**: `WebChromeClient.onGeolocationPermissionsShowPrompt`
  invokes the callback with `allow=true` (`BaseWebViewActivity.java:141-143`).
- **Third-party cookies accepted** (`Z0()`, `:933-935`).
- **User-Agent fingerprint**: the UA is suffixed with `" korailtalk AppVersion/<VERSION_NAME>"`
  (`:1125`, `g.PUSH_APPTYPE` + `G4.a.VERSION_NAME`) — a distinctive marker for app WebView
  traffic.
- **WebContents debugging**: `BaseWebViewActivity.java:1104-1106` is literally
  `if (I4.a.IS_DEBUG_LOG) { WebView.setWebContentsDebuggingEnabled(false); }` — the value
  passed is hardcoded **`false`**, so contents debugging is **never** enabled in the base
  activity. (Contrast the MaumAI V2 WebView, which sets it `true` under `IS_DEBUG_LOG`;
  see 6.1.2.)

### 6.3 `loadUrl` / `postUrl` targets & intent-extra contract

`BaseWebViewActivity` is driven entirely by intent extras (`V0()` `:1008-1061`,
`W0()` `:1063-1088`):

| Intent extra | Meaning |
| --- | --- |
| `WEB_GET_URL` / `WEB_POST_URL` | target URL |
| `WEB_GET_PARAMETER` / `WEB_POST_PARAMETER` | query / body params |
| `WEB_POST_JSON_BODY` | JSON body for the `IS_SRT_WEB_RESERVE` manual POST |
| `IS_WEB_DEFAULT_PARAMETER` (bool) | prefix `COMMON_PARAMETER` |
| `IS_MAAS_URL` (bool) | append with `&` if URL already has `?` |
| `IS_SRT_WEB_RESERVE` (bool) | use manual `HttpURLConnection` POST path |
| `IS_MAP_VIEW` (bool) | cache mode |

- **GET**: `loadUrl(url + "?" + params)` (`:1053` / `:1060`).
- **POST**: `postUrl(url, params.getBytes())` (`:1049` / `:1087`) — body is the
  form-encoded param string.
- When `IS_WEB_DEFAULT_PARAMETER` is true, params are prefixed with
  **`g.COMMON_PARAMETER = "Device=AD&Version=250601003&Key=korail1234567890"`**
  (`K4/g.java:11`, appended at `:1032` / `:1074`) — a hardcoded API key + version sent to
  every such WebView URL. (The Python client stores the same triple in
  `constants.py:2-4`.)

**SRT cross-booking (manual JSON POST).** For `IS_SRT_WEB_RESERVE` +
`WEB_POST_JSON_BODY`, the app bypasses the WebView and issues a manual
`HttpURLConnection` POST (`S0()`, `BaseWebViewActivity.java:831-893`) with
`Content-Type: application/json; charset=utf-8` and
`Accept: text/html,application/json,*/*`, then renders the response via
`loadDataWithBaseURL` / `loadUrl` (`Q0()`, `:808-823`). Related rewrites:

- `Y0()` (`:921-926`) rewrites `http`→`https` for any `*.srail.kr` URL; `b1()` (`:956`)
  does the same specifically for `teapp.srail.kr` / `app.srail.kr`.
- `U0()` (`:997-1006`) force-upgrades in-WebView `srail.kr` navigations to HTTPS.
- `f1()` (`:959-974`) copies `Set-Cookie` headers from the manual POST into the WebView
  `CookieManager`.

**Fixed WebView URLs** (`K4/g.java`): `TRAIN_MAP_URL = https://gis.korail.com/korailTalk/entrance`
(`:83`, loaded at `BaseWebViewActivity.java:804` / `:896` with an ~8 s watchdog reload
`T0()`/`O0()`); content pages `FIND_ALL_URL` (`https://www.korail.com/ticket/membership/findMember`,
`:16`), `PRIVACY_URL` (`https://info.korail.com/info/contents.do?key=2812`, `:59`),
`N_CARD_*_URL` (`https://smart.letskorail.com/returninfo/html/…`, `:54-56`),
`TRIP_QR_AUTH_*_URL` (`https://smart.letskorail.com/…`, `:84-85`); limousine pages point at
`blog.naver.com/korailblog/` (`:30-31`).

**WebView host constants** (`K4/g.java`): `HOST_DEV = mobiledev.letskorail.com` (`:20`),
`HOST_REAL = smart.letskorail.com` (`:21`), `HOST_REAL_MULTI_LANG = www.korail.com`
(`:22`), `HOST_REAL_MULTI_LANG_HOST_TEST = dev5.letskorail.com` (`:23`),
`HOST_STAGING = dev2.letskorail.com` (`:24`), `HOST_TEST = dev3.letskorail.com` (`:25`).

### 6.4 In-WebView custom-scheme routing

Central policy is `S4/C0804d.isShouldOverrideUrlLoading` (`C0804d.java:120-137`), invoked
from `BaseWebViewActivity.e.shouldOverrideUrlLoading` (`:693-716`):

- `javascript:` / `about:blank` / `http` / `https` → pass through (load in WebView).
- `tel:` → `callPhone`.
- `market:` → Google Play.
- `intent:` → `S4.G.playApp`.
- `korailtalk://stnLeadNavi?stnCd=<code>` → launch external station-guide app via the
  `korail_app_link://userservice` scheme, targeting package
  `com.korail.futuretech.userservice` (`C0804d.java:133-134`).

**`IntegrationWebViewActivity`** adds extra in-WebView `korailtalk://` authority handling
(`IntegrationWebViewActivity.java:48-106`):

| In-WebView URL | Params | Action | Source |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `korailtalk://productTrainSearch` | `trnGpCd,type,startStation,endStation,jobDv` | `TourTrainInfoDao` → `DiscountTourTrainBookingActivity` | `:61,131-142` |
| `korailtalk://payment` | `strVrRsNo,strGdSqno` | `ProductPaymentCheckDao` → `PaymentActivity` | `:63,120-128` |
| `korailtalk://login` | – | `C0815o.moveToLogin` | `:65` |
| `korailtalk://supermove` | `txtGoStart,txtGoEnd,txtGoAbrdDt,txtGoHour,txtPsgFlg_1..6,txtSeatAttCd_2..4,selGotrain,trnGpCd,txtMenuId` | `IntroActivity` carrying SRT search params (SRT→KTX cross-search) | `:67-92` |

It also forwards `tmobileid://` and `mobileid://` schemes to an external `VIEW` intent
(`:93-102`).

**OnePass WebView flow.** The OnePass return scheme is `korailtalk://certify`
(`korailtalk_scheme_certify`, strings.xml, matched by `S4/u.isOnepassScheme`
`u.java:134-136`). Handled in `OnepassWebViewActivity.c.shouldOverrideUrlLoading`
(`:58-99`) and `BaseWebViewActivity.f` (`:719-763`): on match it returns the `userKey`
query param to the caller as an Intent extra and finishes. Both also special-case package
`kr.go.gfido.m` (government FIDO) via `Intent.parseUri`, route `intent://` via `G.playApp`,
and the base `f` class references `letskorail.com/happy_talk/initChat.html` (chat).

### 6.5 Manifest intent-filters (inbound deep links)

All app-owned custom-scheme filters use scheme `korailtalk`:

| Deep link | Target activity | Exported / launchMode | Source |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `korailtalk://navigation` | `NavigationActivity` | exported, `singleTask` | `AndroidManifest.xml:180-187` |
| `korailtalk://member_info` | `DataActivity` | exported, `singleInstance` | `:188-195` |
| `korailtalk://approve` | `PaymentActivity` | exported, `singleTask` | `:196-203` |
| `korailtalk://railpluscardinfo` | `RailPlusActivity` | exported | `:206-213` |
| `kakao{kakao_app_key}://oauth` | Kakao `AuthCodeHandlerActivity` | Kakao SDK | `:130-137` |
| `naver3rdpartylogin://authorize` (path `/`) | Naver `OAuthCustomTabActivity` | Naver SDK | `:307-314` |

The Kakao row is written as a rule, not a literal: the SDK's callback scheme is the
fixed prefix `kakao` concatenated with the app's Kakao app key
(`res/values/strings.xml` `kakao_app_key`). That key is the app vendor's credential,
is unused by this client, and was stripped from this repository and its git history —
so the scheme cannot be, and need not be, spelled out here.

The `<queries>` block also declares verify-action schemes `mobileid` and `tmobileid`
(`:51-57`) and an `https` `BROWSABLE` probe (`:61-65`).

#### 6.5.1 `korailtalk://navigation` — unauthenticated deep-link dispatcher

`NavigationActivity` (`scheme/NavigationActivity.java`) reads the `?view=` query param and
routes to one of ~20 activities via `S4/y.getClassNm` (`y.java:26-94`); all other query
params are copied into the launch `Bundle` (`y.getNavigationBundle` `:96-104`). This
activity is **exported and unauthenticated** — any app or web page can open
`korailtalk://navigation?view=…` to deep-link into booking / ticket / mileage / coupon
screens.

`?view` → Activity map (`S4/y.java:26-94`):

| `view` value | Activity |
| --- | --- |
| `booking` | `MainBookingActivity` |
| `ticket` | `TicketListActivity` |
| `memberCard` | `MemberCardActivity` |
| `web` | `IntegrationWebViewActivity` |
| `discountCoupon` | `DiscountCouponActivity` |
| `delayDiscountCoupon` | `DelayDiscountCouponActivity` |
| `mileage` | `MileageHistoryActivity` |
| `seasonTicket` | `CommutationBookingActivity` |
| `periodSeasonTicket` | `PeriodCommutationBookingActivity` |
| `reservation` | `BixbyReservationActivity` |
| `discountMenuList` | `NewDiscountMenuActivity` |
| `tripMenuList` | `NewTripMenuActivity` |
| `pushHistory` | `PushHistoryActivity` |
| `bookedTicket` | `ReservedTicketActivity` |
| `bookedTrip` | `TripBookingListActivity` |
| `offlineTikcetReturn` *(sic)* | `OfflineTicketReturnActivity` |
| `accumulatingMilege` / `tourReserved` / `ticketRefund` / `offlineTicketRefund` / `togetherMileage` / `tourMenuList` | `AccumulatingKTXMileageActivity` |
| `mainBannerStation_Init` | `MainBookingActivity` |
| *(any other)* | `null`; remaining params → `Bundle` via `getNavigationBundle` |

#### 6.5.2 `korailtalk://member_info` — credential export (SSO handoff)

`DataActivity` (`scheme/DataActivity.java`) reads the stored login prefs
(`KEY_LOGIN_TYPE` / `KEY_LOGIN_ID` / `KEY_LOGIN_PW`, `KEY_AUTO_LOGIN`, `KEY_MEMBER_NUM`),
**decrypts** the id/pw (`F4.a.decryptAES`), packages them as JSON, base64- and
server-encrypts them (`EncryptDao`), then **broadcasts the credentials out** via a `VIEW`
intent to `korailtalklite://member_info?value=<encValue>`
(`data_login_scheme`, `strings.xml:495`, `sendLoginData()` `:78-84`). This is an
app-to-app auto-login (SSO) handoff to a companion **`korailtalklite`** app. Because this
exported `singleInstance` activity exposes an auto-login credential export path, it is a
notable attack surface.

#### 6.5.3 `korailtalk://approve` & `korailtalk://railpluscardinfo` — payment return channels

- `korailtalk://approve` → `PaymentActivity` is the return channel for external payment
  apps. Matched by `S4/D.isApproveScheme` (`D.java:193-194`:
  `scheme + "://" + authority == "korailtalk://approve"`). `PaymentActivity.onNewIntent`
  (`:665-683`) copies all query params into a `Bundle` (special-casing `otcNo`: space→`+`)
  and forwards to the easy-payment fragment.
- `korailtalk://railpluscardinfo` → `RailPlusActivity.onNewIntent` (`:177-195`) reads
  `RET_CODE` (`000000` = success), `RET_MSG`, and query params `BALANCE` / `CARD_NO`
  (`w0()` `:112` / `x0()` `:116`).

payment / partner hand-off:

### 6.8 Security notes

- **Exported, unauthenticated deep links**: `korailtalk://navigation` is a broad
  dispatcher into ~20 activities; `korailtalk://member_info` exports decrypted-then-
  re-encrypted auto-login credentials to a companion app; `korailtalk://approve` and
  `korailtalk://railpluscardinfo` are exported payment/return channels. All are declared
  exported in the manifest (§6.5).
- **Web-controlled native actions**: the `korailtalk` bridge lets page JS initiate payment
  (`goPayment`/`goMaasPayment`), return auth/identity results, and choose the JS callback
  function the app invokes on it (`K0()`).
- **Permissive WebView**: mixed content always allowed, geolocation auto-granted,
  third-party cookies accepted, multiple windows enabled (§6.2).
- **Hardcoded API key**: `COMMON_PARAMETER` embeds `Key=korail1234567890` and
  `Version=250601003`, appended to WebView URLs and mirrored by the Python client
  (`constants.py:2-4`).
- **Third-party bridge reach**: the vendored `m2uWebViewNative` bridge exposes the device
  phone number and microphone control to web content.

> Obfuscation note: bridge/client classes are single-letter obfuscated (bridge inner class
> `c`, `WebViewClient` `e`/`f`, `WebChromeClient` `a`/`d`; helpers `S4.C0804d`, `S4.u`,
> `S4.y`, `S4.D`, `S4.G`; MaumAI V2 controller `t5.c` with inner `C0414c`). The
> `@JavascriptInterface` method names are **not** obfuscated because they must match the
> web contract, so the JS API surface is fully readable.


## 7. Security & anti-tamper

This section covers the Korail app's (`com.korail.talk` v6.5.0) transport security, session handling, the DynaPath anti-macro/request-integrity mechanism, root/emulator/debugger/hook detection, the NetFunnel virtual-waiting-room queue, and the app's obfuscation posture. The overall picture is that Korail delegates nearly all of its anti-tamper and request-integrity to a commercial third-party SDK (DynaPath by Scripters); the app's own code contributes almost no in-house security controls, and its own API traffic is **not** certificate-pinned.

### 7.1 Networking stack summary

The app runs two distinct HTTP stacks:

- **Korail API traffic** — the legacy Square **Retrofit v1** (`retrofit.RestAdapter` + `GsonConverter`) over a `UrlConnectionClient` transport (i.e. `java.net.HttpURLConnection`), with 60 s connect and 60 s read timeouts. Requests execute on an `AsyncTask`. Endpoint host resolves from `S4.z.getSSLHost()`, which returns `https://smart.letskorail.com` in production (server type `REAL`, selected by `K4.g.SERVER_TYPE = EnumC5607a.fromCode("3")`).
  - `analysis/jadx/sources/com/korail/talk/network/ExecuteDao.java:18-61`
  - `analysis/jadx/sources/com/korail/talk/network/BaseDaoHelper.java:24-97`
  - `analysis/jadx/sources/S4/z.java:46-49`
  - `analysis/jadx/sources/K4/g.java:74,89-91`
- **NetFunnel queue traffic** — a separate stack using Apache `DefaultHttpClient` (`U6.a`), used only by the virtual-waiting-room subsystem (see 7.6).
  - `analysis/jadx/sources/U6/a.java`

### 7.2 TLS / certificate pinning posture

**There is NO SSL/certificate pinning on Korail's own API traffic.**

- The network security config declares **no** `<pin-set>`. It only *enables cleartext* for a handful of non-Korail domains (a bot-detection LB and the SRT companion hosts): `1.255.59.22`, `bot-dev-lb-...naverncp.com`, `teapp.srail.kr`, `app.srail.kr`. No Korail host is pinned or otherwise constrained.
  - `analysis/apktool/res/xml/network_security_config.xml:1-10`
- The Retrofit v1 / `HttpURLConnection` stack installs **no** custom `TrustManager`, `HostnameVerifier`, or `SSLSocketFactory`; `ExecuteDao` only overrides `openConnection()` to set timeouts and (conditionally) attach the DynaPath header.
  - `analysis/jadx/sources/com/korail/talk/network/ExecuteDao.java:19-56`
- OkHttp3's `CertificatePinner` is present **only inside bundled third-party libraries** (obfuscated class `a8.k`, used by Kakao/Firebase SDKs) and is not wired to Korail API calls. No `com/korail/` class references `CertificatePinner`, `X509TrustManager`, `HostnameVerifier`, or `setSSLSocketFactory`.

**Consequence:** Korail API traffic is interceptable by a system- or user-installed CA proxy; TLS provides confidentiality against passive eavesdroppers but no pinning-based protection against active MITM with a trusted CA.

> Note: an earlier internal note mis-identified this pinner class as `X7.C0891h`. Verified correct identifier is `a8.k`; `X7/C0891h.java` contains no `CertificatePinner` reference. The substantive conclusion (pinner lives only in bundled libs, unused by Korail traffic) is unchanged.

### 7.3 Session / auth transport

- Session is a **`JSESSIONID` cookie**, managed by `java.net.CookieManager` with `CookiePolicy.ACCEPT_ALL`, installed globally via `CookieHandler.setDefault(...)`. The cookie is read back in `KTApplication.getCookie()`.
  - `analysis/jadx/sources/com/korail/talk/application/KTApplication.java:72-116`
- `http.keepAlive` is disabled application-wide (`System.setProperty("http.keepAlive","false")`).
  - `analysis/jadx/sources/com/korail/talk/application/KTApplication.java:92-94`
- **No HMAC / nonce request-signing** is applied to ordinary API calls. The DynaPath token (7.3, only on 6 sensitive paths) is the *sole* request-integrity mechanism observed on the wire.

### 7.4 DynaPath anti-macro / request-integrity SDK

The app embeds the **DynaPath Mobile SDK** (`kr.scripters.dynapath`, a commercial anti-bot / anti-macro product by Scripters). It is the app's primary integrity and anti-automation control.

**Lifecycle & gating**

- Initialized once in `IntroActivity.onCreate()` via `DynaPathMobileSDK.Companion.initialize(getApplication())`.
  - `analysis/jadx/sources/com/korail/talk/ui/intro/IntroActivity.java:584`
- On each outbound request, **only when `I4.a.IS_MACRO_ACTIVE == true`**, `ExecuteDao` matches the request URL against a fixed allowlist of **6 sensitive paths**. If matched, it calls `DynaPathMobileSDK.Companion.generate()` and attaches the result as HTTP request header **`x-dynapath-m-token`**. If token generation throws `DynaPathException`, it is swallowed (logged only) and the request proceeds without the header.
  - `analysis/jadx/sources/com/korail/talk/network/ExecuteDao.java:26-52`

  The 6 gated paths (exact strings):
  - `/classes/com.korail.mobile.certification.TicketReservation`
  - `/classes/com.korail.mobile.nonMember.NonMemTicket`
  - `/classes/com.korail.mobile.seatMovie.ScheduleView`
  - `/classes/com.korail.mobile.seatMovie.ScheduleViewSpecial`
  - `/classes/com.korail.mobile.trn.prcFare.do`
  - `/classes/com.korail.mobile.login.Login`

**Remote toggle**

- `I4.a.IS_MACRO_ACTIVE` defaults to `false`.
  - `analysis/jadx/sources/I4/a.java:14`
- It is set at runtime from the **CommonCode** server response: `IntroActivity` sets it from `commonCodeResponse.getData().getIsMacroEnable() == "Y"` (default `"N"`). So DynaPath enforcement is **server-controlled** and can be turned on/off without a client update.
  - `analysis/jadx/sources/com/korail/talk/ui/intro/IntroActivity.java:663`

**Server enforcement**

- On an **HTTP 403 Forbidden** whose response carries header **`DynaPath-Result` < 0**, `BaseDaoHelper` reads the JSON body's `message` field and surfaces a macro-block dialog via `setMacroShowDialog(...)`. This is how the server rejects bot/invalid requests.
  - `analysis/jadx/sources/com/korail/talk/network/BaseDaoHelper.java:50-92`

**Token payload (`b.C1229b.a()`)**

The token is a URL-encoded `k=v&k=v` string of device / tamper telemetry, then wrapped by a custom base-N / salted encoder (`l1.AbstractC5980b.a`, with a random 4-char salt drawn from `[a-zA-Z0-9]`) so the header is opaque on the wire.

- `analysis/jadx/sources/b/C1229b.java:88-247`
- Inputs assembled in `b/AbstractC1228a.java`; signing-cert SHA-256 in `l1/AbstractC5987i.java`.

| Field | Meaning | Source |
|---|---|---|
| `ai` | package name | `b/AbstractC1228a.java` |
| `di` | `Settings.Secure` android_id (device id) | `b/AbstractC1228a.java` |
| `as` | APK signing-cert SHA-256, first 32 hex chars (bracket-wrapped on wire, e.g. `[<32hex>]`) | `l1/AbstractC5987i.java` |
| `su` | root detected (bool) | `l1.AbstractC5981c.b` |
| `dbg` | debugger detected (bool) | `l1.AbstractC5981c.a` |
| `emu` | emulator detected (bool) | `l1.AbstractC5981c.a`/`b` |
| `hk` | hooking (Frida/Xposed) detected (bool) | `l1.AbstractC5981c.c` |
| `it` | init timestamp (ms) | `b/C1229b.java:113` |
| `ts` | current timestamp (ms) | `b/C1229b.java:114-117` |
| `rt` | up to 5 inter-`generate()` timing deltas (bot-timing heuristic) | `b/C1229b.java:76-91,119-127` |
| `os` | `Build.VERSION.RELEASE` | `b/C1229b.java:128-131` |
| `dm` | `Build.MODEL` | `b/C1229b.java:132-135` |
| `st` | literal `"Android"` | `b/C1229b.java:136` |
| `sv` | SDK version, literal `"v1.0.3"` | `b/C1229b.java:137` |

> The `as` value is bracket-wrapped because `DynaPathMobileSDK` passes `AbstractC5987i.a(ctx).toString()` (an `ArrayList`), yielding e.g. `[<32hex>]` on the wire. The per-cert truncation to the first 32 hex chars (`substring(0,32)`) is in `l1/AbstractC5987i.java`.

### 7.5 Root / emulator / debugger / hook detection

All environment/tamper detection lives **inside the DynaPath SDK** (`l1.AbstractC5981c`), and its boolean outputs feed the token fields above. There is **no** equivalent detection in Korail's own code.

- `analysis/jadx/sources/l1/AbstractC5981c.java`

| Check | Method | What it inspects |
|---|---|---|
| **Root** | `b(Context)` | 10 su-binary paths (`/system/xbin/su`, `/sbin/su`, `/system/bin/su`, `/system/app/Superuser.apk`, `/data/local/su`, `/su/bin/su`, `/data/local/xbin/su`, `/data/local/bin/su`, `/system/sd/xbin/su`, `/system/bin/failsafe/su`); 12 root-manager packages via `getPackageInfo` (`com.topjohnwu.magisk`, `eu.chainfire.supersu`, `com.koushikdutta.superuser`, `com.kingroot.kinguser`, `com.noshufou.android.su`(`.elite`), `com.thirdparty.superuser`, `com.yellowes.su`, `com.kingo.root`, `com.smedialink.oneclickroot`, `com.zhiqupk.root.global`, `com.alephzain.framaroot`); and `Build.TAGS` containing `test-keys`. |
| **Debugger** | `a(Context)` | `ApplicationInfo` `FLAG_DEBUGGABLE` \| `Debug.isDebuggerConnected()` \| `Debug.waitingForDebugger()` |
| **Anti-hook** | `c()` | scans the current thread's stack-trace class names (lowercased) for `xposed` / `lsposed` / `edxposed` / `frida` |
| **Emulator** | `a()` | `Build.MODEL` (`google_sdk`, `Emulator`, `Android SDK built for x86`), `MANUFACTURER` (`Genymotion`), `HARDWARE` (`goldfish`, `vbox86`, `vbox86p`, `ranchu`), `PRODUCT` (`sdk`, `google_sdk`, `sdk_x86`, `vbox86p`), `BOARD` (`goldfish`) |
| **Emulator/QEMU** | `b()` (SDK ≥ 28 only) | device-node/lib files: `/dev/socket/qemud`, `/dev/qemu_pipe`, `/system/lib/libc_malloc_debug_qemu.so`, `/sys/qemu_trace`, `/system/bin/qemu-props`, `/dev/socket/genyd`, `/dev/socket/baseband_genyd` |

> This materially corrects the older agent report `docs/deep-dive/agent-reports/16-antiautomation-queue-security.md` (lines 15, 152-161), which stated that runtime root/su/Magisk/Xposed/Frida/emulator detection was "not observed" and that only manifest `<queries>` declarations existed. `l1.AbstractC5981c` genuinely implements all of it, wired into the DynaPath token.

### 7.6 App-level anti-tamper (Korail's own code)

App-level anti-tamper written by Korail (in `com.korail.talk`) is **minimal** — essentially everything is delegated to DynaPath.

- The only in-app environment check is `IntroActivity.H0()`: `Build.FINGERPRINT.contains("generic")`, but it is gated behind `IS_DEBUG_LOG` (dev-only, not a production emulator gate), together with `o0()` which is merely a Google Play Services availability check (not a security control).
  - `analysis/jadx/sources/com/korail/talk/ui/intro/IntroActivity.java:292-321`
- `VersionActivity` has a hidden easter-egg (`v0()`): after tapping 10× (`i % 10 == 9`) it `Toast`s both the `versionCode` **and** `g.SERVER_TYPE.name()`. This exposes build/server info but is not a security control.
  - `analysis/jadx/sources/com/korail/talk/ui/setting/VersionActivity.java:72`
- No standalone in-app root / Frida / signature-verification logic exists.

### 7.7 NetFunnel virtual waiting room (queue / anti-surge)

The queue / anti-surge system is **STCLab NetFunnel**. It is a *load/queue gate*, not an integrity control — its requests carry **no signing**.

- Client core is the obfuscated package `T6` (`T6.g` singleton; protocol driver `T6.d`; HTTP via Apache `DefaultHttpClient` in `U6.a`).
- **Runtime server config** is set in `KTApplication.g()`: protocol `https` (Kakao `Constants.SCHEME`), host `nf.letskorail.com`, port `443` (`U.DEFAULT_PORT_SSL`), serviceID `service_1`, actionID `act_8`, timeout `3 s`. Path/query is `ts.wseq`.
  - `analysis/jadx/sources/com/korail/talk/application/KTApplication.java:78-86`
- **Hardcoded fallback default** (`T6.h`): `http://nf2.netfunnel.co.kr:80/ts.wseq`, serviceID `service_1`, actionID `act_1`, timeout `3 s`, retry `2`, maxTTL `30`, `notupdate_waitcount_bypass_limit=100`, `checktime=5`.
  - `analysis/jadx/sources/T6/h.java:9-58`

**Flow:** `GET_TID_CHK_ENTER (5101)` obtains a queue TID `key` → poll `CHK_ENTER (5002)` on a TTL loop while showing wait UI → `ALIVE_NOTICE (5003)` keep-alive → `SET_COMPLETE (5004)` on entry. A bypass safeguard exists for error / IP-block and stalled wait-count cases.
  - `analysis/jadx/sources/T6/d.java`, `analysis/jadx/sources/T6/g.java:224-251,313-356`

**Opcode catalog (`T6.c`):** `None=0`, `CHK_ENTER=5002`, `ALIVE_NOTICE=5003`, `SET_COMPLETE=5004`, `GET_TID_CHK_ENTER=5101`, `INIT=5105`, `STOP=5106`.
  - `analysis/jadx/sources/T6/c.java:4-11`

**Action-ID catalog (`K4.g`, serviceID `service_1`):** `act_8` (booking/simple-purchase, default), `act_8_2` (peak-season booking), `act_6` (product), `act_14` (reserve), `act_18` (pay), `act_21` (reserved-list), `act_22` (refund), `act_4` (test); default in `T6.h` is `act_1`.
  - `analysis/jadx/sources/K4/g.java:43-51`

**Call sites:** booking (`MainBookingActivity:759`, `OldMainBookingActivity:331`), direct inquiry (`DirectInquiryActivity:442/469/499`), reserved-ticket list (`ReservedTicketActivity:553`).

**Response fields (`T6.i.Parser`, format `code:k=v&k=v`):** leading int = status code (see `T6.a`), `key` (queue TID), `utime`, `ip` (redirect host), `port` (redirect port), `ttl` (poll interval s), `tps`, `nwait` (people ahead), `nnext`.
  - `analysis/jadx/sources/T6/i.java:35-69`

**Status/event codes (`T6.a`, partial):** `Success=200`, `Continue=201`, `ContinueDebug=202`, `TsBypass=300`, `TsBlock=301`, `TsIpBlock=302`, `TsExpressNumber=303`, `Wait=102`, `Stopping=111`, `ErrorAuth=900`, `ErrorSecurity=996`, `ErrorSystem=999` (full enum ≈ 60 codes).
  - `analysis/jadx/sources/T6/a.java`

### 7.8 Obfuscation posture

- The app is **R8 / ProGuard-minified**: most of Korail's own support classes and all bundled security / NetFunnel code carry single/short obfuscated package+class names — e.g. `I4.a` = `KTConst`, `S4.z` = host resolver, `T6.g` = NetFunnel, `U6.a` = HTTP client, `b.C1229b` = DynaPath token, `l1.AbstractC5981c` = tamper checks.
- **No general runtime string-decryption routine** was found in `com.korail.talk` itself. The only bespoke string obfuscation is the DynaPath token encoder (`b`/`l1.AbstractC5980b`).
- Debug logging is compile-gated by `G4.a.IS_DEBUG_LOG` (surfaced as `I4.a.IS_DEBUG_LOG`), so verbose network logging is off in production builds.
  - `analysis/jadx/sources/I4/a.java:7`

### 7.9 Endpoint catalog

#### Korail API base (Retrofit v1 + HttpURLConnection, conditional DynaPath header)

| Name | Method | Path | Key params / headers | Response | Auth | Source |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| Korail API base | GET/POST (Retrofit v1) | per-DAO service interfaces under `https://smart.letskorail.com` (prod) | Header `x-dynapath-m-token: <opaque>` — **only** when `IS_MACRO_ACTIVE==true` AND URL matches one of the 6 sensitive paths | per-DAO models; on bot/invalid: HTTP 403 + header `DynaPath-Result` < 0 + JSON `message` | `JSESSIONID` cookie (`CookieManager` `ACCEPT_ALL`); **no cert pinning, no custom TrustManager** | `com/korail/talk/network/ExecuteDao.java:18-61`; `BaseDaoHelper.java:50-92`; `S4/z.java:46-49` |

TLS/HTTPS only, no pinning. The DynaPath token (only on the 6 gated paths) is the sole request-integrity control; ordinary calls have no HMAC/nonce signing.

#### NetFunnel virtual waiting room (STCLab; Apache DefaultHttpClient)

Host in prod: `nf.letskorail.com:443` (`https`); hardcoded fallback default `nf2.netfunnel.co.kr:80` (`http`). No request signing on any of these.

| Name | Method | Path | Key params | Response | Auth | Source |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| GET_TID_CHK_ENTER (enter queue / obtain TID) | GET | `/ts.wseq` | `opcode=5101`, `sid=service_1`, `aid=<act_*>` | `NetFunnelResponse` (`T6.i`): `code:key=..&utime=..&ip=..&port=..&ttl=..&tps=..&nwait=..&nnext=..` | none (token issuance) | `T6/d.java:90-113` |
| CHK_ENTER (poll queue position) | GET | `/ts.wseq` (or `ip:port` from prior response) | `opcode=5002`, `key=<TID>` | `NetFunnelResponse` (`T6.i`) | queue key (TID) | `T6/d.java:45-67` |
| ALIVE_NOTICE (keep-alive) | GET | `/ts.wseq` | `opcode=5003`, `key=<TID>` | `NetFunnelResponse` (`T6.i`) | queue key (TID) | `T6/d.java:21-43` |
| SET_COMPLETE (release slot on entry) | GET | `/ts.wseq` | `opcode=5004`, `key=<TID>` | (ack) | queue key (TID) | `T6/d.java:69-88` |

CHK_ENTER is polled on a TTL loop until the status code is no longer `Continue(201)`/`ContinueDebug(202)`; a bypass safeguard triggers if the wait-count stops updating. SET_COMPLETE is invoked via `g.END()` when the guarded action completes (`NetfunnelDao.runRunner → T6.g.END`).


## 8. Coverage vs implemented client

This section cross-references the endpoints discovered by static APK analysis (Sections 1–7) against
what the Python client under `src/korail_mobile_api/`
actually implements and is *permitted* to call.

The client enforces coverage through two allowlists, so "implemented" means both a callable client
method **and** an entry in the runtime allowlist:

- **Route allowlist** — `KORAIL_READ_ONLY_ROUTES` (`src/korail_mobile_api/safety.py:22-108`). Every
  outbound request is checked by `assert_read_only_route()` (`safety.py:618-629`); any `(method, path)`
  not in the frozenset is rejected with `KorailProtocolError`.
- **Field allowlist** — `KORAIL_EXACT_REQUEST_FIELDS` / `..._FIELD_ORDERS` (`safety.py:112-526`),
  enforced by `assert_read_only_request_fields()` (`safety.py:632-699`).
- **Write-domain block** — `EXCLUDED_API_DOMAINS = {reservation, payment, refund, check-in,
  member-drop, push-sms, points-mileage, dynapath-token-generation}` (`safety.py:9-20`). Everything in
  these domains is deliberately *not* wired to an HTTP call.

Net result: the client is **read-only**. All state-changing endpoints from the catalog (booking,
payment, cancel/change, refund, check-in, mileage, gifting, member-drop, pass purchase) are absent by
design.
