### 8.5 Path / param divergences and notable observations

1. **`logout()` does not hit the network.** The discovered logout is `GET
   /classes/com.korail.mobile.login.Logout`, but `KorailClient.logout()` (`client.py:222-223`) only
   calls `clear_session()` (`client.py:219-220`) to drop the local cookie jar. The server-side
   `Logout` route is neither in `KORAIL_READ_ONLY_ROUTES` nor called anywhere. Server session
   invalidation is therefore *not* performed.

2. **`ScheduleViewSpecial` (getRsvProductInquiry) is scaffolded but unreachable.** A form builder
   (`_build_product_train_inquiry_form`, `read_payloads.py:1215`) and a parser
   (`parse_product_train_inquiry_response`, `read_parsers.py:2139`) exist, and the path is listed in
   `DYNAPATH_ALLOWLIST_PATHS` (`constants.py:40`), yet there is **no client method** and **no
   `KORAIL_READ_ONLY_ROUTES` entry**. `assert_read_only_route()` would reject an actual call. This is
   dead/partial coverage.

3. **Booking/cancel mutation helpers are schema-only, never sent.** `build_single_adult_reservation_form`
   (`mutation_payloads.py:45`) and `build_unpaid_reservation_cancel_form` (`mutation_payloads.py:169`),
   plus parsers `parse_reservation_hold_response` / `parse_reservation_payment_response`
   (`mutation_parsers.py`), exist and `TicketReservation` + `NonMemTicket` are in
   `DYNAPATH_ALLOWLIST_PATHS` (`constants.py:37-38`). None are wired to `http.post_form`; the
   `reservation`/`payment` domains are in `EXCLUDED_API_DOMAINS`. Consistent with `SAFETY_DEFAULTS`
   (`safety.py:705-711`): booking/payment default to disabled / dry-run only.

4. **`DYNAPATH_ALLOWLIST_PATHS` ⊋ callable routes.** The DynaPath token allowlist (`constants.py:35-44`)
   lists 6 paths, but only 3 (`ScheduleView`, `trn.prcFare.do`, `login.Login`) are also in
   `KORAIL_READ_ONLY_ROUTES`. The other 3 (`TicketReservation`, `NonMemTicket`, `ScheduleViewSpecial`)
   have token support prepared but no callable route — see items 2 and 3.

5. **`actualTrainSchedule.do` omits `Key` — client matches.** The catalog notes this endpoint uniquely
   sends only `Device`/`Version` (no `Key`, no `Sid`). `build_train_schedule_form` (`payloads.py:205-215`)
   correctly emits exactly `{Device, Version, runDt, trnNo}` and the call sets `include_common=False`
   (`client.py:1024`). No divergence.

6. **`getMaasCancel` path has a double slash** in the catalog
   (`/classes//com.korail.mobile.addService.cancelPay.do`). Irrelevant to the client since the endpoint
   is a write and not implemented, but noted for catalog fidelity.

7. **`ReservationList` (GET) is used for three discovered flows** (inquiryTicketRsv, applyDisability,
   cert lookup) — none implemented. Note this is the *certification* `ReservationList`, distinct from the
   implemented *product* `ReservationList` (`client.py:534`) and *reservation* `ReservationView`
   (`client.py:593`); the client covers the latter two only.

8. **Field-level lock-down on implemented reads.** Beyond route allowlisting, 30+ implemented paths have
   an exact field contract in `KORAIL_EXACT_REQUEST_FIELDS` (`safety.py:112-438`), and several also pin
   field *order* (`KORAIL_EXACT_REQUEST_FIELD_ORDERS`, `safety.py:441-526`), including bespoke validators
   for the repeated-field shapes of `cmtrInfo.do`, `tk.pbpAcepSpec.do`, and `tk.plfNo.do`
   (`safety.py:537-593`). Requests that add, drop, or reorder fields are rejected even on allowlisted
   routes.


## 9. Discrepancies & open questions

This section reconciles the present analysis against the pre-existing repository
documentation under [`docs/`](../). It is organized as: (9.1) what this analysis
adds that the docs did **not** already contain; (9.2) two places where the
analysis directly **contradicted** the docs and the docs turned out to be
right; (9.3) factual **corrections folded in** during verification; (9.4)
recurring **cosmetic / non-material** drift; and a final, clearly-fenced
`⚠️ Unverified / low-confidence` subsection. Every load-bearing claim below was
re-checked against the decompiled source; items that could not be confirmed to
the literal value are quarantined in 9.5 and are **never** stated as fact.

### 9.1 What is NEW vs already-documented

Across all 24 dimensions the endpoint-level catalog (paths, HTTP verbs, `@Field`
/ `@Query` names, request/response DAO classes) was found to be **already
documented** and **consistent** with `docs/api-endpoints.md`,
`docs/api-status-by-service.md`, `docs/deep-dive/api-contracts.md`,
`docs/deep-dive/network-model-fields.md`, and the per-domain agent reports
(03–17). The analysis is, for the endpoint tables, a faithful and
higher-fidelity re-derivation rather than new discovery. The genuinely **new**
material is infrastructure/behavioral depth and dead-code determinations:

| Area | New vs docs | Source |
| --- | --- | --- |
| Login crypto chain | AES/CBC/PKCS5 password path `pwdAESCphd=='Y' → F4.a.encryptBase64(C0812l.encryptAES(key,str))`; else `encryptBase64(str)` | `BaseActivity.java:145-161`, `k5/b.java:124-140`, `S4/C0812l.java` |
| Anti-macro token | DynaPath `x-dynapath-m-token` flow, 6-URL allowlist, gated by **both** `a.IS_MACRO_ACTIVE` and list membership | `ExecuteDao.java:26-27,45`, `I4/a.java:14` |
| Session propagation | `JSESSIONID` via `CookieManager` (`ACCEPT_ALL`) bridged into WebView; `http.keepAlive=false` | `KTApplication.java:73-75,92-94` |
| nFilter | Explicit **dead-code** verdict: `getPublicKey()` has zero callers; `NFilterCreateKeyDao`/`NFilterService` referenced only within the nFilter package | (call-graph grep, self-referential smali only) |
| Tamper detection | Root/su/Frida/emulator detection **is** implemented (see 9.2) — corrects doc 16 which said "not observed" | `l1/AbstractC5981c.java`, `DynaPathMobileSDK.initialize` |
| Pass-payment | Two additive endpoints not surfaced in reports 07/08 (table below) | `PassService.java:19,39` |
| Payment WebView gap | `stbkAcntStlR.do` is a WebView GET, not a Retrofit POST (see 9.3) | `B6/AbstractC1269e.java:171,284` |
| WebView flows | Onepass `certify`/`userKey` return, `NavigationActivity ?view→Activity` map, `DataActivity korailtalklite://` SSO credential export, MaumAI V2 second `korailtalk` bridge | `docs/deep-dive/agent-reports/13` complements |
| Travel products | WebView POST-body composition (`COMMON_PARAMETER` + per-URL `POST_DATA`), `qry_dv_val=B/M/E` receipt distinction, `EnumC5608b` obfuscation | `K4/g.java`, ProductService callers |
| Account integration | Response-replay for account linkage, `poppCfmRec.do` side-channel, social-linkage readback via `KorailPointInquiryDao.h_logn_tp_cd1/2/4/5` | `S4/u.java`, `KorailPointInquiryDao.java` |

**Newly surfaced / gap endpoints** (present in source, additive vs reports 07/08):

| Name | Method | Path | Key params | Response | Auth | Source |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| commPayment | POST | `/classes/com.korail.mobile.pass.passPayIssue` | Device, Version, Key, hidPayAmount, FieldMap, FieldMap | `CommPaymentDao.CommPaymentResponse` | session (Key) | `PassService.java:19` |
| passPayment | POST | `/classes/com.korail.mobile.pass.passOtrPayIssue` | Device, Version, Key, hidPayAmount, h_rcvd_prc, hidWctNo, FieldMap, FieldMap | `PassPaymentDao.PassPaymentResponse` | session (Key) | `PassService.java:39` |
| stbkAcntStlR (내통장결제) | GET (WebView, UI-assembled) | `{webHost}/classes/com.korail.mobile.pay.stbkAcntStlR.do?{COMMON_PARAMETER}&trPrice={amount}` | COMMON_PARAMETER, trPrice | WebView page (no Retrofit DTO) | session cookie | `B6/AbstractC1269e.java:171,284` |

> The `stbkAcntStlR.do` handoff is documented in
> [`agent-reports/08-pay-providers.md:82,90,139`](agent-reports/08-pay-providers.md).
> It qualifies the analysis's blanket "every payment endpoint is
> `@FormUrlEncoded` POST except two `@Query` GETs" — this one is neither; it is a
> WebView URL, not a `PayService` method.

### 9.2 Contradictions with existing docs (docs were right)

Two claims in the analysis under review directly contradicted the repository
docs. In both cases re-verification against source shows the **docs are
correct** and the analysis was wrong. These are recorded here so the errors are
not silently propagated.

1. **Reservation cancel two-step ordering was INVERTED.** The analysis stated
   `ReservationCancelChk` is a pre-flight eligibility check called *first*,
   followed by `ReservationCancel`. The code proves the opposite order. In
   `DReservationConfirmActivity.onReceive` (lines 408–437) the `ReservationCancel`
   response (`f.dao_rsv_cancel`) arrives **first** and raises the
   `common_reservation_cancel_message` "do you want to cancel?" dialog; on
   confirm, `G0()` (138–142) → `D0()` (118–127) fires `ReservationCancelChk`,
   whose response (`f.dao_rsv_cancel_check`, line 423) then shows
   `common_reservation_cancel_complete_message`. The same pattern appears in
   `ReservationWaitActivity` (250–264) and `LimousineActivity` (374+). Correct
   semantics: **`ReservationCancel` = step 1 (initiate), `ReservationCancelChk` =
   step 2 (confirm/complete)** — not an eligibility pre-check. This matches
   [`agent-reports/10-refund-delay-compensate-cancel.md:141-146`](agent-reports/10-refund-delay-compensate-cancel.md).

2. **`NETFUNNEL_ACTION_PRODUCT_ID="act_6"` does NOT have "zero references".**
   The analysis claimed `act_6` exists only at `K4/g.java:46` and is unused.
   It is in fact referenced at `b5/c.java:439` (selected when
   `this.f837Z instanceof ProductTrainInquiryRequest`) and passed to
   `g.BEGIN(NETFUNNEL_SERVER_ID, str, …)` at `b5/c.java:450`, i.e. wired to a
   **live** NetFunnel queue gate for the 관광열차/tour-train seat-inquiry flow
   (`ProductTrainInquiryDao`, launched from `DiscountGoodsBookingActivity`); the
   smali confirms `B5/c.smali:3227`. The correct, narrower statement is that
   `act_6` is *not* attached to the four `ProductService` travel-package DAOs
   (`ReservationList`/`Detail`/`payInfo`/`Cancel`). The docs already state this
   correctly at
   [`agent-reports/05-train-search-schedule.md:34`](agent-reports/05-train-search-schedule.md)
   and `docs/IMPLEMENTATION_PROGRESS.md:153`.

### 9.3 Corrections folded in

Material precision fixes applied during verification (facts changed, but the
load-bearing conclusion of each finding survived):

- **`poppCfmRec.do` (registerUserInfo) `notiTpCd` sent-values.** Trigger
  conditions (response `notiTpCd ∈ {MC, MM, MS}`) are correct, but the **sent**
  set is only `{MC, MS}`. In `S4/u.java` the `MM` branch (68–77) builds
  `new HashMap().put("notiTpCd","MM")` then **discards** that map and calls
  `loginPopUpData2.setExecuteData(map)` reusing the first map, which holds
  `"MC"` (line 59). So a `notiTpCd=="MM"` login response transmits `"MC"` (twice),
  never `"MM"`; the `'MM'` guard at `C0811k.java:50` is effectively dead code.
- **`encTotTxnAmt` is DOUBLE-Base64-wrapped.** `S4/C0812l.java:26-40`
  `getAmountEncrypt` returns `F4.a.encryptBase64(encryptAES(key, amount))` on the
  `'Y'` path, and `encryptAES` already Base64-encodes its AES output
  (`C0812l.java:23`). Wire value is `Base64(Base64(AES/CBC/PKCS5(amount)))` when
  `pwdAESCphd=='Y'`, else `Base64(amount)`. The analysis's `Base64(AES(...))`
  dropped the outer layer. (Mirrors `src/korail_mobile_api/crypto.py:transform_login_password`.)
- **IV derivation is char-based.** `encryptAES` uses `key.substring(0,16)` on
  **characters** then `.getBytes()` (`C0812l.java:19`) — identical to "first 16
  bytes" for ASCII keys, divergent for non-ASCII. Already flagged at
  [`full-api-analysis-2026-07-20.md:439`](../full-api-analysis-2026-07-20.md).
- **`CertificatePinner` class mis-identified.** Not `X7.C0891h`; the only
  `CertificatePinner` reference in jadx is `a8/k.java` (package `a8`, class `k`).
  The substantive claim stands and is verified: no `com/korail/` class references
  `CertificatePinner`/`X509TrustManager`/`HostnameVerifier`/`setSSLSocketFactory`
  — pinning lives only in bundled libs, none of it is wired.
- **Tamper detection is real (corrects doc 16).**
  [`agent-reports/16-antiautomation-queue-security.md:15,152-161`](agent-reports/16-antiautomation-queue-security.md)
  said root/su/Magisk/Xposed/Frida/emulator detection was "not observed".
  `l1/AbstractC5981c` implements it: `b()` checks 10 su paths + 12 root-manager
  packages + `Build.TAGS` test-keys; `a()` checks `FLAG_DEBUGGABLE` /
  `isDebuggerConnected` / `waitingForDebugger`; `c()` scans stack traces for
  xposed/lsposed/edxposed/frida; emulator/qemu/genymotion device-node checks
  (SDK ≥ 28) — all wired into the DynaPath token via `DynaPathMobileSDK.initialize`.
- **FileProvider path-config attribution corrected.** The manifest declares
  exactly **one** FileProvider (`androidx.core.content.FileProvider`, authority
  `com.korail.talk.fileprovider`, `AndroidManifest.xml:102`) whose
  `FILE_PROVIDER_PATHS` (`:103`) references **only** `@xml/provider_paths`.
  `res/xml/file_paths.xml` (legacy, referenced only in `R.java`) and
  `res/xml/image_share_filepaths.xml` (a Kakao SDK resource) are **not** path
  configs for the Korail provider.
- **`ReservationChangeResponse` is a list wrapper.** `ReservationChangeDao.java:151-160`:
  `private List<JrnyInfo> jrnyList` (getter `getJrnyList`); inner `JrnyInfo` holds
  one `String lumpStlTgtNo`. It is a `List`, not a bare object.
- **`PaymentMethod` does not implement `IPaymentRequest`.** `PaymentMethod.java:6`
  extends `LinkedHashMap<String,String>` only; `getEasyPaymentMethod()` (returning
  `paymentMethod.get("spayDvCd_1_1")`) lives on the request DTOs
  `CommPaymentRequest` (`CommPaymentDao.java:28-31`) and `PassPaymentRequest`
  (`PassPaymentDao.java:37-40`). The `spayDvCd_1_1` value key is correct; the class
  attribution was misplaced.
- **Refund `keyFinding #4` conflation.** Only `verifyOnlineRefunds` takes
  `retNo1-4 + strName` (`RefundService.java:31-33`); `executeOnlineRefunds` takes
  `pnrNo + ogtk* + retAmt/retFee` echoed from the verify response
  (`RefundService.java:15-17`) — no `retNo`/name.
- **Train-search namespace grep count.** A smali sweep of
  `com.korail.mobile.{research,seatMovie,schedule}.*` returns **14** endpoints,
  not 16; the remaining 2 (`getNCardHistory` = `ticket.dcntCrdUseQry.do`,
  `setNCardExtension` = `reservation.dcntCrdExtn.do`) live in other namespaces.
  The **total of 16 is correct**; only the "all in namespace" characterization was
  imprecise.
- **`DynaPathMobileSDK.generate()` shorthand.** Actual call is the Kotlin
  companion form `DynaPathMobileSDK.Companion.generate()` (`ExecuteDao.java:45`);
  `initialize` likewise via `.Companion` (`IntroActivity.java:584`).
- **Two stale line-refs in doc 02, analysis is the accurate one.**
  `agent-reports/02-network-core.md` cites DynaPath `initialize` at
  `IntroActivity.java:657` and `setSessionId` at `LoginDao.java:236`; the current
  jadx dump places these at `:584` and `:241` (confirmed).

### 9.4 Cosmetic / non-material drift (no factual impact)

These recur across many dimensions and were confirmed harmless:

- **Gson annotation.** Every `@SerializedName("h_msg_cd")` etc. is, in the
  decompiled artifact, the obfuscated alias `@S3.c(...)` (`S3.c` = relocated
  `com.google.gson.annotations.SerializedName`). Wire names are exactly as
  reported.
- **Package casing.** `k5/b.java` / `w4/a.java` / `v4/a.java` on disk declare
  `package K5;` / `W4;` / `V4;`. The JADX header marks a "case insensitive
  filesystem" (macOS); same single file, no conflict.
- **Line-range drift.** Numerous `sourceRef` line numbers drift by 1–3 lines
  (e.g. `BaseActivity.L()` 145–161 vs cited 145–158; `k5/b.java E0()` 124–140;
  many `LoginService`/`K5/b.java` refs). In every case the quoted logic is
  verbatim-correct at the actual location.
- **`IS_MACRO_ACTIVE` outer gate.** The `x-dynapath-m-token` block is gated by
  **both** `if (a.IS_MACRO_ACTIVE)` (`ExecuteDao.java:26`) and URL-list
  membership; some findings cited only the list. The list is the exact 6 URLs
  (`TicketReservation`, `NonMemTicket`, `ScheduleView`, `ScheduleViewSpecial`,
  `trn.prcFare.do`, `login.Login`). Adding the outer gate only strengthens the
  "no token on domain X" conclusions.
- **`http.keepAlive` literal.** `System.setProperty("http.keepAlive", C6077m.FALSE)`
  (`KTApplication.java:93`) uses an obfuscated constant resolving to `"false"`,
  not an inline string literal.
- **`Sid` plaintext.** `"AD" + new Date().getTime()` (`S4/C0812l.java:45`),
  functionally identical to `System.currentTimeMillis()`.

### 9.5 ⚠️ Unverified / low-confidence

The following could **not** be confirmed to their literal values from source and
must not be treated as established fact. Each is flagged with what *was* vs *was
not* verified.

- **Easy-login provider codes `G='Google'`, `D='Onepass'`.** Read through
  obfuscated constant references in `S4/u.java:127`
  (`R1.x.MAX_AD_CONTENT_RATING_G` and `HelpSrvCustDao.HelpSrvCustRequest.f28874D`),
  not visible string literals. `D=='D'` is **corroborated** by
  `LoginActivity.java:250` (`easyLoginSuccess(f28874D, userKey)` on the onepass
  path). `G=='G'` is **plausible** (AdMob content-rating-G constant) but the
  literal value is **inferred, not proven** in this file.
- **NetFunnel/tamper status codes `TsBlock=301`, `TsIpBlock=302`,
  `TsExpressNumber=303`.** In `T6/a.java` these three enum entries are defined via
  obfuscated constants (`Map.MapException.StatusCode.AUTH_IS_VAILD_FAILED_VALUE` /
  `AUTH_INVALID_AUTH_TOKEN_VALUE` / `AUTH_FAILED_VALUE`); the literal integers are
  **not directly visible**. Values 301/302/303 are **inferred** from NetFunnel
  protocol convention and enum ordering, not numerically confirmed. All other
  cited `T6.a` codes (`0,102,111,200,201,202,300,900,996,999`) are literal ints
  and **confirmed**.
- **NetFunnel poll-loop line windows `T6.g.java:224-251 / 313-356`.** The overall
  flow (`5101` TID → `5002` poll on TTL → `5003` keep-alive → `5004` complete) and
  the notupdate-waitcount bypass safeguard (`T6.g.java:113-124`) are **confirmed**;
  the exact 224–251 / 313–356 windows were only **spot-checked**, not line-exact.
- **~20 loosely-cited SharedPreferences keys.** Keys such as
  `IS_TICKET_AUTO_REFRESH`, `IS_MULTI_LANGUAGE_TYPE`, `REQUIRED_PERMISSIONS`,
  `KEY_SETTING_SECURE_ANDROID_ID`, `VIEW_VISIBILITY`, etc. were each **confirmed
  to exist** as literal string keys via grep, but a few **class attributions are
  approximate** — e.g. `IS_MULTI_LANGUAGE_TYPE` is in `S4/r.java`,
  `REQUIRED_PERMISSIONS` in `S4/E.java`, `VIEW_VISIBILITY` in `Y4/Q.java` — rather
  than the exact class originally named.
- **`GifticketReturnDao.getId()` returning `f.dao_gifticket_reservation`.** The
  duplicate resource id is confirmed in the DAO. Whether this is a harmless
  copy/paste or a genuine functional mismatch depends on the caller
  (`B6/b.java:295-302`, which
  [`agent-reports/12`](agent-reports/12-products-cart-addons-gifts-bus.md) says
  expects `dao_gifticket_return`); **the caller was not independently
  re-verified** here. The endpoint path/model facts are unaffected either way.

- Live status of the online-refund endpoints (`verify/executeOnlineRefunds`),
